### [1] [General Revenue Adequacy Conditions for Energy Transport Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21620)
*Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Anatoly Zlotnik,Aleksandr M. Rudkevich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性物理网络流（如电力系统和天然气管道网络）的优化问题，研究了收入充足性（revenue adequacy）的条件。研究建立了一个通用的数学框架，验证了直流/交流潮流和稳态天然气管道流下的收入充足性条件，为市场定价机制提供了理论保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场中的优化调度和节点定价依赖于收入充足性保证，以确保市场管理者能从阻塞租金中收取足够费用来结算金融输电权。类似地，天然气市场的节点交易估值也需要收入充足性及覆盖压气机运行成本的保证。然而，交流潮流方程的非凸性使得收入充足性条件的推广面临挑战，因此需要建立通用的数学框架来研究这一问题。

Method: 本文开发了一个通用的形式化数学框架来描述非线性物理网络流，并在该框架下考察收入充足性条件。然后将该结果分别应用于直流潮流（DC power flow）、交流潮流（AC power flow）以及管道网络中的稳态天然气流（steady-state gas flow）三种具体场景进行验证。

Result: 成功建立了通用的数学框架，并验证了该框架下收入充足性条件对于直流潮流、交流潮流和稳态天然气管道流均成立。

Conclusion: 本文的工作为非线性物理网络流场景下的市场定价收入充足性提供了理论保证，为将类似电力市场的节点定价机制推广至天然气市场奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: Optimization is widely used to determine the physical and financial exchange of wholesale electricity in organized markets. Guarantees of solution optimality and feasibility rest largely on convexity, which is not in general a characteristic of the governing equations for power grid and gas pipeline networks. Policy decisions that base the scheduling and locational pricing of electricity transactions on optimization rely on the guarantee of revenue adequacy, which ensures that the market administrator will collect enough payments in congestion rents to settle financial transmission rights. Developing a similar mechanism for locational trade valuation of natural gas also requires assurance that pricing outcomes are revenue adequate, and also cover the costs of gas compressor operation. However, it has been shown that the AC power flow equations are in general non-convex and hence conditions for guaranteeing revenue adequacy in optimal power flow solutions are challenging to generalize. In this study, we develop a general formal mathematical setting for nonlinear physical network flows and examine the conditions for revenue adequacy. The result is verified for DC and AC power flow as well as steady-state gas flow in a pipeline network.

</details>


### [2] [On Moment-Based Recovery of Measures with Atomic and Continuous Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21644)
*Ruben Karapetyan,Shenyuan Ma,Aleš Wodecki,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究从矩（moments）和伪矩（pseudo-moments）恢复概率测度的问题，提出了一种新的恢复问题形式，该形式假设测度具有紧支撑且满足温和的分离准则，不仅涵盖有限原子测度，还能处理连续分量。通过研究Gelfand-Naimark-Segal构造的谱表示及其与正交多项式的联系，证明了三种不同情境下的恢复保证，并提出了新的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩恢复问题依赖于flat-extension性质来保证测度是有限原子的，但对于许多测度类，flat extension从不发生，因此需要新的假设和方法来恢复更一般（含连续分量）的测度。

Method: 提出一种新的恢复问题形式，假设测度具有紧支撑且满足温和分离准则；利用Gelfand-Naimark-Segal构造的谱表示与正交多项式理论，分析不同情境下的恢复保证。

Result: 证明了三种不同情境下的恢复保证，提供了新的理论洞察，并基于这些理论开发了新算法，通过基准测试验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了矩恢复问题的适用范围，使其能处理含连续分量的测度，同时为原子测度恢复的常用算法提供了新的理论视角，并产出了可实际应用的新算法。

Abstract: Recovering probability measures from moments is a central theme in statistics and optimization. In particular, we focus on the recovery of measures from moments and pseudo-moments, which may come from solving the moment-SOS hierarchy in one dimension. A typical strategy when recovering a measure from moments is to verify the flat-extension property, which certifies that the underlying measure is finitely atomic and ultimately leads to recovery. For many classes of measures, however, the flat extension never occurs and thus if one aims to recover the measure corresponding to the moments, assumptions need to be made. We formulate a new kind of recovery problem, where one assumes that the measure has compact support and a fulfills a mild separation criterion. The key feature of this recovery problem formulation is that it covers not only finitely atomic measures, but also measures with continuous components. We study this new problem and describe three situations in which different guarantees can be proven. These guarantees are developed by studying the spectral representation of the Gelfand-Naimark-Segal construction and its connection to orthogonal polynomials, which ultimately allows us to provide several additional insights, which apply to algorithms widely used for the recovery of atomic measures from moments. Furthermore, the statements proven lead to novel algorithms, which we benchmark, further confirming the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [3] [Spectral analysis of the logit mapping and implications for stochastic user equilibrium algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21843)
*Debojjal Bagchi,Stephen D. Boyles*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了随机用户均衡（SUE）中Logit映射的雅可比矩阵结构，并基于此提出了两种改进的路径基SUE算法：一种是具有自适应常步长的MSA方法，实现全局收敛与渐进线性收敛；另一种是基于牛顿法的根求解方法，利用雅可比矩阵结构避免了稠密Hessian矩阵和流形优化。实验表明新方法在大规模网络和高需求场景下表现优异，首次在芝加哥和费城等大型网络上报告了运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径基SUE算法（如MSA和梯度投影法）收敛速度慢或需要计算稠密的Hessian矩阵，难以应用于大规模交通网络。作者希望通过分析Logit映射雅可比矩阵的结构性质，设计更高效的算法以提升大规模SUE问题的求解性能。

Method: 首先理论分析Logit映射雅可比矩阵的分解性质，证明其可分解为一个零化可行路径流差分的矩阵和一个特征值非正实数的矩阵（在单调非减可分离的路段成本假设下）。基于此性质，设计自适应常步长MSA：在保持全局收敛性的同时实现渐进线性收敛。进一步提出牛顿型方法，将SUE重构为求根问题，利用雅可比矩阵的稀疏结构避免稠密Hessian计算，无需流形优化。

Result: 证明了MSA采用小常步长$s$时线性收敛，收敛速率为$1-s$，最大允许步长由Logit映射雅可比矩阵的特征值决定。数值实验表明牛顿型方法在大多数测试网络上达到超线性收敛，且在大规模网络或高需求下优于现有方法。首次在Chicago Regional和Philadelphia等大规模网络上报告了logit基SUE的运行时间。

Conclusion: 利用Logit映射雅可比矩阵的结构性质可以有效改进SUE算法的收敛性能，所提两种方法分别在线性收敛和超线性收敛方面取得突破，为大规模网络上的SUE求解提供了可行方案和性能基准。

Abstract: We analyze the Jacobian of the logit mapping for stochastic user equilibrium (SUE) and use it to develop two improved algorithms for path-based SUE. We show that the Jacobian decomposes into two matrices: one that annihilates differences of feasible path flow vectors, and another whose eigenvalues are all non-positive reals, provided link costs are monotone non-decreasing and separable. Using these properties, we first show that the method of successive averages (MSA) with a small constant step-size $s$ converges linearly at a rate $1-s$, with the largest admissible step-size depending on the eigenvalues of the Jacobian of the logit mapping. Building on this result, we develop an adaptive constant step-size rule that retains the global convergence of MSA while achieving asymptotic linear convergence. Our second algorithm is a Newton-based method using a reformulation of SUE as a root-finding problem. Unlike gradient-projection approaches that operate on the Hessian of the SUE objective function (a dense matrix), our method exploits the structure of the Jacobian of the logit mapping, making computations tractable and removing the need for manifold optimization. Numerical experiments show superlinear convergence on most tested networks, with our methods outperforming existing approaches on large networks or when demand is high. To our knowledge, this article is the first to report runtimes for logit-based SUE on networks as large as Chicago Regional and Philadelphia, providing a benchmark for future algorithmic development.

</details>


### [4] [Proximal Gradient-based Low Rank Tensor Decomposition for State Dependent Riccati Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21885)
*Jiahua Jiang,Carmeliza Navasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模离散维度控制系统的偏微分方程最优控制问题，提出了一种基于CP张量分解的降阶建模方法。通过稀疏优化和灵活混合方法获取低秩CP张量基元素，构建降阶控制模型，并求解降阶的状态依赖Riccati方程，从而高效解决最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模离散维度控制系统中偏微分方程最优控制问题的计算复杂性，通过降阶模型提升求解效率。

Method: 利用规范多元分解（CP分解）从快照数据中提取张量基元素；采用稀疏优化和灵活混合方法实现降维，获得低秩CP张量基；构建降阶最优控制问题并求解降阶的状态依赖Riccati方程。

Result: 成功获得低秩CP张量基元素，构建了降阶控制模型，并通过求解降阶Riccati方程高效处理了最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 基于CP张量分解结合稀疏优化与混合方法的降阶策略，能有效降低大规模离散控制系统最优控制问题的计算负担，具有良好的可行性。

Abstract: We address the optimal control problems arising from partial differential equations with large discrete dimensional control systems. To obtain reduced order models, we find basis elements from the canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition. Tensor datasets are from snapshots of the large models. Our method to reduce the control system is to use dimensionality reduction approaches through sparse optimization and flexible hybrid methods is to obtain low rank CP tensor basis elements. The reduced optimal control problem leads to reduced state-dependent Riccati Equations which can be solved efficiently.

</details>


### [5] [Exact Uniform L1 Spacing for Solow-Polasky Diversity on Lines and Ordered Pareto Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21922)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich,Mahboubeh Nezhadmoghaddam,Jesús Guillermo Falcón Cardona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在指数核（逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性/有限度量幅度）下，一维及有序度量空间上固定基数的最大化子集是均匀间距点集；该结论通过等距映射推广到有序ℓ1曲线，进而应用于单调双目标Pareto前沿的最优有限逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性（等价于指数核下的有限度量幅度）在固定基数最大化问题中的最优子集结构，特别是探索一维有序度量集上最大化子集的均匀间距性质，并将其推广至更一般的度量空间和多目标优化应用。

Method: 基于指数核下有限线间隙公式（将过剩逆矩阵多样性表示为连续间隙函数之和），推导区间定理证明[0,1]上唯一最大化k点子集为等间距集；再通过逆核命题证明相邻间隙可加性结构强制指数族；最后利用等距映射将结果转移至有序ℓ1曲线。

Result: (1) 对每个k≥2，[0,1]上唯一最大化k点子集是等间距集；(2) 在归一化非增距离核中，要求相邻间隙可加性结构只能是指数族；(3) 结果通过等距映射推广到有序ℓ1曲线，均匀间距按累积ℓ1弧长定义；(4) 单调双目标Pareto前沿的最优有限逼近在累积目标空间变化上均匀分布。

Conclusion: Solow-Polasky多样性在指数核下自然选择均匀间距表示，该性质通过等距推广到ℓ1曲线，为多目标优化中Pareto前沿的均匀覆盖逼近提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality maximization of the inverse-matrix Solow--Polasky diversity, equivalently finite metric magnitude for the exponential kernel, on one-dimensional and ordered metric sets. The analysis starts from the known finite-line gap formula for the exponential kernel, which writes the excess inverse-matrix diversity as a sum of functions of consecutive gaps. Building on this formula, the main interval theorem proves that, for every $k\geq 2$, the unique maximizing $k$-point subset of $[0,1]$ is the equally spaced set. Thus the objective selects a uniform gap representation on the real line. A converse kernel proposition shows that, among normalized non-increasing distance kernels, requiring the corresponding adjacent-gap additive structure forces the exponential family. Further results transfer the interval theorem to ordered $\ell_1$ (L1, or Manhattan) curves by isometry: the maximizing sets are uniform in accumulated $\ell_1$ length. As a consequence, monotone biobjective Pareto fronts admit Solow--Polasky optimal finite approximations that are uniformly spaced in accumulated objective-space change, a natural representation when all parts of a continuous front should be covered. Examples, including a dense connected front and a finite disconnected ZDT3 front, illustrate how the continuous uniform-gap result appears on discrete candidate sets.
  Solow-Polasky diversity; diversity measures; finite metric magnitude; L1 distance; uniform spacing; Pareto-front approximation; multiobjective optimization; fixed-cardinality subset selection

</details>


### [6] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机方向探索步骤，可附加于任何基础优化方法，在非光滑非凸约束优化中几乎必然保证所有聚点达到方向平稳性（d-平稳性），且不破坏基础方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束非光滑非凸优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性，而实现方向平稳性（d-平稳性）在温和正则性条件（局部Lipschitz连续、方向导数存在且连续）下仍然困难。

Method: 在任意基础优化方法上增加一个随机方向探索步骤：采样一个方向和步长，基于函数值比较接受候选点。该探索步骤仅需一次额外评估。

Result: 所有聚点几乎必然达到d-平稳性，与基础方法行为无关；且保持了基础方法（如DCA和prox-linear类方案）的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索是一种简单有效的通用策略，能以高概率保证d-平稳性，并得到数值实验验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [7] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对成本函数中包含由未知线性随机动态演变的潜在时变参数问题，提出了一种结合Gauss-Markov估计、工具变量估计和参数预测的三步在线优化算法，并给出了期望跟踪误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际问题中成本函数的参数往往是不可测量的潜在变量，且随时间动态变化，其动态模型未知，现有方法难以处理这种双重不确定性的在线优化问题。

Method: 1) 使用Gauss-Markov估计器从含噪梯度观测中重构潜在参数；2) 利用工具变量估计器辨识参数动态；3) 预测未来参数值并计算相应的最小化点。

Result: 给出了期望跟踪误差的理论界，并通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法结合控制理论与优化技术，有效解决了具有未知动态潜在参数的在线强凸优化问题。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [8] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nash-MADDPG方法，将纳什议价解融入多智能体深度确定性策略梯度，用于V2V能量交易。实验表明，在30天连续运行中，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%，并在6-100智能体范围内验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2V能量交易方法要么需要集中式优化（存在计算限制），要么缺乏公平性保证，难以协调具有多样化充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间的自利电动汽车智能体。

Method: 将纳什议价解（Nash Bargaining Solution）集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）中：纳什议价确定高效的双边定价，纳什引导的价格接近度奖励引导智能体学习向议价最优策略收敛。

Result: 30天连续运行中，相比Double Auction方法：社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%。在6-100智能体规模及持续车辆更替场景下验证了可扩展性，定价稳定接近纳什议价基准。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG有效实现了激励一致的V2V能量交易，在提升社会福利和交易量的同时保证了公平性，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>


### [9] [Global Convergence of Control-Based Lagrangian Flows for Non-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22486)
*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化连续时间动力学流，重点分析了由比例积分和反馈线性化控制器所诱导的动力学，并证明了在约束流形上满足特定凸性性质的非凸问题具有全局指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的收敛性结果依赖于目标函数的强凸性或有界性假设，本文旨在利用约束诱导的几何结构来突破这些限制，为非凸优化问题提供更一般的收敛性保证。

Method: 采用控制理论中的拉格朗日方法，研究由比例积分和反馈线性化控制器诱导的连续时间动力学，利用约束流形的几何结构（而非传统的强凸性假设）进行分析，并证明全局指数收敛性。

Result: 对于在约束流形上满足特定凸性性质的非凸问题，该方法能够实现全局指数收敛，无需依赖目标函数的强凸性或全局有界性假设。

Conclusion: 本文通过挖掘约束流形的几何结构，为等式约束优化问题提供了更普适的收敛性分析框架，拓展了控制理论拉格朗日方法在非凸场景下的适用性。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在内在横截性条件下导出了可行性和最优性度量的收敛率，证明了每个聚点都是一阶稳定点；在球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入近似和压缩模式计算等稀疏/低秩优化问题上的数值实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的几何方法有效解决了两个流形交集上的优化问题，理论等价性为交切空间投影提供了可行性，双方向更新策略兼顾了可行性与最优性，具有良好的理论保证和实际表现。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

### [14] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于金融交易序列建模的时间对比变换器（TCT），采用自监督对比学习生成行为嵌入。嵌入单独使用时取得不错性能（AUC 0.8644），但与领域工程特征结合时未带来显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），表明学习到的表示与已有特征存在重叠。模型虽未达到生产就绪，但展示了减少特征工程依赖的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融欺诈检测中，传统方法依赖大量手工特征工程，耗时费力且难以泛化。作者希望构建一个自监督表示学习框架，从原始交易序列中自动捕获行为时序模式，从而减少对领域特征工程的依赖。

Method: 提出TCT（Temporal Contrastive Transformer）框架，使用Transformer架构结合对比学习目标进行自监督训练，将金融交易序列映射为行为嵌入向量。下游任务采用梯度提升分类器进行评估。

Result: 单独使用TCT嵌入的AUC为0.8644，结合领域工程特征后AUC为0.9205，而仅用领域特征的基线AUC为0.9245，两者无显著差异。

Conclusion: TCT能够捕获有意义的时序行为模式，但学习到的表示与现有领域特征存在较大重叠，难以产生叠加增益。该工作表明自监督时序表示学习在金融欺诈检测中具有潜力，但仍处于中间阶段，需在架构、训练目标和集成策略上进一步探索。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [15] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用小型语言模型（SLM）在实验前预测研究想法的实证成功。通过构建11,488个基于PapersWithCode客观结果的 idea 对数据集，发现SFT可将8B模型准确率从30%提升至77.1%（超越GPT-5的61.1%），RLVR方法达到71.35%且可解释。模型对表面启发式具有鲁棒性，并展现出跨领域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型加速科研自动化时，评估和筛选大量AI生成的假设成为新瓶颈，亟需一种无需穷举实验即可预测想法成功与否的方法。

Method: 构建 comparative empirical forecasting 任务，收集PapersWithCode上的11,488个 idea 对；使用SFT和RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）训练8B参数小模型，使其学习预测哪类想法能取得更好基准性能。

Result: SFT模型达77.1%准确率，超越GPT-5（61.1%）；RLVR模型达71.35%且生成可解释推理路径；模型对表面启发式鲁棒，在跨领域时间分割测试集和独立构建测试集上均有迁移能力。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小型语言模型可以充当有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [16] [The Attribution Impossibility: No Feature Ranking Is Faithful, Stable, and Complete Under Collinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21492)
*Drake Caraker,Bryan Arnold,David Rhoads*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特征共线性条件下，任何特征归因排名都不可能同时具备保真性、稳定性和完备性，并提出了DASH（集成平均SHAP）方法来解决这一问题。通过305条Lean 4定理进行了形式化验证，这是可解释AI领域首个经过形式化验证的不可能定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征归因方法（如SHAP）在特征共线性下存在根本性局限，但此前缺乏严格的理论刻画。作者希望从理论上证明这种不可能性，量化其程度，并提供实用的解决方案和形式化验证。

Method: 理论证明归因排名在共线性下同时满足保真性、稳定性和完备性的不可能性；对四种模型类（梯度提升、Lasso、随机森林等）量化归比分值发散行为；提出DASH方法，证明其在无偏聚合中达到Cramér-Rao方差下界；使用Lean 4定理证明器进行机械验证（305条定理，16条公理）。

Result: 归因设计的完备空间存在严格二分：保真-完备方法（不稳定，排名翻转率高达50%）和集成方法如DASH（稳定，对称特征报告平局）。归因比分在梯度提升中以1/(1-ρ²)发散，在Lasso中无穷大，在随机森林中收敛。77个公共数据集中68%存在归因不稳定性。

Conclusion: 共线性下SHAP-based代理歧视审计不可靠；切换为条件SHAP无法规避该不可能性；DASH是帕累托最优解。该框架包含实用诊断工具（Z检验流程、单模型筛查工具），是首个经过形式化验证的可解释AI不可能定理。

Abstract: No feature ranking can be simultaneously faithful, stable, and complete when features are collinear. For collinear pairs, ranking reduces to a coin flip. We prove this impossibility, quantify it for four model classes, resolve it via ensemble averaging (DASH), and machine-verify it with 305 Lean 4 theorems. We characterize the complete attribution design space: exactly two families of methods exist -- faithful-complete methods (unstable, with rankings that flip up to 50% of the time) and ensemble methods like DASH (stable, reporting ties for symmetric features) -- and no method lies outside this dichotomy. The impossibility is quantitative: the attribution ratio diverges as 1/(1-rho^2) for gradient boosting, is infinite for Lasso, and converges for random forests. DASH (Diversified Aggregation of SHAP) is provably Pareto-optimal among unbiased aggregations, achieving the Cramer-Rao variance bound with a tight ensemble size formula. In a survey of 77 public datasets, 68% exhibit attribution instability. Switching to conditional SHAP does not escape the impossibility when features have equal causal effects. The framework includes practical diagnostics -- a Z-test workflow and single-model screening tool -- and has direct consequences for fairness auditing: SHAP-based proxy discrimination audits are provably unreliable under collinearity. The design space theorem, diagnostics, and impossibility are mechanically verified in Lean 4 (305 theorems from 16 axioms, 0 sorry) -- to our knowledge, the first formally verified impossibility in explainable AI.

</details>


### [17] [Don't Collapse Your Features: Why CenterLoss Hurts OOD Detection and Multi-Scale Mahalanobis Wins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21493)
*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GOEN框架，通过多尺度特征、L2归一化、马氏距离和校准头实现OOD检测，并发现CenterLoss正则化会降低OOD检测性能，最优变体GOEN-NoCenterLoss在CIFAR-10上达到0.9483的OOD AUROC，超越深度集成、KNN和ODIN等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法通常使用仅针对分类精度优化的特征表示，忽视了认知不确定性的特殊需求，导致模型难以可靠识别分布外输入，影响安全部署。

Method: GOEN采用多尺度特征提取、L2归一化、基于马氏距离的距离度量，以及使用真实困难OOD样本训练的校准头。通过消融实验系统评估各组件贡献，发现CenterLoss虽提升分类精度但损害OOD检测性能。

Result: GOEN-NoCenterLoss在CIFAR-10基准上平均OOD AUROC达0.9483，超越深度集成（0.8827）、KNN（0.8967）和ODIN（0.8870），同时在单个GPU上训练时间不超过20分钟。

Conclusion: 更好的分类几何结构并不自动带来更好的认知不确定性估计；过紧的特征聚类会压缩类间边界并扭曲协方差结构，损害OOD检测效果。GOEN为构建能可靠识别自身局限性的AI系统提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The ability to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs is fundamental to safe deployment of machine learning systems. Yet, current methods often rely on feature representations that are optimised solely for classification accuracy, neglecting the distinct requirements of epistemic uncertainty. We introduce GOEN (Geometry-Optimised Epistemic Network), a simple pipeline that combines multi-scale features, L2 normalisation, Mahalanobis distance, and a calibration head trained with real hard OOD examples. Through systematic ablation we uncover a counter-intuitive finding: CenterLoss, a popular regulariser for feature compactness, significantly degrades OOD detection performance, reducing average OOD AUROC from 0.9483 to 0.9366 despite improving classification accuracy. The best variant, GOEN-NoCenterLoss, achieves an average OOD AUROC of 0.9483, surpassing all baselines including deep ensembles (0.8827), KNN (0.8967), and ODIN (0.8870) on CIFAR-10 benchmarks, while maintaining competitive in-distribution accuracy. Our results challenge the prevailing assumption that better classification geometry automatically leads to better epistemic uncertainty. Instead, we show that overly tight feature clusters compress inter-class margins and distort the covariance structure needed for effective OOD detection. GOEN is efficient, training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU, and provides a practical blueprint for building AI systems that reliably recognise their own limitations.

</details>


### [18] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在含有污染数据的线性回归中，过参数化模型是否会出现"双下降"现象。结果表明，高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器在极大过参数化条件下确实展现出双下降，其泛化性能甚至超过了多种鲁棒替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在训练数据被污染（存在异常值）的情况下，过参数化模型的"双下降"现象是否依然存在，并比较鲁棒与非鲁棒估计器在此场景下的表现。

Method: 在含有污染数据的线性回归设定中，对比高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与多种鲁棒替代方法的泛化性能，观察随模型复杂度增加时测试误差的变化趋势。

Result: 过参数化程度足够大时，最小二乘插值估计器展现出双下降现象，其泛化性能优于多种鲁棒替代方法。

Conclusion: 在污染数据场景下，极大过参数化可以使非鲁棒的最小二乘插值器获得优异的泛化性能，甚至超越鲁棒方法，表明双下降现象在存在异常值时依然成立。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [19] [HealthCraft: A Reinforcement Learning Safety Environment for Emergency Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21496)
*Brandon Dent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HealthCraft 是基于 Corecraft 改编的首个公开强化学习环境，用于在真实急诊医学条件下评估轨迹级安全性。它包含 FHIR R4 世界状态（14 个实体类型、3,987 个种子实体）、24 个 MCP 工具、双层评分标准（安全违规即零奖励）、195/205 个任务与 2,255/2,337 个二元标准（其中 515 个为安全关键）。前沿模型表现极差：Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1 仅 24.8%，GPT-5.4 仅 12.6%，安全失败率分别为 27.5% 和 34.0%；多步骤工作流性能近乎归零。基础设施缺陷修复会重新排序模型表现排名。环境、任务、评分标准和评估框架已以 Apache 2.0 协议开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型正以快于安全评估基础设施部署的速度进入临床工作流，而静态医学问答基准无法捕获急诊医学中的关键失效模式（轨迹级安全崩溃、工具误用、在持续临床压力下的妥协），亟需一个能奖励轨迹级安全性的强化学习环境。

Method: 基于 Corecraft 改编，构建 FHIR R4 世界状态（14 实体类型、3,987 种子实体），暴露 24 个 MCP 工具，定义双层评分标准（违反任何安全关键标准即零奖励）。发布 195/205 个任务（六类），2,255/2,337 个二元标准（515 个安全关键）。使用确定性 LLM 评判覆盖层限制评估器噪声，并设计了负类烟雾测试检验奖励信号的可游戏性。按照 Corecraft 第 5.2 节设计 Megatron+SGLang+GRPO 训练循环耦合。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4]，安全失败率 27.5%；GPT-5.4 Pass@1 12.6% [10.2-15.6]，安全失败率 34.0%。多步骤工作流性能近乎归零（Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%）。v2 到 v8 修复的 6 个基础设施缺陷会重新排序模型排名。负类烟雾测试显示约束标准通过率为 0.929，表明该奖励信号不可直接用于训练。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在急诊医学安全评估中表现极差，多步骤工作流能力几乎为零，基础设施缺陷会影响评估排名。该环境为未来训练奖励消融研究提供了基础设施，但奖励信号本身尚不可直接用于训练。

Abstract: Frontier language models are being deployed into clinical workflows faster than the infrastructure to evaluate them safely. Static medical-QA benchmarks miss the failure modes that matter in emergency medicine: trajectory-level safety collapse, tool misuse, and capitulation under sustained clinical pressure. We present HealthCraft, the first public reinforcement-learning environment that rewards trajectory-level safety under realistic emergency-medicine conditions, adapted from Corecraft. It is built on a FHIR R4 world state with 14 entity types and 3,987 seed entities, exposes 24 MCP tools, and defines a dual-layer rubric that zeroes reward whenever any safety-critical criterion is violated. We release 195 tasks across six categories, graded against 2,255 binary criteria (515 safety-critical); a post-hoc 10-task negative-class slate extends this to 205 tasks and 2,337 criteria. V8 results on two frontier models show Claude Opus 4.6 at Pass@1 24.8% [21.5-28.4] and GPT-5.4 at 12.6% [10.2-15.6], with safety-failure rates of 27.5% and 34.0%. On multi-step workflows - the closest proxy to real emergency care - performance collapses to near zero (Claude 1.0%, GPT-5.4 0.0%) despite partial competence on individual steps. Six infrastructure bugs fixed between pilots v2 and v8 re-ordered which model "looks stronger," evidence that infrastructure fidelity is part of the measurement. A deterministic LLM-judge overlay bounds evaluator noise, and a 60-run negative-class smoke pilot shows the reward signal is not drop-in training-safe: restraint criteria pass at 0.929 prevalence, a gameability an eval harness can tolerate but a training reward cannot. We scaffold coupling to a Megatron+SGLang+GRPO loop per Corecraft Section 5.2 and leave training-reward ablations as future work. Environment, tasks, rubrics, and harness are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [20] [Predicting Performance of Symbolic and Prompt Programs with Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21515)
*Chengqi Zheng,Keya Hu,Shuzhi Liu,Tao Wu,Kevin Ellis,Yewen Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究程序性能预测问题，提出RAP方法，通过检索相似任务和提示程序构建代理先验，来预测程序在未见任务上的表现。发现符号程序（如Python）性能呈"全有或全无"分布，而提示程序呈扩散先验，这解释为什么少量测试能验证符号程序但不能验证提示程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM提示虽广泛应用于自然语言任务，但可靠性不足——可能在少量测试用例上成功，部署时却失败。因此需要一种方法，在给定程序和少量域内样本的情况下，预测其在未见任务上的真实性能。

Method: 采用简单抛硬币模型，将每次程序执行视为伯努利随机变量，成功概率为程序未知性能。性能取决于：(1)测试用例上的执行结果；(2)性能先验。从多样化的程序与任务语料库中编译经验性能先验，并开发RAP（Retrieved Approximate Prior）方法，通过检索相似任务和提示程序构建代理先验来预测性能。

Result: 发现符号程序性能呈"全有或全无"分布（先验尖锐），提示程序呈扩散先验（存在大量接近正确的程序）。这一差异解释了少量测试用例可有效验证符号程序但无法验证提示程序。RAP方法在实际预测中取得了良好表现。

Conclusion: 符号程序与提示程序具有本质不同的性能先验分布，RAP通过检索构建代理先验能有效预测程序性能，为LLM提示的可靠性评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM prompting is widely used for naturally stated tasks, yet it is unreliable it may succeed on a few test cases but fail at deployment time. We study performance prediction: given a program, either symbolic (e.g. Python) or a prompt executed on an LLM, and a few in-domain examples, predict its performance on unseen tasks from the same domain. We use a simple coin-flip model, treating each pass/fail program execution as a Bernoulli random variable, whose success probability is the programs unknown performance. In this model, performance depends entirely on: 1) the observed execution outcomes on test cases, and 2) a prior over performances. We compile empirical performance priors from a corpus of diverse programs and tasks, and find that performance for symbolic programs (e.g., Python) are all or nothing, while prompt programs have a diffuse prior with many nearly-correct programs. This difference explains why a few passing tests can certify symbolic programs but not prompt programs. Building on this insight, we develop RAP (Retrieved Approximate Prior), which retrieves similar tasks and prompt programs from an existing corpus to construct a proxy prior, which is then used to predict performance. We show RAP achieves solid performances.

</details>


### [21] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐的角度系统研究了LLM Agent的Harness设计，将Harness分为任务分解和引导执行两个机制，量化了工作流粒度、重试预算和动作重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失败模式，并发现部分结构化（仅指定初始步骤）的Harness可能优于完全结构化的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Harness工程技术虽然能通过任务分解和引导执行提升LLM Agent的长期性能，但更精细的Harness并不总是更好，有时反而会降低任务成功率。作者希望从理论角度解释这一现象，量化Harness设计中的关键因素如何影响性能上限，并揭示具体的失败模式。

Method: 提出推理时轨迹对齐的理论视角，将Harness解耦为任务分解（将任务结构化子目标）和引导执行（重塑局部动作分布）两个机制。通过量化工作流粒度、重试预算和引导引起的动作重加权来分析Harness设计的性能极限，并通过受控合成实验和真实终端Agent基准进行验证。

Result: 揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行三种失败模式；通过实验验证了理论预测；发现部分结构化的Harness（仅指定初始步骤，后续由Agent自主执行）在通过率上可能优于完全结构化的工作流。

Conclusion: Harness设计并非越精细越好，适度的部分结构化设计（如仅指导初始步骤）可以达到更好的效果。这为LLM Agent的推理时工程提供了理论指导和实用设计原则。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [22] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将管道优化编码为可追溯的日志实体，实现对组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性的系统分析。在 Pima 和 Stroke 数据集上评估超 18,000 种管道配置，发现搜索空间结构化且部分冗余，性能由少量关键交互组件主导。集成模型表现优异（Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89；Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94），但 Stroke 上 Macro-F1 较低（0.67）。组件相似性分析揭示了高度冗余，跨种子分析揭示了性能-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质性特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡导致疾病风险预测的准确性和可重复性仍具挑战。现有 AutoML 方法缺乏对管道配置的细粒度可追溯性和可复现性分析，难以系统理解组件贡献与交互。

Method: 提出 yvsoucom-iterkit，一种确定性、日志驱动的 AutoML 框架。每个管道编码为可追踪的日志实体，支持组件归因分析、相似性分析（RMS 距离）、跨种子鲁棒性分析。采用 Random Forest 重要性分析关键驱动组件，集成模型用于最终预测。

Result: 在 Pima 数据集上，增强（0.454）、模型选择（0.198）、不平衡处理（0.101）为关键驱动；Stroke 上不平衡处理主导（0.406）。组件相似性分析显示高度冗余：biMax-biMean RMS 距离 0.0252，mixup 与无增强距离 0.0279，TomekLinks 与无不平衡处理距离 0.0325。集成模型取得 Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88；Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94, Macro-F1 0.67。集成模型跨种子变异性低（0.023-0.026），SVM 变异性高。

Conclusion: 有效的 AutoML 优化可聚焦于少量高影响组件，搜索空间存在高度冗余。性能与鲁棒性之间存在权衡，集成方法在两者间取得更好平衡。该工作为可解释、可复现的自动化机器学习管道优化提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [23] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOptim+框架，通过基础状态和增量状态的双重设计，在遗忘与保留目标间实现自适应平衡，并推出8bit量化版本降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型机器遗忘方法在遗忘目标与保留目标之间存在梯度冲突，难以同时兼顾两者性能，亟需一种能在遗忘与保留目标间实现更好权衡的优化框架。

Method: 引入基础状态捕获遗忘和保留目标的共享表征，以及增量状态保留各目标特有的残差信息。优化器根据遗忘与保留梯度的方向冲突，自适应地在共享状态和分离状态之间桥接。同时提出DualOptim+ 8bit量化变体降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构数据遗忘、真实数据遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习等任务中，DualOptim+ consistently在遗忘与保留目标之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: DualOptim+是一个有效的优化框架，能够自适应地平衡遗忘与保留目标间的冲突，其量化版本在降低内存的同时不损失性能。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [24] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种CPU-GPU协同框架，通过批处理分支定界节点和轻量级自定义内核，将GPU加速引入离散组合优化问题，在基数约束广义线性模型上实现1-2个数量级加速，并支持Rashomon集合收集以进行下游统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU在连续优化中取得了显著成功，但未能有效迁移到含离散变量、组合结构及非线性目标的优化问题（如基数约束广义线性模型的最优解认证），主要挑战包括分支定界法中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU-GPU间的频繁数据搬运。

Method: 提出一个简单、通用、模块化的CPU-GPU框架：在GPU上批量处理多个分支定界节点；围绕少量GPU高效例程构建；采用填充（padding）结合轻量级自定义内核处理不规则的节点数据结构。

Result: 在具有挑战性的实例上实现了1-2个数量级的加速，且最优性间隙为零。框架还可扩展以收集整个Rashomon集合，支持变量重要性分析等下游统计任务。

Conclusion: 证明了通过合理的批处理设计和GPU定制优化，可以克服离散组合优化中GPU加速的核心障碍，同时为后续的统计分析和模型选择提供了统一框架。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [25] [Fast Reconstruction of Exact Maxwell Dynamics from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20514)
*Dan DeGenaro,Xin Li,Obed Amo,Michael Pokojovy,Sarah Adel Bargal,Markus Lange-Hegermann,Bogdan Raiţă*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLASH-MAX是一种浅层、精确构建的神经网络架构，用于从稀疏点观测预测均匀电磁场。每个隐藏神经元代表麦克斯韦方程组的独立精确解，网络通过构造符号化满足控制方程，可在数秒内从稀疏数据端到端训练。证明了该模型在任意域上的普适逼近性。从约1K稀疏点观测中数秒内达到低于1%的相对验证误差，同时保持零PDE残差，即使仅从3D空间中100个观测样本也能保持个位数误差。结果表明，将控制结构从损失函数转移到假设类中，可以显著改善科学机器学习中精度与优化速度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习方法通常将物理方程作为损失函数中的惩罚项（软约束），导致需要在精度和优化速度之间权衡。本文旨在通过将控制方程直接构建到网络架构中（硬约束），从而在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅提升训练效率。

Method: 提出FLASH-MAX架构，每个隐藏神经元对应一个麦克斯韦方程组的精确解（如平面波解），网络输出为这些解的线性组合。通过构造保证网络输出精确满足麦克斯韦方程组（零PDE残差），并证明该模型类具有普适逼近性。仅需从稀疏点观测数据中学习线性组合系数。

Result: 从约1000个稀疏点观测数据中，在数秒内达到低于1%的相对验证误差，保持零PDE残差；即使在3D空间中仅100个观测样本，误差仍保持在个位数百分比。

Conclusion: 将物理控制结构从损失函数转移到假设类（即网络架构本身），可以显著改善科学机器学习中精度与优化速度的权衡，为物理信息神经网络等方法的效率问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce FLASH-MAX, a shallow, exact-by-construction neural network architecture for predicting homogeneous electromagnetic fields from sparse pointwise observations. Each hidden neuron represents a separate exact solution to Maxwell's equations, so that the network satisfies the governing equations symbolically by construction and can be trained end-to-end from sparse data within seconds. We prove a universal approximation result showing that this exact model class remains universal on arbitrary domains. FLASH-MAX reaches sub-1% relative validation error from about 1K sparse pointwise observations in seconds, all while maintaining a zero PDE residual, and keeps single-digit errors even for only 100 observations sampled from 3D space. These results suggest that moving governing structure from the loss into the hypothesis class can dramatically improve the trade-off between precision and optimization speed in scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [26] [Discovering Entity-Conditioned Lag Heterogeneity: A Lag-Gated Neural Audit Framework for Panel Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21542)
*Andi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AC-GATE模型，通过适应性条件编码和尺度不变滞后门控，在时序面板数据中实现实体特定滞后结构的直接可审计发现，将有效滞后作为模型结构性输出而非事后解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法提供可直接审计的实体特定滞后摘要，缺乏将滞后发现作为结构性模型输出的手段。

Method: AC-GATE模型包含自适应条件编码器和尺度不变滞后门控，通过可观测实体级代理变量条件化滞后权重分布，实现条件性调节分布式滞后。

Result: 在合成数据上成功恢复异质性滞后结构，在真实数据上生成非退化、具有外部结构的有效滞后。

Conclusion: AC-GATE能够将有效滞后作为模型结构性输出，支持分层审计协议，实现可审计的实体条件滞后发现。

Abstract: Country-level temporal panels are widely used in empirical analysis. Researchers often need to audit how different entities respond to historical signals over different time horizons. Current approaches typically do not provide directly auditable entity-specific lag summaries. We formulate entity-conditioned heterogeneous lag discovery as a temporal panel mining task and propose AC-GATE, an Adaptive-Conditioning Encoder with a Scale-Invariant Lag Gate. It instantiates conditional Moderated Distributed Lag by using observable entity-level proxies to condition lag-weight distributions over historical observations, thereby making effective lags structural outputs of the model rather than post-hoc explanations. The evaluation is based on a layered audit protocol that separates predictive calibration from lag discovery. A synthetic panel with known ground-truth lags is used for mechanism recovery testing, and two real-world country-level panels are used for external audit and stress testing. The results show that AC-GATE can recover heterogeneous lag structure in synthetic data, and generates non-degenerate, externally structured effective lags in real data.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管WPO在连续动作空间的强化学习中取得了实证成功，但其在连续状态和动作空间中的理论收敛性质尚未完全建立，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程框架，利用平均场分析中的对数Sobolev不等式来研究梯度流的收敛性；假设梯度流方程存在足够正则的解，证明单调能量耗散并建立局部对数Sobolev不等式。

Conclusion: 本文为WPO在连续空间中的收敛性提供了理论依据，展示了其在线性收敛速率下的全局最优性保证。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [28] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合包络共形选择（JECS）方法，用于多模型基准数据去污染，通过共形p值聚合和包络重建实现全局污染率控制，比基线方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准数据污染导致LLM评估不可靠，现有方法缺乏理论保证（基于分数）或仅能控制单模型误发现（共形方法），无法实现多模型间的公平比较。

Method: 提出JECS：计算每个模型的共形p值，取逐样本最大值聚合；从右尾观测数据驱动阈值之上重建保守的max-p零分布包络；对包络重缩放后的值应用自适应Benjamini-Hochberg过程选择基准，实现全局污染率控制。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上的实验表明，JECS在保持目标GCR控制的同时，比max-p基线方法具有更高的统计功效。

Conclusion: JECS形式化了多模型基准去污染问题，提供了有理论保证的全局污染率控制方法，在多模型评估场景下优于现有基线。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [29] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题，提出了期望一致性条件（ECC）作为充分必要条件，并基于此设计了一种无监督域适应损失函数（ECL），理论上证明了其样本复杂度与ECE相同，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统置信度校准方法假设训练和测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下失效；现有方法依赖不稳定的重要性加权，且难以同时处理类别级、规范级和top-label校准。

Method: 首先推导出协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；然后基于该条件提出期望一致性损失（ECL），一种无监督域适应损失；最后证明ECL的计算样本复杂度与ECE相同，并提供基于小批量的可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度校准失效，期望一致性条件提供了比全局分布对齐更弱的校准条件；ECL损失兼容多种校准形式且无需重要性加权。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [30] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感中的校准性能，基于66个NIR数据集（54个回归+12个分类任务）进行基准测试。研究表明，经过预处理优化的TabPFN在回归任务中取得最佳平均排名，显著优于PLS、CatBoost和CNN-1D；在分类任务中，直接使用原始光谱的TabPFN表现最佳。但TabPFN在光谱异常值和外推样本上的优势减弱，经典化学计量学模型仍具竞争力。结论认为表格基础模型可补充小到中等规模校准场景下的化学计量学工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIR光谱传感的实用部署需要能处理高维共线光谱、小样本、预处理依赖、光谱异常值和域外推的校准模型，现有方法存在局限性，因此探索表格基础模型能否提供新的校准策略。

Method: 在66个NIR数据集上，将TabPFN（直接推理+预处理优化推理）与PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、CatBoost、一维CNN进行基准对比。采用统一验证框架，预处理和模型选择仅在校准数据上进行，然后进行外部测试评估。

Result: 回归任务中，预处理优化的TabPFN最佳平均排名，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始光谱TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计相当。分类任务中，原始光谱TabPFN最佳平均排名。但TabPFN在光谱异常值和外推样本上优势减弱。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可补充传统化学计量学工作流程，尤其适用于小到中等规模校准场景，但需开发光谱特定的先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [31] [Dropout Universality: Scaling Laws and Optimal Scheduling at the Edge-of-Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21648)
*Lucas Fernandez Sarmiento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Dropout作为临界信号传播扰动的平均场理论，推导了相关衰减的临界和交叉标度律，发现光滑激活函数与ReLU类激活函数分属不同普适类，并据此提出前置加载的Dropout调度策略，在无额外计算开销下显著降低测试损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对Dropout的理解不够深入，缺乏从临界信号传播和标度律角度的统一框架，尤其在不同激活函数类型下Dropout对信息传播影响的差异尚不明确。

Method: 建立了Dropout作为临界信号传播扰动的平均场理论，推导相关衰减的临界和交叉标度律；通过分析相关映射的解析结构，区分光滑激活和ReLU类激活的普适类；提出秩流决胜机制选择前置加载的Dropout调度策略。

Result: 光滑激活和ReLU类激活具有不同的临界指数和普适类；在固定预算下获得饱和Dropout分布，前置加载策略显著降低测试损失，准确率提升作为一致的第二效应。

Conclusion: Dropout在临界初始化的神经网络的信号传播中引入有限深度尺度，不同激活函数类型导致不同的普适类行为；基于理论推导的前置加载调度策略在MLP和Vision Transformer上取得显著效果。

Abstract: We develop a mean-field theory of dropout as a perturbation of critical signal propagation at the edge of chaos. Dropout shifts the perfect-alignment fixed point, making the depth scale for information propagation finite even at critical initialization. We derive critical and crossover scaling laws for correlation decay and establish that smooth activations and kinked, ReLU-like activations constitute distinct universality classes, with different critical exponents and a universal two-parameter scaling collapse in detuning and dropout strength. The distinction traces to the analytic structure of the correlation map: smooth activations admit a Taylor expansion near perfect alignment, while kinked activations develop a branch point with universal non-analyticity. As a corollary, the framework yields saturated dropout profiles under fixed budget; a rank-flow tie-breaker then selects front-loaded schedules, substantially reducing held-out test loss at no extra computational cost, with accuracy gains as a consistent secondary effect. We test the predictions in MLPs and Vision Transformers and discuss CNN/ResNet extensions.

</details>


### [32] [PeakFocus: Bridging Peak Localization and Intensity Regression via a Unified Multi-Scale Framework for Electricity Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21550)
*Wangzhi Yu,Peng Zhu,Qing Zhao,Yiwen Jiang,Dawei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PeakFocus统一框架，通过联合监督时序定位与强度回归、多尺度混合定位器缓解表示冲突、位置感知解码器注入峰值时序上下文，在电力负荷峰值预测中同时提升时序精度与强度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力负荷峰值预测方法存在三个局限性：(1) 两阶段"先预测后定位"范式割裂了时序定位与强度回归的联系；(2) 多尺度表示冲突导致峰值误判和时序错位；(3) 缺乏显式峰值时序上下文导致强度平滑，预测被全局平滑趋势主导。

Method: 提出PeakFocus统一框架，包含三个组件：(i) 统一峰值感知管道(UPAP)，使用三元混合损失联合监督时序定位与强度回归，并设计基于容忍度的评估协议；(ii) 多尺度混合峰值定位器(MSM-PL)，利用粗粒度特征缓解局部波动导致的峰值误判，通过级联机制注入细粒度特征解决时序错位；(iii) 位置感知解码器(LAD)，将峰值时序上下文注入强度回归过程，提供显式引导以对抗强度平滑。

Result: 在公开Electricity(ELC)数据集和工业级World Large-scale Electricity Load(WLEL)数据集上的实验表明，PeakFocus在时序精度和强度估计上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PeakFocus通过统一框架解决了现有方法在峰值时序定位与强度回归上的三大局限性，显著提升了电力负荷峰值预测的准确性。

Abstract: Electricity load peak forecasting (ELPF), simultaneously predicting peak timing and intensity, is a prerequisite for effective grid scheduling and risk management. However, existing methods face three limitations. First, they adopt a two-stage predict-then-locate paradigm, which severs the link between temporal localization and intensity regression. Second, they still struggle with the multi-scale representation conflict, leading to peak misjudgment and timing misalignment. Third, the lack of explicit peak timing context during intensity regression causes intensity smoothing because predictions are dominated by global smoothing trends. To address these limitations, we propose PeakFocus, a unified framework for ELPF. (i) A Unified Peak-Aware Pipeline (UPAP) utilizes a triple hybrid loss to jointly supervise temporal localization and intensity regression, alongside a tolerance-based evaluation protocol. (ii) A Multi-Scale Mixing Peak Locator (MSM-PL) exploits coarse-grained features to mitigate peak misjudgment caused by local fluctuations, and injects them into fine-grained features via a cascade mechanism to resolve timing misalignment. (iii) A Location-Aware Decoder (LAD) injects peak timing context into the intensity regression process, providing explicit guidance to counteract intensity smoothing and improve peak intensity estimation. Extensive experiments on the public Electricity (ELC) dataset and our industrial-scale World Large-scale Electricity Load (WLEL) dataset show that PeakFocus outperforms baselines in both timing precision and intensity estimation.

</details>


### [33] [Representation Gap: Explaining the Unreasonable Effectiveness of Neural Networks from a Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21692)
*David Perera,Victor Moura,Lais Isabelle Alves dos Santos,Michel F. C. Haddad,Flavio Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“表示差距”（Representation Gap）这一与泛化误差密切相关的指标，并证明其在等变扩散模型中的渐近行为由任务的“内在维度”单一参数控制。该参数易于解释、高效估计，且能与常见神经网络架构的等变性关联，在合成和真实数据集上均验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络泛化误差的精确刻画依赖于启发式方法和从业者的直觉，缺乏可用可高效估计的参数进行严格表征的工具，亟需一种更易处理的替代指标来弥合理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 引入表示差距（Representation Gap）作为泛化误差的替代指标；利用最优量化与点过程理论，推导其在等变扩散模型下的渐近等价形式；证明该渐近行为由单一参数——任务的内在维度（intrinsic dimension）控制。

Result: 表示差距的渐近等价形式由内在维度单一参数决定；该渐近动态可推广到更广泛的任务和训练算法；在合成数据集（已知真实内在维度）和真实数据集上均验证了所提渐近律和估计方法的准确性。

Conclusion: 表示差距提供了比泛化误差更易处理的渐近分析工具，内在维度作为其核心参数易于解释和高效估计，为理解神经网络架构的等变性对泛化性能的影响提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Characterizing precisely the asymptotic generalization error of neural networks using parameters that can be estimated efficiently is a crucial problem in machine learning, which relies heavily on heuristics and practitioners' intuition to make key design choices. In order to mitigate this issue, we introduce the Representation Gap, a metric closely related to the generalization error, but admitting better-behaved asymptotic dynamics. Focusing on equivariant diffusion models and leveraging results from optimal quantization and point-process theory, we derive a precise asymptotic equivalent of the Representation Gap and show that it is governed by a single parameter, the \textit{intrinsic dimension} of the task, which is easy to interpret, efficient to estimate, and can be linked to the equivariances of common neural network architectures. We show that this asymptotic dynamic also extends to a broader range of tasks and training algorithms. Finally, we demonstrate empirically that our asymptotic law and intrinsic dimension estimation are accurate on a wide range of synthetic datasets, where these quantities are known, as well as on more realistic datasets, where we obtain results consistent with the related literature.

</details>


### [34] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于递归优化确定性等价（OCE）的风险敏感强化学习的样本复杂度，揭示了当效用函数u的定义域非全实数时问题不可PAC学习，并给出了CVaRₜ下复杂度与1/τ²的紧致依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险敏感强化学习研究多集中于特定风险度量（如CVaR、熵风险），缺乏对一类风险度量（OCE）的统一样本复杂度刻画，且CVaR下界依赖关系尚未最优。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型驱动方法，通过递归OCE定义目标函数，系统分析效用函数u的域对PAC可学习性的影响，并推导值和策略学习的上下界。

Result: 1) 当dom(u)≠ℝ时对应问题非PAC可学习；2) 值和策略学习的下界在状态-动作空间大小SA上紧；3) CVaRₜ的样本复杂度依赖为1/τ²，较现有最优结果改进1/τ因子，但折扣因子依赖非最优。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了递归OCE下风险敏感强化学习的可学习性边界，为统一理解风险度量在强化学习中的样本复杂度提供了理论基础，并指出了未来改进折扣因子依赖的方向。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [35] [TONIC: Token-Centric Semantic Communication for Task-Oriented Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21553)
*Sige Liu,Kezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TONIC，一种面向任务的令牌中心语义通信框架。发送端根据令牌级任务相关性进行非均匀保护分配，接收端基于令牌置信度进行门控决策，结合Transformer补全模型恢复掩码令牌，在有限信道预算下显著优于分离方案和DeepJSCC等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无线通信以比特级保真度为核心，与下游基础模型实际消费的令牌级表示存在失配。需要一种直接考虑令牌级任务相关性和下游模型需求的通信设计，而非将传输比特平等对待。

Method: 发送端将源样本转为令牌序列，估计令牌级任务相关性，在固定信道预算下通过效用感知的非均匀错误保护分配资源；接收端利用令牌级置信度门控决策，将有害替换转为可恢复擦除，再由Transformer补全模型恢复掩码令牌用于最终推理。建立效用感知的贝叶斯风险解释接收端门控规则。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，TONIC在AWGN、Rayleigh和Rician信道下均一致优于分离方案、像素域DeepJSCC基线以及令牌域基线。

Conclusion: 模块化、可解释的令牌中心语义通信框架通过语义感知保护与置信度感知门控的结合，有效解决了传统通信与模型消费之间的失配问题。

Abstract: Tokens are becoming the basic units through which foundation models represent and process information for understanding and inference. However, traditional wireless communication, centered on bit-level fidelity, faces a mismatch between what is transmitted reliably and what downstream models actually consume. This mismatch calls for a communication design that directly accounts for token-level task relevance and downstream model requirements, rather than treating all transmitted bits as equally important. In this paper, we propose TONIC, a token-centric semantic communication framework for task-oriented wireless systems. The transmitter converts each source sample into a sequence of tokens, estimates token-level task relevance, and allocates protection through utility-aware unequal error protection under a fixed channel-use budget. At the receiver, token-level confidence is used to gate unreliable decisions, turning harmful substitutions into recoverable erasures before a Transformer-based completion model restores the masked tokens for final task inference. Our framework combines transmitter-side semantic-aware protection with receiver-side confidence-aware gating in a modular and interpretable architecture, rather than relying solely on fully black-box end-to-end learning. We further establish a utility-aware Bayes-risk interpretation for the receiver-side gating rule and study its interaction with unequal protection and completion. Experimental results on image classification show that TONIC consistently outperforms separation-based schemes, the pixel-domain DeepJSCC baseline, and token-domain baselines under matched communication budgets over AWGN, Rayleigh, and Rician channels.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 建立了四个主要理论结果：MMD参数化的PAC-Bayesian泛化界、有限样本版本、一致最坏情况风险界及其风险分解、以及测地线保持界。

Conclusion: 信度集解释能够区分认知不确定性和偶然不确定性，并为何时进行自适应提供原则性的决策准则。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了潜神经过程（LNP）与高斯过程（GP）之间预测分布的KL散度，将其分解为标签污染、信息瓶颈和摊销误差三个可解释来源，并给出了各误差项的渐近界，为神经过程架构设计提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经过程通过摊销学习将GP的O(n³)精确后验近似为O(n)的映射，但缺乏对其近似误差的理论理解。本文旨在从理论上刻画LNP与GP预测分布之间的差异，揭示误差的结构化来源及其与数据维度、核函数平滑性、表示维数等关键因素的关系。

Method: 将LNP与GP预测之间的KL散度分解为三个可解释项：标签污染（label contamination）、信息瓶颈（information bottleneck）和摊销误差（amortization error）。对平方指数核和Matérn-ν核分别推导了瓶颈截断误差关于表示维数d的渐近界，并分析了标签污染项中不同成分的衰减速率。

Result: 瓶颈截断误差：平方指数核上O(e^{-c d^{2/d_x}})，Matérn-ν核上O(d^{-2ν/d_x})；标签污染项整体为O(1)，仅观测噪声分量以O(1/n)衰减；摊销误差源于单一编码器在所有上下文上的共享。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了摊销化的固有代价：标签污染项不可渐近消除，表明通过标签相关表示来估计不确定性存在根本性成本。架构建议：在GP摊销场景中仅从上下文位置预测方差，并用二阶池化替代均值聚合以缩小主导的摊销差距。

Abstract: Neural processes amortize Gaussian process inference, replacing the exact $O(n^3)$ posterior with a learned $O(n)$ map from context sets to predictive distributions. For a class of latent neural processes, we bound the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between the GP and LNP predictives, decomposing it into three interpretable sources, namely label contamination as the neural process uses label values to estimate a quantity that is label-independent in the exact GP, an information bottleneck because the finite-dimensional representation cannot resolve the full context geometry, and amortization error from a single encoder network shared across all contexts. The bottleneck truncation term decays in the representation dimension $d$ as $O(e^{-cd^{2/d_x}})$ for squared-exponential kernels on $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ where $c > 0$ is a kernel-dependent constant and as $O(d^{-2ν/d_x})$ for Matérn-$ν$ kernels, directly linking architecture sizing to kernel smoothness and input dimension. The label contamination term is $O(1)$ in general, with only the observation-noise component decaying as $O(1/n)$, identifying a persistent cost of routing uncertainty estimation through a label-dependent representation. These results characterize the costs of amortization within the analyzed class and yield architectural recommendations to predict variance from context locations alone in the GP-amortization regime, and replace mean aggregation with second-order pooling to close the dominant amortization gap.

</details>


### [39] [From Parameters to Data: A Task-Parameter-Guided Fine-Tuning Pipeline for Efficient LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21558)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域适配大型语言模型通常需要高昂的数据和计算开销，而现有方法将数据选择与参数高效微调作为孤立过程处理，未充分探索二者内在耦合关系。

Method: 提出强映射假说：稀疏的注意力头子集在任务适配中起主导作用。基于此构建P2D统一框架，通过轻量代理识别关键注意力头，并利用其作为功能过滤器筛选高质量数据，实现数据选择与结构剪枝的协同。

Result: 仅更新10%的注意力头在10%的数据上，相比强基线获得8.3个百分点的性能增益，实现7.0倍的端到端时间加速。

Conclusion: 精确的参数-数据同步能够消除冗余，为高效对齐提供新范式。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains typically incurs high data and computational overhead. While prior efficiency efforts have largely treated data selection and parameter-efficient fine-tuning as isolated processes, our empirical analysis suggests they may be intrinsically coupled. We posit the Strong Map Hypothesis: a sparse subset of attention heads plays a dominant role in task-specific adaptation, acting as keys that unlock specific data patterns. Building on this observation, we propose From Parameters to Data (P2D), a unified framework that leverages these task-sensitive attention heads as a dual compass for both sample mining and structural pruning. To rigorously quantify the total pipeline cost, we introduce the Alignment Efficiency Ratio (AER) metric for both selection latency and training time. Mechanistically, P2D identifies critical heads via a lightweight proxy and uses them as a functional filter to curate high-affinity data, establishing a synergistic pipeline. Empirically, by updating merely 10% of attention heads on 10% of the data, P2D achieves an 8.3 pp performance gain over strong baselines and delivers a 7.0x end-to-end time speedup. These results validate that precise parameter-data synchronization eliminates redundancy, offering a new paradigm for efficient alignment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的气动载荷模型常依赖对真实物理力的简化假设，限制了其准确性；且在噪声或不完整数据下，模型验证尤为困难。
