Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [41] [AutoMCU: Feasibility-First MCU Neural Network Customization via LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21560)
*Penglin Dai,Zijie Zhou,Xincao Xu,Junhua Wang,Xiao Wu,Lixin Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoMCU是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体系统，通过硬件在环生成和状态隔离调度机制，自动为MCU定制神经网络架构，在严格资源约束下实现约1-2小时的定制时间，相比传统HW-NAS方法节省数百GPU小时，同时保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MCU上部署神经网络面临严格的内存、存储和计算约束，现有方法（模型压缩、HW-NAS）依赖代理指标、搜索成本高，且未能弥合架构设计与实际部署之间的差距。

Method: 提出AutoMCU系统：1) 基于LLM的多智能体框架，包含提案、训练、评估、部署四个阶段；2) 硬件在环架构生成机制，在训练前通过厂商工具链反馈过滤不可部署候选；3) 状态隔离的多智能体调度策略，确保各阶段稳定协作。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，AutoMCU达到竞争性精度，定制时间降至约1-2小时（对比HW-NAS需数百GPU小时）；在NAS-Bench-201上优于ColabNAS和GENIUS；在多个STM32微控制器上完成实际部署验证。

Conclusion: AutoMCU有效解决了MCU场景下神经网络自动定制与部署的难题，证明了LLM驱动的多智能体系统在资源受限边缘智能中的实用价值。

Abstract: Deploying neural networks on microcontroller units (MCUs) is critical for edge intelligence but remains challenging due to tight memory, storage, and computation constraints. Existing approaches, such as model compression and hardware-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS), often depend on proxy metrics, incur high search cost, and do not fully bridge the gap between architecture design and verified deployment. This paper presents AutoMCU, a feasibility-first large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent system for automated neural network customization under MCU constraints. Given natural-language task requirements and hardware specifications, AutoMCU iteratively generates structured architecture candidates, filters infeasible designs through vendor toolchain feedback before training, evaluates feasible models under a controlled protocol, and verifies deployability through backend-grounded deployment analysis. AutoMCU includes two key mechanisms: 1) hardware-in-the-loop architecture generation for early elimination of undeployable candidates under RAM and Flash constraints, and 2) state-isolated multi-agent scheduling for stable coordination of proposal, training, evaluation, and deployment stages. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under strict MCU constraints show that AutoMCU achieves competitive accuracy while reducing customization time to about 1--2 hours, compared with hundreds of GPU hours for representative MCU-oriented HW-NAS baselines. Comparisons with ColabNAS and the LLM-based NAS method GENIUS on NAS-Bench-201 further demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of AutoMCU. Real-device deployments on multiple STM32 microcontrollers validate its practical applicability to MCU-scale edge intelligence.

</details>


### [42] [Objective-Induced Bias and Search Dynamics in Multiobjective Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21561)
*Mathieu Cherpitel,Thomas Bäck,Martijn R. Tannemaat,Anna V. Kononova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无监督特征选择中多目标优化公式的设计，比较了三种评估目标（准确率、轮廓系数、PCA重建损失）与子集大小最小化/最大化组合的六种公式。结果表明，公式选择显著影响搜索动态和帕累托前沿质量，其中PCA损失目标在紧凑性和测试准确率之间取得了最佳平衡，而基于轮廓系数的公式倾向于产生低质量的平凡解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督特征选择常被公式化为多目标优化问题，同时优化子集质量和子集大小，但评估目标的选择、子集大小正则化的方向以及初始化策略等关键因素对公式行为的影响尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 在合成数据集（包含已知的信息特征、冗余特征和无关特征）上进行控制实验，比较三种评估目标（准确率、轮廓系数、PCA重建损失）分别与子集大小最小化和最大化组合的六种多目标优化公式。

Result: 公式选择对搜索动态和帕累托前沿质量有显著影响。基于轮廓系数的公式强烈偏向于低基数的平凡解，且作为预测性能的代理指标表现不佳。提出的PCA损失目标产生的紧凑子集在测试准确率上与直接优化监督准确率的子集相当。

Conclusion: 目标设计是有效多目标无监督特征选择的核心因素，PCA重建损失是更优的评估目标。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection is commonly formulated as a multiobjective optimisation problem that jointly optimises subset quality and subset size. Yet the behaviour of this formulation depends critically on the choice of evaluation objective, the direction of subset-size regularisation, and the initialisation strategy. We study these factors in a controlled setting using a synthetic dataset with known informative, redundant, and irrelevant feature types. Six formulations are compared by combining three evaluation objectives: accuracy, silhouette score, and PCA reconstruction loss with subset-size minimisation or maximisation. The results show that formulation strongly affects both search dynamics and the quality of the resulting Pareto front. Silhouette-based formulations exhibit a strong bias toward trivial low-cardinality solutions and remain weak proxies for predictive performance. In contrast, the proposed PCA loss objective produces compact subsets with test accuracy comparable to subsets obtained by directly optimising supervised accuracy. Overall, the study shows that objective design is central to effective multiobjective unsupervised feature selection.

</details>


### [43] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制公式的生成建模新框架，通过线性规划和对偶变量（最优价值函数）来学习价值驱动传输（VDT）策略，实现了模拟自由、路径笔直、可快速鲁棒采样的生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流、扩散或薛定谔桥的生成模型存在模拟路径弯曲、采样速度慢等问题，需要一种更高效、更鲁棒的生成建模方法。

Method: 将生成建模转化为离散时间随机控制问题，利用控制理论中的经典结果构建线性规划形式，通过对偶变量（最优价值函数）直接编码最优控制策略，开发了免模拟的原始-对偶算法来近似求解价值函数和对应的价值驱动传输（VDT）策略。

Result: VDT策略产生笔直的传输路径，可快速鲁棒地模拟；支持条件生成、无分类器引导、未配对数据翻译等扩展功能；实验结果表明性能优越且具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持扩散和流模型灵活性的同时，通过笔直路径实现了更高效的采样，是一种有前景的生成建模新范式。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [44] [Embedding-Based Federated Learning with Runtime Governance for Iron Deficiency Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21563)
*Fan Zhang,Simon Deltadahl,Majid Lotfian Delouee,Daniel Kreuter,Joseph Taylor,Allerdien Visser,BloodCounts Consortium,James H. F. Rudd,Nicholas S. Gleadall,Suthesh Sivapalaratnam,Folkert Asselbergs,Martijn C. Schut,Michael Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发并实际部署了一种基于嵌入的联邦学习（FL）流水线，用于从常规全血细胞计数（FBC）数据预测缺铁性贫血。该流水线使用冻结的血细胞领域基础模型进行局部特征提取，仅对轻量级下游分类器进行联邦训练，并在两个真实临床环境（AUMC和NHSBT）中部署。由于两个站点数据呈非独立同分布（non-IID）且样本量差异大，标准FedAvg表现不如本地训练，而个性化聚合方法FedMAP在ROC-AUC和平衡准确率上均优于本地训练，表明在临床联邦中应优先采用个性化聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大量医疗联邦学习研究未能实际部署到真实医疗环境，且临床数据存在严重的非独立同分布（non-IID）问题，特别是不同医院间患病率、人群分布差异显著，标准联邦聚合方法（如FedAvg）在此类场景下性能下降。

Method: 提出嵌入型FL流水线：使用冻结的血液学领域基础模型DeepCBC进行局部特征提取，仅对小尺寸下游分类器进行联邦训练，减少通信开销；采用FLA^3平台实现运行时治理（策略授权、审计日志）；对比FedAvg和FedMAP两种聚合策略。

Result: FedAvg因偏向大样本站点导致双方ROC-AUC均低于本地训练；FedMAP在AUMC（0.9470→0.9594）和NHSBT（0.8558→0.8671）均超越本地训练，宏观ROC-AUC达0.9133，宏观平衡准确率最佳。

Conclusion: 在临床联邦场景中，当客户端样本量和任务相关性差异显著时，应优先采用个性化聚合方法而非标准加权平均聚合。

Abstract: Recent reviews find that the vast majority of published healthcare federated learning (FL) studies never reach real-world deployment. We developed an embedding-based FL pipeline for iron deficiency prediction from routine full blood count (FBC) data and deployed it across real institutional environments at Amsterdam University Medical Centre (AUMC) and NHS Blood and Transplant (NHSBT), two clinical environments that differ markedly in iron deficiency prevalence, ferritin distribution, and subject populations. A frozen domain-specific haematology foundation model, DeepCBC, performs site-local representation extraction, restricting federated training to a compact downstream classifier and substantially reducing recurrent communication relative to full-encoder federation. The two clinical datasets are structurally not independent and identically distributed (non-IID), with heterogeneity arising from distinct population differences rather than sampling artefacts. Runtime governance is enforced by FLA$^3$, a healthcare-oriented FL platform providing study-scoped execution, policy-based authorisation, and signed audit logging. Standard sample-size-weighted aggregation (FedAvg) reduced the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) at both sites relative to local-only training, as the global update was biased towards the larger AUMC distribution. FedMAP, a personalised aggregation method, raised ROC-AUC from 0.9470 to 0.9594 at AUMC and from 0.8558 to 0.8671 at NHSBT relative to local-only training, achieving the highest macro ROC-AUC of 0.9133 and the best macro balanced accuracy overall. These results support personalised aggregation in clinical federations where client sample size and task relevance diverge substantially.

</details>


### [45] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多任务算子学习（MNO架构）的逼近与统计复杂度，证明共享表示不会增加总体代价：多任务算子学习遵循与单任务相同的标度律。同时，MNO与基于任务输入拼接的多任务DeepONet在最坏情况逼近复杂度上具有相同渐近速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多任务场景下算子集合学习的逼近与统计复杂度，特别是验证共享表示是否会带来额外的代价。

Method: 针对Lipschitz多算子映射类，推导逼近与统计泛化的近最优上界；并建立参数复杂度的诅咒下界，证明相应的极小化最优速率。

Result: 多任务算子学习的标度律与单任务算子学习相同，即共享表示不增加总体代价；MNO与多任务DeepONet在最坏情况逼近复杂度上渐近等价。

Conclusion: 多任务设置下共享表示不会引入额外复杂度，且不同架构在最坏情况下的逼近性能渐近一致。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>

*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双级难度评估器（话语级和对话级）和学习调度器，解决多模态情感识别中的模态不对齐和失衡学习问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别中，模态不对齐（modality misalignment）和失衡学习（imbalanced learning）严重限制了多模态信息的有效利用，现有方法未能充分解决这些问题。

Method: 提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，包含双级难度评估器（话语级捕捉细粒度模态特定难度，对话级捕捉情感依赖和模态连贯性）和学习调度器（从简单到困难动态引导训练）。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上加权F1提升约+1.2%至+6.6%，在MELD上提升最高达+10.4%，且在不同架构和模态设置下均表现一致。

Conclusion: SPCL作为轻量级即插即用模块，能有效缓解模态不平衡问题，提升模型鲁棒性，展现了良好的泛化性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [47] [Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22738)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Hubert Baniecki,R. Teal Witter,Eyke Hüllermeier,Maximilian Muschalik,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProxySHAP 提出了一种基于代理模型的高效 Shapley/Banzhaf 交互指数估计方法，通过残差校正实现一致性，在理论上将树集成交互指数计算推广到多项式时间，并在大规模基准测试中达到最优近似质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Shapley 和 Banzhaf 高阶交互估计器在速度与精度之间存在权衡，无法同时兼顾高效性与准确性，亟需一种既能保持高样本效率又能保证一致性的新方法。

Method: ProxySHAP 采用树基代理模型实现高样本效率，并引入残差校正（Residual Correction）策略确保一致性；提出多项式时间版本的干预 TreeSHAP（Interventional TreeSHAP）以精确计算树集成的高阶交互指数，避免指数级树深度依赖；通过最大样本复用（MSR）机制分析残差调整的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ProxySHAP 在近似质量上达到新最优标准，在小型和大型预算下均取得最低误差，显著优于先前的 ProxySPEX 和 KernelSHAP-IQ，并在下游可解释性任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: ProxySHAP 成功调和了速度与精度的矛盾，为高阶交互指数估计提供了一种理论有据、计算高效且精度领先的解决方案。

Abstract: Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.

</details>


### [48] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对慢性肾病风险预测模型，发现五种经典机器学习模型在内部测试集上表现完美（AUROC=1.00），但经外部数据验证后性能急剧下降（AUROC降至0.48-0.58），校准误差飙升，共形预测覆盖率从0.80-0.98暴跌至0.21-0.25，部署准备度评分最高仅4/16分。研究强调临床预测模型部署前必须评估校准稳定性与共形预测覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有慢性肾病风险预测模型过度关注区分度（如AUROC），忽视了校准质量（概率输出的准确性）和不确定性量化，导致临床决策缺乏可靠依据。模型在内部测试集上的强区分表现可能无法反映真实部署环境中的性能。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400例，62.5%患病率）上训练逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM和高斯朴素贝叶斯五种分类器；通过Platt缩放和保序回归进行校准；使用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准质量；采用分裂共形预测量化不确定性（目标90%边际覆盖）；通过MIMIC-IV队列（97例，23.7%患病率）进行分布压力测试；设计了八维度16分的部署准备度评估框架。

Result: 所有模型在UCI测试集上AUROC均达1.00，经保序回归校准后ECE降至0.000-0.022；但在MIMIC-IV上AUROC骤降至0.48-0.58，ECE飙升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%），部署准备度评分不超过4/16分。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移至外部数据，校准不稳定性和共形预测覆盖率大幅下降揭示了模型在真实临床环境中的不可靠性。任何临床预测模型在部署前都应系统评估其在外部数据上的校准稳定性与共形预测覆盖表现。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [49] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化证据深度学习（EDL）目标函数的方法，通过将Dirichlet期望损失近似为在Dirichlet均值处的插件损失，证明在温和假设下近似误差随证据积累而衰减，并在语音命令数据集上验证了该方法在预测精度和选择性预测方面与经典EDL相当，同时更易于实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据深度学习（EDL）在传感器学习系统中提供单次不确定性估计，但其Dirichlet期望目标函数比标准监督学习损失更复杂，给分析和实现带来困难。本文旨在简化EDL的目标函数，使其更易于实现和分析。

Method: 将EDL的一阶经验风险最小化目标近似为在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失，分析了该近似在均方误差和交叉熵等损失函数下的误差衰减特性。在Google Speech Commands数据集上验证了简化目标的性能。

Result: 简化后的目标函数在预测精度和选择性预测方面与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。该分析首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖-精度权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化EDL目标函数有效降低了实现复杂度，同时保持了与经典EDL相当的性能，并为softmax在不确定性估计中的使用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [50] [Equilibrium Propagation and Hamiltonian Inference in the Diffusive Fitzhugh-Nagumo Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21568)
*Jack Kendall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将平衡传播框架扩展到斜梯度系统，证明了深度能量模型与哈密顿神经网络之间的等价性，并以扩散耦合Fitzhugh-Nagumo神经元网络为原型示例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展平衡传播框架的应用范围，建立深度能量模型与哈密顿神经网络之间的理论联系，并探索生物物理启发的神经网络（Fitzhugh-Nagumo模型）中的信用分配机制。

Method: 将平衡传播框架推广到斜梯度系统；利用Fitzhugh-Nagumo模型稳态解的自伴算子特性应用平衡传播；利用残差网络拓扑结构下的空间哈密顿量应用哈密顿回波反向传播；推导层状哈密顿递推关系。

Result: 证明了Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络稳态解由自伴算子描述，可应用平衡传播；证明了残差拓扑结构下存在空间哈密顿量，可应用哈密顿回波反向传播；推导了深度Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络和深度能量模型稳态推理的显式层状哈密顿递推关系。

Conclusion: 平衡传播框架可扩展到斜梯度系统，深度能量模型与哈密顿神经网络在数学上等价，为生物物理神经网络中的学习提供理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Equilibrium Propagation framework to skew-gradient systems and show an equivalence between deep Energy-Based Models and Hamiltonian neural networks. We focus on networks of diffusively coupled Fitzhugh-Nagumo neurons as a prototypical example. We show that since stationary solutions of the Fitzhugh-Nagumo model are described by self-adjoint operators, the methods of equilibrium propagation for performing credit assignment can be applied. Furthermore, for Fitzhugh-Nagumo networks with the topology of a deep residual network, we show that the steady state solutions admit a (spatial) Hamiltonian, and thus the methods of Hamiltonian Echo Backpropagation can be applied. We end by deriving an explicit layer-wise Hamiltonian recurrence relation governing inference for stationary solutions of both deep Fitzhugh-Nagumo networks and deep Energy-Based Models.

</details>


### [51] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中标准桥参数化与实际去噪后验之间的不匹配问题，提出了留一法后验（leave-one-out posterior）作为正确目标，并推导了去噪器、留一法后验和分数之间的精确转换。此外，引入均匀扩散的吸收态重构，在保持UDM联合分布的同时简化采样操作。实验表明，留一法参数化持续提升UDM生成质量，而吸收态重构可媲美或超越掩码扩散，说明掩码扩散与均匀扩散的实证差距主要源于参数化和采样设计而非边际分布本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准UDM的桥参数化（plug-in bridge parameterization）实际上被留一法后验优化，而非去噪后验，这导致了插值ELBO与常规交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配。作者旨在厘清这种参数化-目标的错位，并探索更好的参数化与采样策略。

Method: 1) 理论分析揭示UDM中标准桥参数化与留一法后验的关系；2) 推导去噪器、留一法后验和分数之间的精确转换公式；3) 提出基于留一法预测的改进推理方法（预测-校正采样器、温度采样）；4) 提出均匀扩散的吸收态重构，将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作。

Result: 留一法参数化在语言建模任务上持续提升UDM生成质量；吸收态重构匹配或超越掩码扩散的性能。实证结果表明参数化和采样设计比边际分布选择更能影响模型表现。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要源自参数化策略和采样设计，而非边际分布本身的差异。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [52] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact模型，将抗体CDR设计分解为接触预测与序列生成两个显式阶段，通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失函数，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现结构质量（RMSD提升7%）、表位感知（F1提升10%）和序列恢复（AAR 0.38）的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法将"哪些CDR位置会接触抗原"和"这些位置选什么氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，通过统一的全局消息传递迫使模型隐式学习接触推理，导致抗原信号被均匀稀释到所有位置。

Method: ConTact采用contact-then-act级联架构：1）学习表面互补指纹；2）预测CDR-抗原接触；3）通过接触门控将抗原特征注入序列生成头。包含距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码空间近邻先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失聚焦结合关键位置的梯度信号。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact在CDR-H3设计任务中RMSD比次优基线提升7%，表位感知F1分数比GNN基线提升10%，序列恢复率AAR达0.38。

Conclusion: 显式解耦接触预测与序列生成能有效提升抗体CDR设计的结构质量和表位感知能力，验证了分解式架构优于隐式统一建模的设计范式。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [53] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生存扩散概率模型(SDPM)，一种基于去噪扩散模型的连续时间生存分析方法，避免参数假设和时间离散化，在十组真实数据集上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法要么对风险函数施加结构性假设，要么对时间轴进行离散化，这些做法限制了灵活性并引入了近似误差。本文旨在开发一种无需参数假设且无需时间离散化的连续时间生存分析生成方法。

Method: SDPM使用去噪扩散模型对生存结局的条件分布ℙ(T,δ|x)进行建模。在条件独立删失假设下，将模型生成的条件样本通过Kaplan-Meier估计量转换为生存函数估计。模型在变换后的目标空间操作，使用标准化对数时间和删失指示器的连续高斯混合表示。

Result: 在十组真实数据集上与五种强基线方法（包括基于树、基于提升和神经生存模型）比较，SDPM在C-index、time-dependent AUC和Brier score上表现具有竞争力。合成Cox-Weibull数据实验表明SDPM能更准确恢复底层连续生存分布形状。消融研究验证了目标空间变换的重要性。

Conclusion: SDPM通过扩散模型框架有效解决了连续时间生存分析的灵活性问题，避免了参数假设和时间离散化的局限，在预测性能和分布恢复能力上均表现优异，且目标空间变换是关键设计组件。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [54] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权在线自我蒸馏（PW-OPSD）方法，通过诊断发现推理任务中教师token可靠性具有轨迹级结构（而非局部熵），并据此设计递增位置权重，在不增加教师计算量的情况下提升数学推理性能，在多个模型和基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自我蒸馏（OPSD）对所有token均等加权，但教师token在推理中的可靠性并非均匀；基于熵的方法虽试图调节，但高熵在推理中可能反映良性解多样性而非不可靠性，因此需要更准确的可靠性诊断方法。

Method: 提出分支可行性诊断方法，通过记录教师prompt的备选token并在学生prompt后强制插入，测试是否能恢复正确答案；发现轨迹内位置分数（AUROC=0.83）远优于局部不确定性分数（≤0.57）。据此提出PW-OPSD，在保持学生轨迹、教师pass和裁剪前向KL目标不变的前提下，施加递增位置权重。

Result: PW-OPSD在AIME 2024/2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1点；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上同样取得一致的Avg@12改进。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token可靠性具有轨迹级结构，可被利用而不增加额外教师计算开销。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [55] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“匹配原则”：许多看似独立的鲁棒性、领域自适应、对齐安全等方法，本质上都是在估计标签保持的部署扰动的协方差，并用该协方差正则化编码器雅可比矩阵。在线性高斯模型中给出了闭式最优解，并引入无标签的轨迹偏差指数（TDI）作为探测工具。13个预注册实验验证了理论预测的顺序，12个通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性、领域自适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全、经典各向异性正则化等问题通常被当作独立问题处理，各有独立的方法家族。本文认为这些问题的共享结构是一个统计问题：估计标签保持的部署扰动的协方差，并用其正则化编码器。

Method: 提出匹配原则（matching principle）：估计部署扰动的协方差，然后沿覆盖该协方差值域的正定矩阵正则化编码器雅可比。在线性高斯模型中推导闭式最优解，包括立方根注水填充、值域覆盖必要性等理论结果。引入无标签的轨迹偏差指数（TDI）探测嵌入敏感性。在13个预注册实验中验证理论预测的顺序。

Result: 13个预注册实验中12个通过理论预测的顺序（matched → isotropic → wrong-W），唯一例外（Office-31）是预先命名的特征间隔失效。在7B规模下，匹配风格的PMH改进了选择性诚实并保持了风格TDI，而标准DPO会退化风格TDI。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于命名了部署扰动协方差这一核心对象，阐述了正则化器必须满足的条件，并在识别该对象后提供了闭式可证伪的理论，而非追求在每个排行榜上的通用性。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [56] [AgForce Enables Antigen-conditioned Generative Antibody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21610)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现有抗体设计方法存在抗原忽视问题，提出AgForce架构，通过GNN编码器、MDN序列解码器和抗原循环一致性头等创新设计，实现了抗原特异性抗体设计，在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法虽声称依赖抗原结构，但系统评估发现它们实际上忽视了抗原输入。作者识别出三种失败模式：抗原盲视（模型依赖抗体框架而非抗原信息）、词汇坍缩（预测氨基酸种类远低于真实分布）、以及交叉熵损失收敛到位置边缘分布导致无法产生抗原特异性预测。因此需要一种真正利用抗原信息的抗体设计方法。

Method: 提出AgForce编码器-解码器架构：编码器使用GNN，配合框架失活(framework dropout)、门控瓶颈(gated bottlenecks)和双曲交叉注意力(hyperbolic cross attention)阻断抗体捷径路径；解码器采用混合密度网络(MDN)序列头，结合Potts-like成对耦合和退火多选择学习(aMCL)替代交叉熵损失，以及抗原循环一致性头(antigen cycle consistency head)确保预测分布编码抗原身份。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，AgForce同时实现了最佳的结合质量和序列恢复，氨基酸恢复率比最强序列基线提升8%，在所有界面指标上超越基线，并将GNN方法的有效词汇量提升近一倍。

Conclusion: AgForce通过架构创新解决了抗体设计中抗原信息被忽视的根本问题，为抗原特异性抗体设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Antibody design methods condition on antigen structure to generate complementarity-determining regions (CDR), yet a systematic evaluation of baseline methods reveals that they largely ignore the antigen input. We identify three failure modes that explain this behavior. Antigen blindness arises because models derive predictions from antibody framework context rather than antigen information, producing nearly identical CDRs regardless of the target. Vocabulary collapse reduces predicted amino acids to three to five per position, far below the ground truth distribution in native sequences. Moreover, any model trained with standard per-position cross-entropy converges to the positional marginal distribution, making it provably unable to produce antigen-specific sequence predictions. We propose a novel encoder-decoder architecture called AgForce, that uses a graph neural network (GNN) as the encoder and specialized decoders for sequence-structure co-design. Specifically, we apply framework dropout, gated bottlenecks, and hyperbolic cross attention that prevent the antibody shortcut path. In the decoder, a Mixture Density Network (MDN) sequence head with Potts-like pairwise coupling and annealed Multiple Choice Learning (aMCL) replaces the cross-entropy objective with a multi-component distribution whose optimal solution differs from the positional marginal. An antigen cycle consistency head routes gradients through the sequence decoder, forcing predicted distributions to encode antigen identity. AgForce achieves the best binding quality and sequence recovery simultaneously on the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, improving amino acid recovery by 8% over the strongest sequence baseline while surpassing the baselines across all interface metrics, and nearly doubling the effective vocabulary of GNN methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/mansoor181/ag-force.git

</details>


### [57] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，用于从Hi-C等染色体构象捕获技术产生的相互作用图谱中推断参数（如鉴定着丝粒位置），通过自定义模拟器生成训练数据，并在多种物种上准确恢复了着丝粒的基因组位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将相互作用图谱（如Hi-C）中的推断问题形式化为一个通用的逆问题，即从展示实体间成对相互作用的图谱中推断一组参数，且实体数量和大小可变。现有方法难以处理图谱间的共享结构以及真实数据中实体数量和大小的变异性。

Method: 采用Transformer架构处理实体数量和大小的变异性，并设计自定义模拟器生成大量计算成本低廉的合成数据用于模型训练。该方法利用图谱间的共享结构（如局部模式的全局对齐）进行数据驱动学习。

Result: 在着丝粒定位任务上，该方法在多种基因组大小不同的物种上均能准确恢复着丝粒的基因组位置。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了相互作用图谱推断中的变异性问题，通过数据驱动和模拟训练的方式实现了跨物种的准确着丝粒定位，为类似逆问题提供了一种通用解决方案。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [58] [Alike Parts: A Feature-Informed Approach to Local and Global Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21646)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双重特征重要性框架，通过"类似部分"方法增强局部可解释性，并在全局原型选择中引入特征多样性目标，实验表明该方法在保持或提升预测保真度的同时增加了原型的特征多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于原型的可解释方法虽然直观，但缺乏特征级别的细粒度信息，难以让用户理解哪些具体特征驱动了分类决策。

Method: 1) 局部层面：提出"类似部分"方法，利用特征重要性分数突出被分类实例与其最近原型之间最相关的共享特征子集；2) 全局层面：在原型选择目标函数中加入特征重要性项，以主动促进所选原型的特征归因多样性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，增强的原型选择过程能够维持甚至在某些情况下提高代理模型的预测保真度，表明特征多样性不会损害模型保真度。

Conclusion: 特征多样性与预测保真度可以兼得，所提框架在保持模型性能的同时提升了原型的可解释性和特征级细粒度。

Abstract: Prototype-based explanations offer an intuitive, example-based approach to support the interpretability of machine learning black box classifiers but often lack feature-level granularity. We introduce a framework that integrates feature importance at two levels to address this gap. First, for local explanations, we propose \textit{alike parts}: a method that uses feature importance scores to highlight the most relevant, shared feature subsets between a classified instance and its nearest prototype, guiding user attention. Second, we augment the global prototype selection objective function with a feature importance term to actively promote diversity in the feature attributions of the selected prototypes. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that this augmented selection process maintains or, in some cases, increases the prediction fidelity of the surrogate model, suggesting that feature diversity does not compromise model fidelity.

</details>


### [59] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV 是一个原生支持 entmax 的稀疏解码框架，通过查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择和稀疏 entmax 注意力，在加载 KV 缓存前利用稀疏性，在长上下文解码中实现高达 3.36×（softmax）和 5.43×（entmax）的加速，同时保持与全缓存 entmax 相近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，KV 缓存内存流量成为主要瓶颈，因为每个生成的 token 需要关注一个随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有稀疏解码方法针对 softmax 注意力设计，但其密集的尾部使得任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量，导致近似误差。

Method: EntmaxKV 结合查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择和稀疏 entmax 注意力。通过分析截断误差与丢弃概率质量 δ 的关系，证明当 entmax 支持集被恢复时输出误差消失。引入高斯感知的 entmax 选择器，从轻量级页面统计中估计 entmax 阈值，动态调整选择的预算。

Result: 在匹配的 KV 预算下，EntmaxKV 丢弃更少的概率质量，保留更多的支持 token，输出误差更低。在长上下文和语言建模基准上，使用一小部分 KV 缓存即可接近全缓存 entmax 性能，在 1M 上下文长度下实现高达 3.36×（softmax）和 5.43×（entmax）的加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV 利用 α-entmax 产生精确零值的特性，将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转化为支持集恢复问题，实现了高效且精确的稀疏解码，显著降低了长上下文解码中的 KV 缓存内存流量瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [60] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器本质上是放大预训练模型中隐含的"典型性轴"（typicality axis），而非构建真正的人-AI分类边界。该轴在流畅正式文本上有效，但在非母语写作中完全反转，且通过少量样本即可复现全参数微调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI文本检测器的真实工作机制，质疑其是否真正学习到了区分人类与AI文本的语义边界，还是仅仅利用了预训练编码器中已有的典型性偏差。

Method: 在三种架构上，对比原始编码器投影（centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)）与全参数微调的性能；通过非母语ESL写作进行反证实验；使用24样本冻结探针、闭式雅可比预测器、三种操作独立探针（文本表面特征残差化、几何符号消融、闭式文本对预测器）进行验证。

Result: 原始投影在NYT-vs-HC3上达到0.806/0.944/0.834 AUROC（为微调上限的86-106%）；非母语ESL上AUROC降至0.06-0.20；24样本冻结探针匹配全微调性能（0.900 vs 0.895）；三种独立探针一致性为cos 0.74/0.81/1.00；LoRA->全微调偏差差距中≥97%为校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器并未构建AI-vs-人类边界，而是放大了一个预训练中已存在的典型性轴；该轴具有人口层面的共享性和跨架构的观察者不变性；现有干预方法的性能差异主要源于校准偏移而非表征学习。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [61] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家RL方法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中的有效性，证明了其actor更新在期望上等价于值梯度，并通过注意力机制的自动微分近似值信号，最终提出了基于值梯度信号和可达奖励空间的RL有效性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无评论家RL方法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中表现出色，但其工作机制尚未被充分理解，缺乏理论解释为何这些方法有效以及何时能带来最大收益。

Method: 1. 在可微分 rollout 和加性噪声参数化下，证明actor更新在期望上类似值梯度，反向传播的协态条件期望等于值梯度。2. 对于离散transformer策略，证明通过注意力机制的自动微分产生的经验协态近似值信号，误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制。3. 基于上述理论，将RL影响分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励空间，提出RL有效性的判据。

Result: 建立了无评论家RL方法（PPO/GRPO）与值梯度之间的理论联系，揭示了其有效性的数学本质，并给出了沿预训练轨迹判断RL何时最有效的量化准则。

Conclusion: 无评论家RL方法的有效性可归因于其隐式地近似了值梯度更新，RL的效果取决于值梯度信号的强度与可达奖励空间的剩余潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [62] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分层变分模型的框架，将测试时适配转化为摊销化随机策略，在扩散模型中实现快速高质量奖励对齐采样，推理速度提升超5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在适配下游目标（如逆问题）时，通常需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，推理成本高。现有方法在质量与速度之间难以取得良好的权衡。

Method: 将测试时适配形式化为分层变分模型，将控制摊销到一个轻量级但表达能力强的随机策略中。该策略通过提供结构化逐歩控制，支持大步长少步数扩散采样，实现快速推理。进一步扩展到半摊销机制，结合低成本摊销提案与有限测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分辨率任务中，感知质量优于基线，推理速度提升5倍以上。在半摊销机制下，多个逆问题上达到最先进感知质量。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过摊销化策略有效实现了质量-速度的强权衡，显著降低了推理成本，同时保持或超越最新的测试时扩展基线性能。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [63] [X-Token: Projection-Guided Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21699)
*Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Adithyakrishna Venkatesh Hanasoge,Mingyu Yang,Ali Taghibakhshi,Saurav Muralidharan,Ashwath Aithal,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X-Token方法，通过P-KL（消除分词分区）和H-KL（松弛匹配约束）两种互补损失函数，解决跨分词器知识蒸馏中罕见token丢失和过度保守匹配问题，在Llama-3.2-1B上超越当前SOTA GOLD +3.82平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于logits的跨分词器知识蒸馏方法存在两个关键缺陷：(i) 罕见token失败——关键token落在不匹配子集中被抑制；(ii) 过度保守匹配——严格1对1匹配排除了跨表面形式的近似等价token。需要针对性地解决分词不对齐导致的知识传递损失。

Method: 提出X-Token框架，包含两种互补损失函数：P-KL通过稀疏投影矩阵W（从分词器级字符串规则初始化）消除分词分区，对齐学生与教师分布；H-KL保持混合形式，但松弛匹配约束，将每个学生token与W下排名最高的教师映射对齐。两者共享W且可自然扩展到多教师蒸馏。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B上，X-Token以Qwen3-4B为教师时超越GOLD +3.82平均分，以Phi-4-Mini为教师时+0.5分。两教师设置（Phi-4-mini + Llama-3B）比单教师蒸馏提升+1.3分。

Conclusion: X-Token通过针对性的两种损失设计有效解决了跨分词器蒸馏的核心对齐问题，在多个教师设置下均取得显著改进，且支持多教师扩展。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer knowledge distillation allows a student model to learn from teachers with incompatible vocabularies. Prior work operates on hidden states or logits; the latter is preferred as a drop-in replacement requiring no auxiliary components. Logit-based methods either use only the correct-token probability, missing the full 'dark knowledge' in the teacher's distribution, or operate on the full output distribution, relying on strict token partitioning and/or unprincipled heuristic ranking. We identify two key shortcomings of full-distribution, logit-based methods: (i) an uncommon-token failure, where critical tokens fall into the unmatched subset (e.g., Llama's 1100 multi-digit numerals under digit-splitting Qwen supervision) and are suppressed during training, reducing GSM8k from 12.89 to 2.56 compared to same-tokenizer KD from a weaker teacher; and (ii) over-conservative matching, where strict 1-to-1 matching excludes near-equivalent tokens across surface forms. These failures require distinct remedies: eliminating the partition when critical tokens are misaligned, and refining it when alignment is reliable. We propose X-Token, an approach with two complementary loss formulations targeting these issues. P-KL removes partitioning and aligns the student's distribution with the teacher's via a sparse projection matrix W (initialized from tokenizer-level string rules) to address the uncommon-token failure. H-KL retains the hybrid form while relaxing matching to align each student token with its top-ranked teacher mapping under W. Both objectives share W and extend naturally to multiple teachers. Empirically, on Llama-3.2-1B, X-Token outperforms the current state of the art GOLD by +3.82 average points with a Qwen3-4B teacher and by +0.5 with a Phi-4-Mini teacher. Further, a two-teacher setup (Phi-4-mini + Llama-3B) improves over single-teacher distillation by +1.3 points.

</details>


### [64] [TBP-mHC: full expressivity for manifold-constrained hyper connections through transportation polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21724)
*Anton Lyubinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) 及其递归变体 (RTBP) 参数化方法，用于构造精确双随机混合矩阵，在保持 Birkhoff 多面体完全表达能力的同时，避免了迭代归一化和组合爆炸问题，并在语言模型预训练中展现出竞争性能与更好的稳定性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections 通过引入可学习的残差流混合提升了残差网络的性能，但无约束混合导致训练不稳定；现有方法（mHC 通过 Sinkhorn 归一化近似双随机性、mHC-lite 通过置换矩阵凸组合实现精确约束但存在阶乘复杂度、KromHC 使用克罗内克积降低复杂度但限制了混合矩阵的结构子流形）各有不足，因此需要一种既能精确满足双随机约束、又具有完全表达能力且计算高效的参数化方法。

Method: 提出 Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) 参数化方法，以 (n-1)² 个自由度构造精确双随机混合矩阵；进一步提出递归变体 RTBP。该方法避免了 Sinkhorn 迭代归一化和组合爆炸，同时保留了 Birkhoff 多面体的完整表达能力。

Result: 在语言模型预训练任务上，TBP/RTBP 方法展现出具有竞争力的性能，并显著提升了训练稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: TBP 及其递归变体 RTBP 有效地解决了残差网络中超连接混合矩阵的约束问题，在计算效率、表达能力和训练稳定性之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) improve residual networks by introducing learnable mixing across multiple residual streams, but unconstrained mixing leads to training instability. Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by enforcing approximate double stochasticity via Sinkhorn normalization, while mHC-lite ensures exact constraints through convex combinations of permutation matrices at the cost of factorial complexity. KromHC reduces this cost using Kronecker-product parameterizations, but restricts the mixing matrices to a structured submanifold of the Birkhoff polytope .
  We propose Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) parameterizations and their Recursive variants (RTBP), which construct exactly doubly stochastic mixing matrices with $(n-1)^2$ degrees of freedom. Our approach avoids iterative normalization and combinatorial explosion while preserving full expressivity of the Birkhoff polytope. Empirical results on language model pre-training' demonstrate competitive performance with improved stability and scalability.

</details>


### [65] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量的后验分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型是否真正捕捉到领域相关结构，而非利用数据捷径。在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中，该框架揭示了在精度指标上表现相似的模型实际上具有截然不同的分布响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中表现优异，但其强基准性能常被错误解释为具有科学意义的行为，模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差进行预测，这些预测虽在留出数据上有效，但与领域相关结构不一致。现有基于精度的评估方法无法区分这一差异。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，核心是Wasserstein相干性度量(WCM)。给定训练好的黑箱预测器和外部结构先验，在结构引导的输入扰动下评估原始模型输出。通过三个互补度量指标衡量输出分布相干性：基于分位数的度量(QBM)评估位置级相干性，WCM评估序数相干性，平移不变WCM评估形状相干性。在Davis激酶基准上使用KLIFS结合口袋注释，针对三个序列基DTI模型(DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB)进行实例化。

Result: 尽管三个DTI模型在可比预测区间内运行，I-SAFE揭示了它们截然不同的分布响应特征，这种差异在基于精度的评估中是不可见的。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可进行结构分解且可获得外部先验的领域，为科学AI模型提供了一种超越传统精度指标的可信度评估手段。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [66] [Correcting Class Imbalance in Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21742)
*Samuel McDowell,Nathan Stromberg,Lalitha Sankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了类别不平衡对基于先验数据拟合网络（PFN）分类性能的影响，并适配了多种经典不平衡处理方法。实验发现，阈值调整（thresholding）因PFN良好的校准特性表现优异，而下采样（downsampling）因PFN在有限数据下的卓越性能表现相当，且能降低推理计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN在表格分类任务中表现优异，但类别不平衡会导致其在罕见类上性能下降。现有的不平衡处理方法因PFN的上下文学习（ICL）机制而无法直接应用（如基于损失的方法不可行），需要专门适配与分析。

Method: 将多种经典类别不平衡处理技术（如阈值调整、下采样等）适配到PFN分类框架中，并在不平衡数据集上进行实验比较。

Result: 阈值调整方法因PFN良好的校准特性表现最佳；下采样方法性能与之相当，同时额外减少了推理计算开销。

Conclusion: 针对PFN的类别不平衡问题，阈值调整和下采样是两种有效的解决方案，前者利用校准优势，后者兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>


### [67] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于1,375例患者数据，开发了一种利用非对比CT钙化扫描（CTCS）的钙化组学特征（calcium-omics）结合机器学习框架预测心肌缺血的方法。最终模型整合了Agatston评分、8个钙化组学特征和年龄，在5折交叉验证下达到98.9%的精确率、79.2%的灵敏度和87.7%的F1分数。钙化组学特征相比传统临床变量和Agatston评分显著提升了预测性能（p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT钙化扫描（CTCS）已被广泛用于心血管风险分层，但常规CTCS扫描能否用于预测心肌缺血尚未充分探索。本研究旨在开发一种基于常规CTCS扫描的机器学习框架，利用定量冠状动脉钙化评估来预测心肌缺血，以提高心血管风险分层的可及性。

Method: 回顾性分析1,375名同时接受非对比CTCS和regadenoson负荷心脏PET心肌灌注成像的患者（来自University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center）。提取74个变量（临床变量、Agatston评分、钙化组学特征），使用XGBoost结合SHAP进行特征选择，基于5折交叉验证训练和评估预测模型。

Result: 在987名患者中，89名（9%）存在心肌缺血。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙化组学特征和年龄，精确率98.9±3.0%、灵敏度79.2±8.4%、F1分数87.7±5.3%。钙化组学特征的加入显著提升了预测性能。钙化动脉数量虽然在SHAP分析中排名最低，但在逻辑回归中与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63, 95%CI: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001）。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种利用常规非对比CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙化组学特征在传统风险因素和Agatston评分基础上提供了增量预测价值，有望支持更广泛可及的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [68] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Text2Opt-Bench基准，发现大模型在文本到优化任务中存在有效绑定极限（即实例数据增大时准确率崩溃），并提出BIND方法——通过将数值数据外化到结构化文件使模型程序化绑定数据，显著提升GPT-5-Nano（59.1%→82.4%）和GPT-5（86.2%→95.8%）的准确率，同时验证了专注于绑定能力的微调模型优于端到端方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到优化需要建模（选择正确的优化结构）和绑定（将每个系数、索引和参数与具体问题数据关联）两种可分离的能力，现有方法在实例数据增大时准确率严重下降，需要研究这一现象并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出Text2Opt-Bench基准（12类优化问题，含千变量级随机和多目标问题），定义有效绑定极限概念，提出BIND推理方法——将数值数据外化到结构化文件，让模型以编程方式绑定数据而非从提示转录。同时进行专门的绑定能力微调实验。

Result: BIND将GPT-5-Nano准确率从59.1%提升至82.4%（匹配pass@5的82.0%且token成本更低），GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%。绑定专家微调模型（1.5B参数）在三个优化类别上超越端到端SFT和RL训练的7B基线模型。

Conclusion: 文本到优化中的绑定能力是可分离且可专门优化的关键瓶颈，通过数据外化和程序化绑定可有效突破有效绑定极限，绑定专家模型方法比端到端方法更高效。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [69] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL框架，通过非参数对比百分位近似方法建模相对偏好信号，而非绝对交互强度，从而缓解推荐系统中因用户行为强度不均衡导致的偏差问题。离线实验与在线A/B测试（覆盖数十亿用户）均验证了该方法在推荐质量与鲁棒性上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统依赖用户交互数据训练，但用户间的行为强度存在系统性的不均衡（高活跃用户信号被过度放大，低活跃用户信号被低估），导致观测交互无法真实反映用户偏好，损害推荐质量与鲁棒性。现有方法未能有效处理这种偏差。

Method: 提出PEARL（非参数对比百分位近似框架）：(1) 基于相对优势去偏，利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；(2) 引入基于预测的自举机制进行百分位平滑，应对稀疏离散反馈；(3) 提出广义值加权公式与协同训练策略，增强建模灵活性与表征学习。

Result: 离线实验表明PEARL有效缓解行为偏差，在多个排序目标上持续提升推荐性能。在数十亿用户的生产级直播平台在线A/B测试中：观看时长+2.10%，消费量+0.80%，互动率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL通过建模相对百分位偏好而非绝对交互强度，有效解决了行为强度不均衡问题，在理论与实际部署中均验证了其有效性，为大规模推荐系统的偏差缓解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [70] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324例患者数据，利用CatBoost-SHAP方法从CTCS图像中提取的钙化组学和心外膜脂肪组学特征（共424个候选特征，筛选出14个最预测性特征），构建预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病的机器学习框架。优化模型取得了83.1%敏感性、93.8%特异性、85.3%准确率和73.9% F1分数，且在零钙化评分患者中仍具可靠预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种基于非对比CT钙化评分图像的先进机器学习框架，利用冠状动脉钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量分析来预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病，以改善临床决策并减少对对比增强或有创诊断程序的依赖。

Method: 从1,324例SCOT-HEART试验患者的CTCS图像中提取24项临床变量、189项钙化组学和211项心外膜脂肪组学特征，使用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，最终采用CatBoost梯度提升方法对14个最预测性特征进行建模。

Result: 优化模型敏感性83.1±4.6%、特异性93.8±1.7%、准确率85.3±2.0%、F1分数73.9±3.3%；钙化组学和脂肪组学数据的纳入显著提升了预测性能，且在零钙化评分患者中仍保持可靠预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该创新方法有望改善临床决策，减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖，尤其适用于低至中等风险患者群体。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

</details>


### [71] [Position: The Time for Sampling Is Now! Charting a New Course for Bayesian Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21765)
*Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基于采样的推理（SAI）在贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中已与优化方法达到计算同等水平，即将取代后者成为高效推理的主要手段，并呼吁社区克服误解，聚焦后验探索和样本蒸馏两大核心挑战以释放SAI的全部潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAI在实际采用中受限于长期存在的误解，认为其不可行或效率低下，本文旨在纠正这些误解并推动范式转变。

Method: 作为一篇立场论文，通过论证SAI已实现与优化方法相当的计算效率，分析其在模型平均、下游任务和后验景观洞察方面的优势，并提出未来研究方向。

Result: SAI在计算效率上已与优化方法持平，且在预测性能、不确定性量化及下游任务支持方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: SAI正处于取代优化方法的关键转折点，社区需转向解决后验充分探索和高保真蒸馏两大核心问题，以确立SAI在贝叶斯深度学习中的核心地位。

Abstract: The practical adoption of sampling-based inference (SAI) in Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) remains limited, partly due to persistent misconceptions about the feasibility and efficiency of sampling. This position paper argues that SAI has achieved computational parity with optimization-based methods and is at the verge of superseding such methods for effective and efficient inference in BNNs. This development should be in the interest of the whole community, promoting BNNs as a principled paradigm with its long-standing yet unfulfilled promise of providing principled uncertainty quantification for neural networks. SAI can even do more -- yielding superior prediction performance through model averaging, serving as the foundation for a plethora of possible downstream tasks, and providing crucial insights into the landscape of BNNs. In order to make such a change happen and unfold the potential of sampling, overcoming current misconceptions is a necessary first step. The next step is to realign research efforts toward addressing remaining challenges in SAI. In particular, the community must focus on two core problems: sufficient exploration of the posterior landscape and high-fidelity distillation of posterior samples for efficient downstream inference. By addressing conceptual and practical obstacles, we can unlock the full potential of SAI and establish it as a central tool in Bayesian deep learning.

</details>


### [72] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory-R2是一个针对长时记忆增强型LLM智能体的训练框架，其核心算法LoGo-GRPO通过结合局部和全局组相对优化解决多会话环境下的记忆状态不一致导致的信用分配难题，并联合优化记忆形成与演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法（如GRPO）假设不同轨迹样本来自相同的有效环境，但记忆增强型LLM智能体在多会话场景下，不同轨迹会写入、更新或删除不同记忆，导致中间记忆状态不一致，轨迹级比较不公平，长时记忆操作的信用信号存在噪声和偏差。

Method: 提出LoGo-GRPO算法，包含全局优化（端到端长程轨迹级奖励学习）和局部重放（从相同中间记忆状态出发比较不同记忆操作结果）。采用共享参数协同学习设计，通过角色特定提示从同一LLM主干实例化事实提取器和记忆管理器。使用渐进式课程学习，将训练会话长度从8逐步增加到16再到32。

Result: Memory-R2框架在长时多会话场景下有效解决了记忆状态不一致导致的信用分配问题，实现了对记忆增强型LLM智能体的有效训练。

Conclusion: Memory-R2为记忆增强型LLM智能体在长时多会话场景下提供了有效的训练范式，其局部-全局优化、共享参数协同学习和渐进式课程设计共同解决了该领域的核心挑战。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

</details>


### [73] [Manifold-Guided Attention Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21770)
*Ian Li,Kapilesh Guruprasad,Raunak Sengupta,Ninad Satish,Loris D'Antoni,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的注意力干预方法MAGS，通过对注意力头输出进行子空间投影修正，在推理时动态纠正LLM推理错误，在数学、代码和分子生成任务上优于静态干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中频繁出错，而现有的激活干预方法使用固定的、预计算的修正向量，忽略了模型当前的生成轨迹位置，导致对正确步骤和错误步骤进行无差别扰动。

Method: 首先，从正反推理轨迹的对比对中学习特定注意力头的低维正确性子空间；推理时，监测每个注意力头到该流形的接近程度，当偏差超过学习阈值时，应用目标投影修正，将注意力输出引导回正确子空间。

Result: 在数学推理（MATH-500, GSM8K）、代码生成（HumanEval, MBPP）和分子生成（SMILES）基准上，MAGS一致优于无干预基线和静态干预方法。

Conclusion: 正确性流形是大语言模型注意力几何结构中的一般性特征，轨迹感知的动态干预比固定干预更有效。

Abstract: Large language models frequently produce errors in reasoning tasks despite possessing the underlying knowledge required for correct reasoning. One possible approach to improve reasoning consistency is through activation steering. However, existing activation steering approaches apply fixed, pre-computed correction vectors, ignoring where the model currently sits along its generation trajectory; the result is indiscriminate perturbation that disrupts already-correct steps as freely as erroneous ones. We propose Manifold-Guided Attention Steering (MAGS), a trajectory-aware inference-time intervention grounded in a geometric observation: the output activations of specific attention heads diverge from a low-dimensional correctness manifold at the point of error, and this deviation compounds through subsequent steps. For each identified attention head, we learn a low-dimensional subspace from contrastive pairs of correct and incorrect traces that capture the directions along which error behavior deviates from correct behavior. During inference, we monitor each head's proximity to this manifold and apply a targeted projection correction when deviation exceeds a learned threshold, steering the attention output back toward the correct subspace before the error propagates. MAGS consistently outperforms both unsteered baselines and static steering approaches across benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning (MATH-500, GSM8K), code generation (HumanEval, MBPP), and molecular generation (SMILES), suggesting that correctness manifolds are a general feature of LLM attention geometry.

</details>


### [74] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓概念相结合，提出了一个用于认证复合机制（同时包含训练时和推理时随机化）鲁棒性的通用框架，实现了对后门攻击的端到端认证，并在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑认证方法主要针对对抗性扰动（通过随机化训练防御投毒攻击，通过随机化推理防御逃避攻击），但难以扩展到后门攻击场景——因为后门攻击同时扰动训练数据和测试数据，需要在一个统一的鲁棒性证书中分析训练时和推理时的随机化机制。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓（privacy profiles）对偶视角相连接，利用隐私轮廓为异构机制的组合提供数值计算过程，从而实现对复杂组合机制的紧致、模块化、端到端认证。该方法可以利用已有的差分隐私机制分析结果。具体实例化到DP-SGD和Deep Partition Aggregation结合推理时平滑的场景，推导出针对训练时和推理时攻击的联合鲁棒性保证。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验证明了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个有原则且通用的框架，利用复合机制在更复杂的威胁模型下认证鲁棒性，更好地捕捉了现实世界攻击者的能力。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>
