Specifically, given gradient predictions $m_t$, we seek regret bounds that scale with the cumulative prediction error $S_T=\sum_{t=1}^T \|\nabla f_t(x_t)-m_t\|^2.$ We first establish a negative result: under the single-point feedback protocol, an unavoidable $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound persists even when $S_T=o(T)$, showing that the variance of gradient estimation fundamentally obscures the benefit of accurate predictions. To overcome this barrier, we propose \emph{Two-Point Variance-Reduced Optimistic Gradient Descent} (TP-VR-OPT) for the two-point feedback setting. The key idea is a novel variance-reduced gradient estimator whose variance scales with the prediction error rather than the gradient norm. This yields a regret bound of $O\big(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]}\big),$ where $d$ is the decision dimension. Complementing this result, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound that scales as $Ω(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$, providing a fundamental characterization of the best achievable prediction-adaptive regret and showing that TP-VR-OPT is optimal up to a factor of $\sqrt d$. We further develop adaptive variants that eliminate the need for prior knowledge of $\mathbb{E}[S_T]$ or the horizon $T$, and extend our framework to non-stationary environments, establishing dynamic regret guarantees that adapt simultaneously to the cumulative prediction error and the comparator path length.

</details>


### [110] [Reinforced Graph of Thoughts: RL-Driven Adaptive Prompting for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22195)
*Manuel Noah Riesen,Peter Alfred von Niederhäusern*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出强化图思维（RGoT），利用强化学习自动生成操作图，替代GoT中需要人工定义且缺乏适应性的静态操作图，在特定约束下能够根据任务复杂度自适应构建操作图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GoT虽能通过图结构组织LLM的思维进行复杂问题求解，但其操作图需人工手动定义，要求对问题解决方案有深入理解，且静态操作图缺乏适应性，无法根据不同任务动态调整。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）从人工定义的候选操作集中自适应地生成操作图，替代GoT中固定不变的人工定义操作图。

Result: 在特定约束条件下，能够以自动化方式根据任务复杂度自适应地构建操作图。

Conclusion: RGoT实现了GoT范式中操作图的自动化生成与自适应调整，为LLM提示范式提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph of Thoughts (GoT), a generalized form of recent prompting paradigms for large language models (LLMs), has been shown to be useful for elaborate problem solving. By executing a graph of operations, thoughts of the LLM are structured as an arbitrary graph, forming the actual graph of thoughts. Originally, the graph of operations is defined manually, which requires in-depth knowledge about the solution of the problem to solve. Such a static graph of operations is rigid and therefore lacks adaptability. We propose Reinforced Graph of Thoughts (RGoT), an automated approach to the GoT prompting paradigm that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to adaptively generate a graph of operations from a human-defined set. Results indicate that, under certain constraints, it is possible to construct graphs of operations adaptively to the task's complexity in an automated way.

</details>


### [111] [Survive or Collapse: The Asymmetric Roles of Data Gating and Reward Grounding in Self-Play RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22217)
*Sophia Xiao Pu,Zhaotian Weng,Chengzhi Liu,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验发现，自对弈强化学习的稳定性主要由数据级门控（决定哪些生成任务进入训练池）决定，而非奖励设计。严格的门控足以保证稳定，而奖励设计在无门控时无法阻止崩溃。文章还揭示了"有依据的提议者悖论"：拥有真实标签的提议者反而会加速模型崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈强化学习在提升推理能力方面表现优异，但普遍存在训练崩溃和不稳定问题，且其机制尚不明确。现有研究主要将其归因于奖励设计缺陷，作者认为需要重新审视稳定性的真正控制因素。

Method: 在Python输出预测任务和确定性DSL双任务上进行控制实验，分离预训练先验、输出模糊性和执行器噪声的影响。通过对比数据级门控（二值/连续严格度参数ε）与多种奖励变体（包括无真实标签的自一致性奖励）的效果，分析两者对稳定性的不对称影响。

Result: 严格的数据门控在任何奖励变体下都能保证稳定；移除门控后，任何奖励变体都无法阻止崩溃。存在两阶段相变：低ε时训练侧指标解耦，高ε时验证准确率才下降。有真实标签的提议者与自一致性求解器组合时，会通过聚焦于干净任务加速收敛到伪自一致性吸引子，导致更快崩溃。

Conclusion: 数据级门控是自对弈稳定性的刚性约束，而非奖励校准。这一发现挑战了当前以奖励设计为主导的研究范式，提出了"有依据的提议者悖论"这一反直觉现象，揭示了自对弈系统中数据筛选机制的核心作用。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning trains language models on their own generated tasks, co-evolving a proposer and solver without human labels. Recent systems report strong reasoning gains, but collapse and instability are widely observed and poorly understood. The dominant response treats this as a reward-design problem. We argue instead that self-play stability is governed by two distinct levers: a data-level gate that decides which proposer-generated tasks enter the training pool, and the reward signal that updates the policy on tasks already admitted. Through controlled experiments on a Python output-prediction task and a deterministic-DSL twin task that strips pretraining priors, output ambiguity, and executor noise, we find the two levers are asymmetric. A strict gate is sufficient for stability under every reward variant we test, including a self-consistency reward with no access to ground truth; while no reward variant is sufficient once the gate is removed. This asymmetry exposes a counter-intuitive coupling we call the Grounded Proposer Paradox: a proposer with ground-truth access accelerates collapse faster than an ungrounded one when paired with a self-consistency solver, by concentrating training on clean tasks that form the fastest path to a spurious self-consistent attractor. Replacing the binary gate with a continuous strictness parameter $\varepsilon$ further reveals a two-stage phase transition: training-side metrics decouple at low $\varepsilon$, while validation accuracy holds until $\varepsilon$ is much higher. Data-level gating, not reward calibration, is the binding constraint on self-play stability.

</details>


### [112] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了基于累积轨迹训练的decoder-only transformer在回溯搜索中存在的两个问题（分散检索和历史纠缠），并提出了定位化（localization）和选择性状态注意力（SSA）两种修复方法，使模型能根据当前搜索状态而非完整历史做出决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在回溯搜索中，使用累积轨迹训练时，模型依赖于整个历史轨迹而非仅当前搜索状态做出决策，这与最优决策原则（相同状态应有相同可行延续）相悖。

Method: 提出两种修复：1) 定位化（localization）——在轨迹级别重写每个决策块，将状态特征局部暴露；2) 选择性状态注意力（SSA）——固定注意力掩码，在不修改训练数据、目标或参数的情况下，强制基于状态的决策。

Result: 在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析任务上，SSA使相同状态但不同历史轨迹的样本输出一致决策，而因果基线则输出不同决策。

Conclusion: 累积轨迹训练导致的分散检索和历史纠缠是transformer在序列化轨迹数据上的诊断性失败，SSA提供了结构性修复，且推理时上下文清除可作为无需重训的替代方案。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [113] [ARC-STAR: Auditable Post-Hoc Correction for PDE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22222)
*Chengze Li,Lingwei Wei,Li Sun,Hongbo Lv,Jie Yang,Hongrong Zhang,Kening Zheng,Wei-Chieh Huang,Enze Ma,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-STAR框架，一种针对预训练PDE基础模型的冻结求解器后验校正方法。该方法通过全局校正器消除求解器偏差、局部精炼器清理残差，以及基于标签无关评分的高风险区域计算路由，在不重新微调的前提下，将速度场滚动误差降低至少36倍，且全局阶段可减少原始误差91-99%，局部阶段进一步减少残差高达94.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的PDE基础模型在陌生流动场景中预测会逐步漂移，误差集中在少数区域，而重新训练会导致网络不稳定，且统一的校正方法忽略了误差的空间集中性。因此需要一种无需微调、可审计且预算感知的后验校正框架。

Method: ARC-STAR包含三个阶段：(1) 全局校正器消除求解器的整体偏差；(2) 分块局部精炼器清理全局校正后的残差；(3) 部署时利用标签无关评分在计算预算下将精炼路由到高风险区域。整个框架保持预训练求解器冻结，各阶段独立训练与评估。

Result: 在五个流动基准测试（涵盖十个流态单元）中，ARC-STAR是唯一将速度滚动误差在每个单元上较原始Poseidon降低至少36倍的方法。全局阶段将原始宿主误差减少91-99%，局部阶段进一步将剩余残差减少高达94.4%。

Conclusion: ARC-STAR通过其三阶段后验校正框架，在不微调预训练模型的前提下，有效解决了PDE基础模型在陌生流态下的误差漂移和空间集中问题，实现了显著的精度提升，同时保持了可审计性和计算效率。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) foundation models are pretrained networks that forecast how physical fields like velocity and pressure evolve from a single reusable solver. On unfamiliar flows their predictions drift step by step, errors concentrate in a few regions, yet retraining destabilizes the network and uniform post-hoc correction overlooks this spatial concentration. To address this, we propose a frozen-solver post-hoc correction framework, Adaptive Risk-Calibrated Spatial Triage for Auditable Refinement (ARC-STAR). ARC-STAR organizes correction into three stages: a global corrector removes broad solver bias, a blockwise local refiner cleans the post-global residual, and, at deployment, a label-free score routes refinement to high-risk blocks under a compute budget. The framework is designed to be (i) frozen-host, preserving the pretrained solver without fine-tuning; (ii) auditable, with global and local stages trained and evaluated separately for measurable contributions; and (iii) budget-aware, using a blockwise interface that either refines the full field or routes limited compute to high-risk regions. Across five flow benchmarks spanning ten regime cells, ARC-STAR is the only method that cuts velocity rollout error by at least 36x over raw Poseidon on every cell. The global stage reduces raw host error by 91-99%, and the local stage further reduces the remaining post-global residual by up to 94.4%. Our code implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/arc_star.

</details>


### [114] [How Many Different Outputs Can a Transformer Generate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22223)
*Maxime Meyer,Mario Michelessa,Caroline Chaux,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 仅利用Transformer架构的几个特征，就能定性和定量地准确预测其能输出的不同序列数量。提供了关于提示长度的上界，实验证明该上界与真实值相差不超过一个数量级。同时从理论上解释了Transformer在复制、填充等简单序列任务上失效的原因。证明了：(i)可访问序列的最大长度随提示长度线性增长；(ii)超过临界阈值后，可访问序列比例随序列长度指数衰减；(iii)提示长度与可访问序列长度的线性系数存在理论上界。这些结论在上下文和计算时间无界的情况下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅利用Transformer架构的少量特征，能否准确预测其可输出的不同序列数量，并解释先前实验中观察到Transformer在简单序列任务上失效的现象。

Method: 通过理论分析建立Transformer可输出序列数量的上界，并利用实验验证该上界的紧致性（误差在10倍以内）。从数学上证明可访问序列长度与提示长度的线性关系、指数衰减规律以及线性系数的理论上界。

Result: (i)可访问序列最大长度随提示长度线性增长；(ii)超过临界阈值后，可访问序列比例指数衰减；(iii)线性系数存在理论上界。且这些结果在无界上下文和计算时间下仍成立。

Conclusion: Transformer架构本身存在表达能力的固有限制，即使提供无限上下文和计算资源，其可输出的序列多样性也受到严格约束，这解释了其在简单序列任务上的失败原因。

Abstract: We study how we can leverage only a handful of characteristics of a transformer's architecture to closely predict the number of different sequences it can output, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We provide an upper bound depending on the length of the prompt, which we show empirically to be tight up to a factor less than 10, across architectures and model sizes. Our analysis also provides a theoretical explanation for previously observed empirical failures of transformers on simple sequence tasks, such as copying and cramming. Formally, we prove that (i) the maximal length of accessible sequences (those that the transformer can output for some prompt) grows linearly with the prompt length, (ii) beyond a critical threshold, the proportion of accessible sequences decays exponentially with sequence length, and (iii) the linear coefficient relating prompt length to accessible sequence length admits a theoretical upper bound. Notably, these results hold even with unbounded context and computation time.

</details>


### [115] [Holomorphic Neural ODEs with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Interpretable Discovery of Complex Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22235)
*Bhaskar Ranjan Karn,Dinesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holomorphic KAN-ODE框架，用KAN替代MLP学习全纯动力系统，仅需280参数（MLP的1/16）即可达到$R^2>0.95$的速度场拟合，自动提取符号方程，Julia集重建精度达98.0%，且噪声鲁棒性显著优于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全纯动力系统具有复解析几何结构，但传统MLP违背Cauchy-Riemann条件、缺乏复解析先验且无法提取控制方程，亟需一种符合全纯结构、可解释且参数高效的建模方法。

Method: 将Neural ODE中的MLP替换为KAN（边缘可学习B样条激活），并引入Cauchy-Riemann方程作为可微正则化项来强制保持全纯结构。

Result: 在6个全纯动力系统（多项式和超越类）上：280参数下速度场$R^2>0.95$；自动拟合出全部6个符号方程族；Julia集重建匹配度达98.0%；10%噪声下MSE仅退化4%（MLP退化15.2倍）；二次到三次动力学的迁移学习提升90.4%。

Conclusion: KAN作为参数高效、可解释的MLP替代方案，在全纯动力系统的物理信息发现中具有独特优势，能提供符号方程、强制执行全纯结构并具备强噪声鲁棒性，弥补了黑盒架构的不足。

Abstract: Complex dynamical systems governed by holomorphic maps such as $z^2 + c$ exhibit fractal boundaries with extreme sensitivity to initial conditions. Accurately modelling these structures from data requires methods that respect the underlying complex-analytic geometry, yet Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) within Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (Neural ODEs) lack complex-analytic priors, violate the Cauchy--Riemann conditions, and function as opaque approximators incapable of yielding governing equations. We introduce Holomorphic KAN-ODE, a framework that replaces the MLP with a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) whose learnable B-spline activations reside on network edges, and incorporates Cauchy--Riemann equations as a differentiable regularization to preserve holomorphic structure. We evaluate on six families of complex dynamical systems spanning polynomial and transcendental classes. With only 280 parameters ($16\times$ fewer than the MLP baseline), the network achieves velocity-field $R^2 > 0.95$ on all six systems, correctly identifies all six governing symbolic families through automatic spline-to-formula fitting, and reconstructs Julia set fractal boundaries with up to 98.0\% agreement. Crucially, the model exhibits only 4\% MSE degradation under 10\% observation noise versus $15.2\times$ for MLPs, and achieves 90.4\% improvement in transfer learning from quadratic to cubic dynamics. While the MLP attains lower pointwise reconstruction error due to its larger capacity, the KAN uniquely provides interpretable symbolic equations, enforced holomorphic structure, and superior noise resilience, capabilities that are entirely absent in black-box architectures. These results establish KANs as a parameter-efficient, interpretable alternative to MLPs for physics-informed discovery of holomorphic dynamics.

</details>


### [116] [Decomposing Ensemble Spread in Lorenz '96 With Learned Stochastic Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22242)
*Birgit Kühbacher,Daan Crommelin,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用两尺度Lorenz 1996系统作为受控试验台，系统研究了混沌天气和气候预报中多种不确定性来源（内在变率、初始条件扰动和随机模型不确定性）的相互作用，发现集合扰动不增加长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关速度。随机参数化方案，尤其是具有时间持续结构的方法，能够增强早期集合发散并改善发散度-误差一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报和气候预测存在固有不确定性，而业务集合预报常出现欠发散（spread growing too slowly）问题，即集合发散度增长过慢，无法充分反映实际预报误差。现有方法对内在变率、初始条件扰动和随机模型不确定性如何相互作用缺乏系统理解。

Method: 以两尺度Lorenz 1996系统为受控试验平台，设计系统化方法分离内在变率、初始条件扰动和随机模型不确定性。对比多种集合配置和参数化策略，包括现有确定性、自回归方法以及新颖的贝叶斯方法和基于流的方法。

Result: 集合扰动不增加系统长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关速度及其对不变测度的探索速率。具有时间持续结构的随机参数化方案能有效增强早期集合发散度增长，并改善发散度-误差一致性。

Conclusion: 厘清了混沌系统中不同不确定性来源的相互作用机制，为天气和气候模型中随机参数化的设计和评估提供了指导性建议。

Abstract: Weather and climate forecasts are inherently uncertain due to chaotic dynamics, imperfect initial conditions, and incomplete representation of the underlying physical processes. Operational ensemble forecasts aim to represent these uncertainties through forecast spread, yet many approaches yield underdispersive estimates, with spread that grows too slowly relative to forecast error. Using the two-scale Lorenz 1996 system as a widely used, controlled testbed, we design a systematic approach to disentangle intrinsic variability, initial-condition perturbations, and stochastic model uncertainty. We compare multiple ensemble configurations and parameterization strategies, including existing deterministic and autoregressive as well as novel Bayesian and flow-based approaches. Our results show that ensemble perturbations do not increase the system's long-term variance; rather, they regulate how rapidly trajectories decorrelate and explore the invariant measure. Stochastic parameterizations, particularly those with temporally persistent structure, enhance early spread growth and improve spread-error consistency. Overall, we bring clarity to how different sources of uncertainty interact in a chaotic system and provide guidance for the design and evaluation of stochastic parameterizations in weather and climate models.

</details>


### [117] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种探索性AI推荐框架，通过可解释AI技术自动识别特征排除、非线性项和特征交互三种推荐类型，帮助提升传统可解释统计模型的预测性能。在245,614名患者的跌倒风险预测任务中，C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校正效果也得到改善，所有推荐均得到文献支持，并在两个公开数据集上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型在面对高维特征时难以手工优化特征选择、变换和交互建模；复杂机器学习模型虽性能高但"黑箱"特性限制了临床信任和可解释性。现有方法难以在保持模型透明性的同时提升预测性能。

Method: 开发了Exploratory AI Recommender框架，利用灵活的AI建模捕获复杂数据模式，结合可解释AI技术将模式转化为三类推荐：特征排除、非线性项和特征交互。以Cox比例风险模型为基础，将推荐结果增补到模型中与传统基线模型对比评估。

Result: 在245,614名患者的跌倒风险主分析中：推荐排除23个特征、加入2个非线性项和221个特征交互。C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准截距从-0.006改善至0.003，斜率从1.063改善至0.950。所有推荐均有文献支持，并在两个额外公开数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的Exploratory AI Recommender证明了可解释AI和数据驱动的研究设计能够有效改进高维透明预测模型的开发过程及预测性能，在保持模型可解释性的同时缩小了与传统机器学习方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [118] [No Epoch Like the Present: Robust Climate Emulation Requires Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22248)
*Bradley Stanley-Clamp,Anson Lei,Hannah M. Christensen,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出气候仿真是一个分布外（OOD）预测任务，当前机器学习仿真器在气候变化导致的分布偏移下可靠性不足。作者证明季节性变化可作为长期气候偏移的有效代理，并据此提出利用季节偏移的评估框架。研究发现现有混合ML仿真器在此类偏移下性能显著下降，而基于物理先验的组合泛化方法能在保持分布内性能的同时大幅提升OOD鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML气候仿真器在分布内数据上表现优异，但气候变化会引发大气状态分布的系统性偏移（分布外问题），其未来可靠性是未经验证的盲点。现有评估协议无法捕捉这种长期分布偏移，亟需新的理解、评估和设计范式。

Method: 1) 通过统计检验证实气候变化驱动显著且渐进增长的分布偏移；2) 建立季节性变化作为长期气候偏移的实证代理；3) 提出利用季节偏移作为零开销测试床的评估框架；4) 基于组合泛化（将观测到的基本组件进行新组合）设计物理驱动的分解方法提升OOD性能。

Result: 当前最先进的混合ML仿真器在真实分布偏移下性能显著退化；基于物理动机的分解方法能在仅牺牲少量分布内性能的条件下，大幅提升OOD泛化能力。

Conclusion: 气候仿真须从分布内拟合转向分布外鲁棒性设计，组合泛化结合物理先验是实现这一目标的有效路径，有望构建能适应未知未来气候的ML仿真器。

Abstract: Climate emulation is an out-of-distribution (OOD) projection task. This is precisely the challenge where modern Machine Learning (ML) methods are most prone to failure. Consequently, while current ML emulators trained on present climate achieve high in-distribution performance, their future reliability under the inevitable distribution shifts of a changing climate remains a critical, poorly understood blind spot. Addressing this challenge requires a fundamental shift in how we understand, evaluate, and design climate emulators. In this work, we first confirm that climate change drives a statistically significant and progressively growing shift in atmospheric state distributions, rendering standard evaluation protocols insufficient. We empirically establish that seasonal variation serves as an effective proxy for these long-term climate shifts, providing access to $\textit{real-world}$ distribution shifts without recourse to heuristics like synthetic perturbations. Motivated by this link, we introduce a novel evaluation framework that leverages seasonal shifts as a rigorous, zero-overhead testbed for emulator robustness. Our systematic characterisation confirms that current state-of-the-art hybrid-ML emulators degrade significantly under these realistic shifts. Finally, we chart a path forward by identifying compositional generalisation, the ability to form novel combinations from observed elementary components, as a principled route towards robust climate emulation. We demonstrate that physically motivated decompositions substantially improve OOD performance with only modest trade-offs against in-distribution performance, providing an avenue towards ML-driven climate emulators robust to an unknown future.

</details>


### [119] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-based形式定理证明器对问题的表面变化高度敏感，本文引入重写范畴的范畴论框架来形式化两种对称性概念，并提出测试时聚合策略来恢复成功不变性，提升鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM形式定理证明器对语义等价但表面形式不同的问题表现出截然不同的证明成功率，缺乏对形式数学中固有结构对称性的尊重，亟需理解并引入正确的对称性归纳偏置。

Method: 引入重写范畴（rewriting categories）范畴论框架，形式化证明等变性（proof equivariance）和成功不变性（success invariance）两种对称性；提出测试时对等价重写进行聚合的方法，理论上证明可在采样极限下恢复成功不变性。

Result: 状态基的下一步策略证明器自然满足证明等变性；而现有LLM证明器两种对称性均不满足，性能波动大。所提测试时聚合方法在固定推理预算下提升了鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算是实用的近似途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [120] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文感知与领域知识增强的异构传感器融合方法，通过建立直接、指示性和上下文信息的三级证据层次，融合OSINT环境信息，并构建贝叶斯威胁分类机制（含领域知识先验），在仿真场景下实现高达95%的分类准确率，对杂波和先验失配具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个传感器仅能检测部分CBRNE威胁子集且可靠性不一，高杂波率影响融合系统，且高质量数据集有限制约了基于学习的检测分类模型发展。

Method: 1) 建立包含直接信息、指示性信息和上下文信息的新型证据层次结构；2) 通过收集、处理OSINT数据将环境上下文信息引入融合过程；3) 利用证据层次各层级构建具有领域知识先验的贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制。

Result: 在仿真场景下，整体分类准确率高达95%，对杂波和先验失配表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知与领域知识增强融合方法能有效缓解传感器层面的不足，提升CBRNE威胁检测、定位与分类的性能。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [121] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），针对在线自蒸馏（OPSD）在复杂推理中因统一教师方向抑制探索导致性能退化的问题，根据token熵值动态调整蒸馏方向：高熵token推离教师以保留探索，低熵token拉近教师以稳定步骤执行。在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏Avg@16上超过强RLVR和自蒸馏基线，兼顾了探索保留与步骤执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏方法在复杂推理任务中因对模型自身推理过程中的所有token施加统一的教师监督方向，导致高不确定性token的探索行为被抑制，从而削弱了复杂推理能力。

Method: 提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），根据每个token的熵值动态决定蒸馏方向：高熵token被推离特权教师以保留探索能力，低熵token被拉向教师以稳定步骤级执行精度。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏Avg@16上超越强RLVR和自蒸馏基线；Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明，平均增益来源于在不牺牲步骤执行精度的前提下保留了探索能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过熵路由的方向自适应蒸馏机制有效解决了OPSD在复杂推理中的退化问题，证明了在自蒸馏中保留探索与保持步骤精度可以兼得。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [122] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级几何信号，通过测量客户端局部训练对输入空间划分的改变（基于共享探针集上的激活模式），量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差，用于联邦学习中监控客户端行为和实现风险感知聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性导致更新不稳定和全局性能下降，且实际部署中客户端更新还可能因分布偏移或异常输入而偏离预期行为，现有方法难以区分良性非独立同分布与功能性异常偏离。

Method: 设计一个置换不变且可解释的度量，基于共享探针集上客户端模型与全局模型的激活诱导输入空间划分的差异，来量化客户端-全局功能偏离程度。

Result: 该信号能有效识别引起非典型功能变化的客户端，区分稳定但异构的客户端与显著偏离全局状态的更新。

Conclusion: 该度量为联邦学习系统提供了一种简单的客户端行为监控工具，支持风险感知的聚合策略。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [123] [Adaptive Measurement Allocation for Learning Kernelized SVMs Under Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22275)
*Artur Miroszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应测量分配策略，用于从噪声伯努利观测中学习核化支持向量机（SVM），通过结合几何敏感性和主动集不稳定性两种互补原则，将测量预算集中于最影响决策的核矩阵区域。理论分析和实验表明，自适应分配在固定测量预算下显著提升了支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度，并可根据核重要性结构的异质性选择自适应或均匀策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法通常假设核矩阵可无噪声精确获取，但在量子机器学习等新兴场景中，每个核条目需从噪声观测中推断，且精度取决于有限测量预算的分配方式。然而现有方法主要采用均匀分配，忽略了核化分类器对核矩阵的高度非均匀依赖性。

Method: 提出自适应测量分配策略，结合两个互补原则：(i) 几何敏感性——捕捉单个核条目扰动对分类器间隔的影响；(ii) 主动集不稳定性——量化测量噪声导致支持向量成员离散变化的概率。据此定义任务感知的分配方案，将测量集中于核矩阵中最具决策关键性的区域。

Result: 理论分析表明自适应分配的优势取决于核重要性结构的异质性，存在自适应或均匀策略各自适用的不同区间。在合成数据集上，自适应分配在固定测量预算下显著改善了支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度。基于双系数稳定性准则的早停机制可在仅使用部分测量成本时实现接近最优的性能。在真实数据量子核上的实验展现了与核浓缩等已知现象一致的区间依赖行为。

Conclusion: 自适应测量分配策略能有效提升噪声环境下核化SVM的学习性能，其适用性取决于核重要性结构的异质性程度，为量子机器学习等场景中的核方法应用提供了新的分配范式。

Abstract: Kernel methods are typically formulated under the assumption of exact, noise-free access to the Gram matrix. However, in emerging settings such as quantum machine learning, each kernel entry must be inferred from noisy observations, and its accuracy depends on how a limited measurement budget is allocated. Despite this, existing approaches overwhelmingly rely on uniform allocation, which equalizes estimator variance but ignores the highly non-uniform dependence of kernelized classifiers on the Gram matrix. In this work, we introduce an adaptive measurement-allocation strategy for learning kernelized Support Vector Machines (SVMs) from noisy Bernoulli observations. Our approach combines two complementary principles: (i) geometric sensitivity, capturing how perturbations of individual kernel entries affect the classifier margin, and (ii) active-set instability, quantifying the probability of discrete changes in support-vector membership induced by measurement noise. These signals define a task-aware allocation scheme that concentrates measurements on the most decision-critical regions of the kernel matrix. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that the benefit of adaptive allocation is governed by the heterogeneity of the induced kernel importance structure, leading to distinct regimes in which adaptive or uniform strategies are preferable. Empirical evaluations on synthetic datasets demonstrate that adaptive allocation significantly improves support-vector recovery, margin estimation, and decision-function accuracy under fixed measurement budgets. A dual-coefficient stability criterion further enables early stopping, achieving near-optimal performance while using only a fraction of the measurement cost. Additional experiments on quantum kernels derived from real-world data reveal a regime-dependent behavior aligned with known phenomena such as kernel concentration. Together...

</details>


### [124] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel多会话咨询数据集和EmoTrack抑郁追踪框架，结合LLM提取的临床信号和冻结的语义嵌入，在单会话和多会话情景下实现稳健的PHQ-8预测，在DAIC-WOZ上较最强基线MAE降低13.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的AI心理健康咨询中，需通过对话记录监测抑郁严重程度并及时标记需要人工干预的会话。现有方法存在两难：微调方法监督丰富但数据稀缺时泛化差，提示方法数据高效但缺乏对纵向上下文的支持。亟需一种在单会话和多会话情景下均能稳健预测抑郁的方法。

Method: 提出EmoTrack框架：1) 利用LLM从转录中提取临床信号；2) 结合冻结的turn级语义嵌入形成转录表示；3) 训练症状特异性预测器。当存在先前会话时，通过紧凑的跨会话记忆机制整合历史信息。同时构建LongCounsel数据集，提供会话级PHQ-8标注以支持重复会话追踪。

Result: 在DAIC-WOZ基准上，EmoTrack相比最强基线实现13.5%的MAE相对降低；在LongCounsel上与最强纵向基线方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 结合LLM临床信号提取与冻结语义嵌入的混合方法，能够在不牺牲数据效率的前提下提升抑郁预测的鲁棒性，为单会话和多会话情景下的抑郁监测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [125] [Long-term Fairness with Selective Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22291)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对选择性标签（仅正向决策可观测标签）场景下的长期公平性问题，提出了一种结合观测数据与标签预测模型来估计真实公平性指标的新框架，并基于理论推导设计了强化学习算法，在半合成环境中实现了接近理想标签访问水平的公平性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期公平算法假设标签完全可观测，但在借贷、招聘等实际场景中，标签（如偿还能力）仅在正向决策（如批准贷款）后才被揭示，构成选择性标签问题。作者论证了朴素方法无法保证公平性，因此需要专门针对该场景设计解决方案。

Method: 将真实公平性指标分解为可观测公平性与标签预测偏差两部分；利用预测模型的置信度推导出从可观测量保证真实公平性的充分条件；基于理论结果提出一种新的强化学习算法，用于选择性标签下的长期公平决策。

Result: 在半合成环境中，所提算法在公平性和性能指标上均达到了与拥有真实标签访问权限的理想agent相当的水平。

Conclusion: 选择性标签场景下长期公平性需要专门处理，所提出的分解框架与强化学习算法能够有效解决该问题，弥合了理论与实际部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Long-term fairness algorithms aim to satisfy fairness beyond static and short-term notions by accounting for the dynamics between decision-making policies and population behavior. Most previous approaches evaluate performance and fairness measures from observable features and a label, which is assumed to be fully observed. However, in scenarios such as hiring or lending, the labels (e.g., ability to repay the loan) are selective labels as they are only revealed based on positive decisions (e.g., when a loan is granted). In this paper, we study long-term fairness in the selective labels setting and analytically show that naive solutions do not guarantee fairness. To address this gap, we then introduce a novel framework that leverages both the observed data and a label predictor model to estimate the true fairness measure value by decomposing it into the observed fairness and bias from label predictions. This allows us to derive sufficient conditions to satisfy true fairness from observable quantities by using the confidence in the predictor model. Finally, we rely on our theoretical results to propose a novel reinforcement learning algorithm for effective long-term fair decision-making with selective labels. In semisynthetic environments, the proposed algorithm reached comparable fairness and performance to an agent with oracle access to the true labels.

</details>


### [126] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR)方法，基于重尾自正则化理论为Transformer各层分配不同学习率，在LLaMA、GPT-nano等模型上实现最高1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习中所有层使用统一学习率的做法忽略了Transformer架构的结构异质性，限制了LLM的性能潜力。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化理论，通过分析权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度量化各层的重尾程度，为弱重尾层分配较大学习率加速训练，强重尾层分配较小学习率，实现层间平衡训练。

Result: 在多种架构（LLaMA到GPT-nano）、优化器（AdamW和Muon）和参数规模（60M-1B）上，实现最高1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且调参开销低。

Conclusion: LLR通过层级别差异化学习率策略有效提升了Transformer训练效率和泛化性能，且能直接从基准统一学习率迁移近似最优设置。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [127] [Chebyshev Policies and the Mountain Car Problem: Reinforcement Learning for Low-Dimensional Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22305)
*Stefan Huber,Hannes Unger,Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过解析求解Mountain Car问题的36年未解的最优控制方案，发现现代RL算法与最优解之间存在巨大差距，并基于此提出了Chebyshev策略——一种可替代神经网络的轻量级通用策略类，在多个RL任务中参数量减少277倍、后悔值降低4.18倍，显著提升样本效率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补Mountain Car问题36年来缺乏解析最优控制解的空白，并揭示现代RL算法与最优解之间的性能差距；同时受最优控制分析启发，设计更高效、可解释的RL策略类。

Method: 1) 解析求解Mountain Car的最优控制问题，推导出闭式最优策略；2) 基于最优控制结构，从第一性原理提出Chebyshev策略——使用Chebyshev多项式作为基函数的策略参数化方法，可直接替换神经网络作为策略网络；3) 在多个RL算法（PPO、ARS、REINFORCE）和任务（含真实非线性运动控制平台）上进行实验验证。

Result: Chebyshev策略相比神经网络：参数量减少277倍，后悔值降低4.18倍；在PPO、ARS、REINFORCE算法下均一致优于神经网络；在真实非线性运动控制测试平台上同样表现更优。

Conclusion: Chebyshev策略是低维控制任务中神经网络的一种极具竞争力的轻量级替代方案，能够在保持高性能的同时大幅减少参数、提升样本效率和可解释性，并具备实时推理能力。

Abstract: We analytically solve the Mountain Car problem, a canonical benchmark in RL, and derive an optimal control solution, closing a gap after 36 years. This enables us to reveal two surprising insights: The optimal control is quite simple, yet modern RL agents display a large gap to optimality. Motivated by the analysis of the optimal control, we introduce Chebyshev policies as a universal (i.e. dense) class of RL policies from first principles. They can be trained as drop-in replacements of neural nets, reducing the regret by a factor of 4.18, while requiring 277 times fewer parameters, fostering sample efficiency, explainability and realtime capability. Chebyshev policies are evaluated on further RL tasks, including a real-world nonlinear motion control testbed. They consistently improve performance over neural nets with PPO, ARS and REINFORCE. Our results demonstrate how Chebyshev policies offer a compelling and lightweight alternative or addition to neural nets for low-dimensional control tasks.

</details>


### [128] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SepsisAI-Orchestrator开源模块化平台，解决早期脓毒症检测模型从研究到临床部署的工程鸿沟。该平台集成FHIR兼容的CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（REST API）和Streamlit仪表盘，通过Kubernetes编排。在12线程CPU上，负载测试（50-1000并发用户）发现副本数需匹配物理线程数：从3副本扩展到12副本将p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（降低57.3%），消除全部请求失败；过度扩展到24或48副本因调度竞争导致性能下降。首次量化了临床AI推理工作负载中的U型扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽然预测性能优异，但因系统层面障碍（异构数据表示、缺乏标准化部署流程、研究原型与医院环境并发/延迟需求不匹配）难以转化到临床床旁使用。现有文献缺乏对临床AI推理负载下水平扩展行为的量化表征。

Method: 构建SepsisAI-Orchestrator平台：集成HL7 FHIR风格的CDA数据预处理管道、NoSQL存储（MongoDB/Elasticsearch）、容器化LightGBM分类器（通过REST API暴露）、Streamlit临床仪表盘，使用Docker和Kubernetes编排。对预先验证的LightGBM模型（在PhysioNet 2019上F1 0.87-0.94）不做修改，仅构建和表征基础设施。使用k6工具进行50-1000并发虚拟用户的负载测试，系统化变更副本数量（3/6/12/24/48）并测量p95延迟和请求失败率。

Result: 在12线程CPU主机上，3副本时p95延迟3.3s且有请求失败；副本数增至12（匹配线程数）时p95降至1.41s（降57.3%），零请求失败；继续增至24或48副本时延迟和失败率反而上升，呈现U型扩展曲线。这是首次对临床AI推理工作负载下此现象的量化表征。

Conclusion: 临床AI模型部署中，副本数量必须与主机的物理CPU线程数匹配，过度扩展会因Kubernetes调度器竞争导致性能退化。该平台和发现可指导同类临床AI系统的生产级部署配置。未进行前瞻性临床验证，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [129] [Riemannian geometry meets fMRI: the advantages of modeling correlation manifolds and eigenvector subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22334)
*Mario Severino,Manuela Moretto,Robert A. McCutcheon,Mattia Veronese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的几何框架，包含Off-log度量（将相关矩阵平滑映射到对称零对角矩阵，支持闭式距离、弗雷歇均值和线性模型）和Grassmannian子空间判别（通过主角度距离比较特征向量子空间），在两个临床队列（帕金森病、精神病）和三个老龄化fMRI数据集上验证，证明几何感知表征能提高敏感性和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准功能脑网络分析常将相关矩阵条目独立处理，忽略相关空间的弯曲几何结构；现有几何方法缺乏闭式操作或依赖任意区域排序，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出两个组件：(i) Off-log度量——将相关矩阵平滑变换为对称零对角矩阵，提供闭式距离、弗雷歇均值和线性模型；(ii) Grassmannian子空间判别——通过特征向量子空间之间的主角度距离比较被试，解决符号和基的歧义性。两者均可集成到标准机器学习流程中。

Result: 在帕金森病、精神病两个临床队列和三个老龄化fMRI数据集中，Off-log度量在置换检验中敏感性更高，分类性能匹配或超越黎曼和欧几里得基线；脑龄预测性能相当，Grassmannian方法持续优于欧几里得基线。

Conclusion: 几何感知表征能够提高敏感性和预测性能，同时保持可扩展性，易于大规模部署。

Abstract: Correlation matrices are fundamental summaries of functional brain networks, yet standard analyses often treat entries independently, ignoring the curved geometry of correlation space. Existing geometric methods frequently lack closed-form operations or depend on arbitrary region ordering, limiting scalability. We introduce a scalable geometric framework with two components: (i) the Off-log metric, a smooth transformation mapping correlation matrices to symmetric zero-diagonal matrices. This enables closed-form expressions for distances, Frechet means, and linear models, allowing standard statistical modeling without complex manifold optimization. (ii) Grassmannian subspace discrimination, which compares subjects via principal-angle distances between eigenvector subspaces, resolving inherent sign and basis ambiguities. Both components integrate into standard machine-learning workflows for inference, regression, and classification. Validated across two clinical cohorts (Parkinson's and psychosis) and three ageing fMRI datasets, the Off-log metric increased sensitivity in permutation tests and matched or exceeded Riemannian and Euclidean baselines in classification. Brain-age prediction performance was comparable, with Riemannian metrics excelling in two of three cohorts. The Grassmannian method consistently outperformed Euclidean baselines, highlighting disease-relevant networks. Overall, geometry-aware representations improve sensitivity and predictive performance while remaining straightforward to deploy at scale.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Causal Orderings for In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22335)
*Sascha Xu,Sarah Mameche,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabOrder模型，在表格数据预测中同时推断和强制执行因果结构（拓扑变量顺序），通过因果顺序约束注意力机制，仅基于目标变量之前的特征进行预测，在标准函数模型类下验证了无监督学习最优变量排序的合理性，并在预测、插补和真实生物数据干预任务中取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据的上下文学习主要依赖相关性结构，在分布偏移或干预下不可靠；而因果发现方法通常专注于结构可识别性，与受益于因果结构的预测架构脱节。因此需要弥合这两个视角，在表格预测中同时推断和强制执行因果结构。

Method: 提出TabOrder模型，采用因果顺序约束注意力机制（causal order-constrained attention），仅基于学习到的因果顺序中目标变量之前的特征进行预测；通过基于似然的目标函数以无监督方式学习最优变量排序，并在标准函数模型类下论证该选择的合理性。

Result: TabOrder能够恢复准确的变量排序，有效处理预测和插补任务，并在干预下的真实生物数据中提供有意义的洞察。同时研究了表格数据中常见的样本缺失问题与因果方向识别的交互关系。

Conclusion: 因果结构推断与预测架构可以有效地结合，TabOrder通过因果顺序约束注意力机制实现了这一目标，在多种任务中表现出色，为表格数据预测提供了更可靠的因果基础。

Abstract: In-context learning for tabular data sets strong predictive standards in observational settings; it however primarily relies on correlational structure, which becomes unreliable under distribution shift or intervention. While established methods to discover causal structure exist, they are often focused on structure identifiability and decoupled from the predictive architectures that could benefit from them.
  To bridge these perspectives, we study how to simultaneously infer and enforce causal structure in the form of topological variable orderings into tabular prediction. Unlike standard architectures, our model TabOrder uses causal order-constrained attention, basing predictions only on features that precede a target under a learned causal order. Similar to causal discovery methods, TabOrder learns the optimal variable ordering in an unsupervised manner through a likelihood-based objective. We justify this choice under standard functional model classes and also study how sample missingness, a common challenge in tabular data, interacts with causal direction identification. Empirically, we confirm that TabOrder recovers accurate variable orderings while addressing prediction and imputation tasks, as well as gives insight into real-world biological data under intervention.

</details>


### [131] [Physics-Informed Generative Solver: Bridging Data-Driven Priors and Conservation Laws for Stable Spatiotemporal Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22338)
*Ziyuan Zhu,Keyu Hu,Zhifei Chen,Yuhao Shi,Ming Bao,Jing Zhao,Gang Wang,Haitan Xu,Jiadong Li,Qijun Zhao,Xiaodong Li,Minghui Lu,Yanfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息生成式求解器，通过分离稳定先验学习与推断时守恒律强制，从稀疏测量中重建连续物理场。该方法结合了Martingale正则化的分数匹配预训练和物理信息隐式分数采样，在声学和气象学领域验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的生成模型在从稀疏测量重建物理场时，可能产生违反支配动力学定律的状态，亟需一种既能学习数据先验又能强制执行物理守恒律的方法。

Method: 1) Martingale-Regularized Score Matching (MRSM)：用Score Fokker-Planck约束正则化分数预训练，获得动态稳定的先验；2) Physics-Informed Implicit Score Sampling (PIISS)：通过物理残差的梯度引导去噪轨迹，无需重训练即可将样本投影到可接受流形上。

Result: 在声学中协同生成压力和粒子速度，抑制空间混叠；在ERA5气象场极端稀疏观测下同样有效。

Conclusion: 建立了一个可推广的高维逆问题求解范式，弥合了生成式人工智能与第一性原理科学之间的差距。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is a central inverse problem, but data-driven generative models can produce states that violate governing dynamics. We introduce a physics-informed generative solver that separates stable prior learning from inference-time enforcement of conservation laws. Martingale-Regularized Score Matching regularizes score pretraining with a Score Fokker-Planck constraint, yielding a dynamically stable prior. Physics-Informed Implicit Score Sampling then guides denoising trajectories by gradients of physical residuals, projecting samples toward admissible manifolds without retraining. In acoustics, the method co-generates pressure and particle velocity from sparse sensors, enabling dense virtual arrays that suppress spatial aliasing. The same framework generalizes to real-world ERA5 meteorological fields under extreme sparsity. Together, this work establishes a rigorous and generalizable paradigm for solving high-dimensional inverse problems, bridging the gap between generative artificial intelligence and first-principles science.

</details>


### [132] [From Snapshots to Trajectories: Learning Single-Cell Gene Expression Dynamics via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22340)
*Siyu Pu,Qingqing Long,Xiaohan Huang,Haotian Chen,Jiajia Wang,Meng Xiao,Xiao Luo,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出scFM（单细胞流匹配）框架，通过耦合条件流匹配、双向速度场优化和分布级对齐正则化，解决时间序列scRNA-seq数据中未配对快照导致的轨迹推断不稳定和长时预测分布漂移问题，在插值与外推任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列scRNA-seq轨迹推断方法面临两大挑战：(i) 未配对的快照数据导致相邻时间点间的局部转换不明确，使得监督信号不稳定；(ii) 长期预测依赖重复积分，微小建模误差会累积并导致分布漂移。

Method: 提出scFM框架：(1) 计算相邻快照间的熵正则化最优传输（OT）耦合，构建软加权流匹配目标以学习时间相关速度场；(2) 学习双向速度场并利用其一致性来优化耦合，增强稀疏监督下的时间连贯性；(3) 引入分布级对齐和潜在动态正则化来锚定长程推演，缓解漂移。

Result: 在真实时间序列scRNA-seq数据集上，scFM在时间插值与外推的分布预测性能上均一致提升，实现了更准确的轨迹重建和时间一致的降维可视化。

Conclusion: scFM通过耦合条件流匹配结合双向一致性与分布级正则化，有效解决了未配对快照数据下轨迹推断的不稳定性与长时预测漂移问题，更忠实地恢复了潜在的时间基因表达动态。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) provides high-dimensional profiles of cellular states, enabling data-driven modeling of cellular dynamics over time. In practice, time-resolved scRNA-seq is collected at only a few discrete time points as unpaired snapshot populations, leaving substantial temporal gaps. This motivates trajectory inference at unmeasured time points. Existing methods mainly follow two directions, optimal-transport (OT) alignment provides distribution-level matching between observed snapshots, while continuous-time generative models support forecasting via learned dynamics. However, two challenges remain: (i) unpaired snapshots render local transitions between adjacent time points ambiguous, leading to unstable supervision; and (ii) long-horizon prediction relies on repeated integration, where small modeling errors compound and cause distribution drift. To address these challenges, we propose single-cell Flow Matching (scFM), a latent generative framework based on coupling-conditioned flow matching. First, we compute entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots and use them to construct soft, weighted flow-matching targets for learning time-dependent velocity fields. Second, we learn bidirectional velocity fields and leverage their consistency to refine couplings and improve temporal coherence under sparse supervision. Third, we introduce distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts and mitigate drift. Experiments on real-world time-series scRNA-seq datasets show that scFM consistently improves distributional prediction performance for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. Moreover, scFM yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations where intermediate time points are absent, indicating a more faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics.

</details>


### [133] [A Boundary-Layer Mechanism for One-Third Scaling in Online Softmax Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22341)
*Marcel Kühn,Yoon Thelge,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了硬标签分类中，使用平滑代理损失（如softmax交叉熵）导致训练动态产生幂律学习曲线的渐近机制。通过在线教师-学生模型分析发现，边界层宽度随学生-教师对齐度D增长而缩小至O(D^{-1})，固定学习率的随机梯度下降维持非零残差方差Δ，导致测试损失和泛化误差ε_g以α^{-1/3}幂律衰减，远慢于贝叶斯最优的α^{-1}。调整学习率调度可将ε_g改善至α^{-1/2}幂律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解硬标签分类中平滑代理损失与离散标签不匹配所导致的渐近学习行为，特别是幂律学习曲线的产生机制。

Method: 采用在线教师-学生模型进行理论分析，通过减去平均logit后研究热力学极限下的中心化变量动态（学生-教师对齐度D和残差学生方差Δ），结合边界层分析和固定学习率在线梯度下降的随机动力学。

Result: 测试损失和泛化误差ε_g随训练时间α以α^{-1/3}幂律衰减；调整学习率调度可将ε_g改善至α^{-1/2}幂律；理论预测与仿真结果一致。

Conclusion: 该机制是神经缩放定律谱解释的互补机制而非替代方案；数据结构（如相关高斯输入和白化预训练特征）可主导瞬态行为。

Abstract: Hard-label classification is usually trained with smooth surrogate losses, most prominently softmax cross-entropy. We isolate an asymptotic mechanism by which this mismatch between smooth surrogate and discrete labels produces power-law learning curves in an online teacher-student model. After subtracting the mean logit, the thermodynamic-limit dynamics close in centered variables: a growing centered student-teacher alignment $D$ and the residual student variance $Δ$. At late times, examples away from teacher decision boundaries are already classified confidently and contribute exponentially little. Only boundary layers of width $O(D^{-1})$ remain active, while the noise of fixed-learning-rate online gradient descent maintains a nonzero $Δ$. As a function of the training time $α$ the late-time solution yields a $α^{-1/3}$ power law not only for the test loss but also for the generalization error $ε_g$, i.e., one minus test accuracy. This is much slower than the $α^{-1}$ Bayes-optimal reference for the same model. We further show that learning-rate schedules can improve the generalization error towards a $ε_g \sim α^{-1/2}$ power law. Simulations support the predicted order parameter dynamics and learning curves. Controlled experiments with correlated Gaussian inputs and whitened pretrained features show that data structure can dominate transients. Therefore, our result is an asymptotic, complementary mechanism rather than an alternative to spectral explanations of neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [134] [Partial Fusion of Neural Networks: Efficient Tradeoffs Between Ensembles and Weight Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22350)
*Fabian Morelli,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络的局部融合方法，通过在集成学习与权重聚合之间插值，实现计算成本与性能的灵活权衡，并展示了基于部分最优传输的神经元匹配与聚合方法，以及将权重聚合/局部融合推广为集成模型的广义剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络集成虽然性能优越但计算成本高，权重聚合虽低成本但精度较低，缺乏一种可以在两者之间灵活权衡的方法。

Method: 提出局部融合（partial fusion），通过部分最优传输（partial optimal transport）识别并匹配最相似的神经元，仅聚合这些神经元的权重；同时将权重聚合与局部融合推广为集成模型的广义剪枝（generalized pruning），允许神经元被删除或线性组合。

Result: 局部融合能够在计算成本与性能之间实现灵活的权衡；广义剪枝应用于单个网络时，通过隔离、删除和线性组合相似神经元也能获得类似局部融合的收益。

Conclusion: 局部融合是一种有效的模型融合策略，可在集成与权重聚合之间平滑调节；广义剪枝视角为理解和优化神经网络融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Ensembles of neural networks typically outperform individual networks but incur large computational costs, whereas weight aggregation produces less costly, yet also less accurate, aggregate models. We introduce partial fusion of networks, which interpolates between ensembles and weight aggregation and thus allows for a flexible tradeoff between computational cost and performance. A direct way to achieve this is to extend existing weight aggregation methods based on neuron-level similarity between different networks, where partial fusion then only aggregates weights of neurons which are most similar. We showcase one particular method to jointly identify which neurons are most similar and match them via partial optimal transport. Further, we consider the more general perspective of weight aggregation and partial fusion as generalized pruning of ensemble models, where neurons cannot just be deleted, but also linearly combined. Finally, we show that generalized pruning applied to a single network yields similar benefits as partial fusion by allowing for a tradeoff between isolating, deleting, and linearly combining neurons based on similarity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Fabian-Mor/partial_fusion_nn.

</details>


### [135] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VeriScale框架，通过对抗性实现驱动的测试用例扩增与精简，生成高质量基准测试集VerinaPlus和VerinaLite，揭示LLM在代码规范生成与代码生成任务中的薄弱点，实现更精准的模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试因正负测试用例数量和质量有限，导致对LLM生成规范与实现的能力评估虚高，缺乏对代码形式可验证性的有效评测。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两阶段：测试套件扩增（构建多样且具挑战性的测试用例）和测试套件精简（蒸馏为紧凑且具区分力的套件），并在Verina上实例化得到VerinaPlus和VerinaLite。

Result: VerinaPlus将原始测试套件扩增83倍以上，在8个SOTA LLM上的实验表明其在SpecGen和CodeGen任务上均使分数显著下降，暴露模型弱点；VerinaLite以14倍缩减保持同等区分力并降低评估成本。

Conclusion: VeriScale框架有效提升了代码基准测试的质量与区分度，为LLM在软件工程中的可靠评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [136] [ASAP: Attention Sink Anchored Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22372)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Hanyoung Kim,Yanggee Kim,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP提出了一种无需训练的Vision Transformer令牌缩减框架，通过将注意力汇聚现象重新定义为特征，利用懒随机游走模型和径向扩散聚类，在保持甚至提升精度的同时实现高达48%的吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在高分辨率下自注意力机制的二次复杂度导致严重计算瓶颈，现有令牌缩减方法依赖局部度量（如单层注意力分数）易受注意力汇聚现象影响，导致无信息令牌被保留而前景对象被错误丢弃。

Method: 将ViT信息流建模为懒随机游走，利用累积转移矩阵计算到注意力汇聚点的扩散距离，通过径向扩散聚类划分令牌，并通过过渡权重池化单次压缩背景冗余。

Result: 在图像、视频和视觉-语言任务中，ASAP优于现有方法，吞吐量提升高达48%，同时保持甚至超过基线准确率。

Conclusion: ASAP通过重新利用注意力汇聚现象而非回避它，提供了一种训练免费的ViT加速方案，有效解决了令牌缩减中的结构性偏差问题。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) face severe computational bottlenecks due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. Existing token reduction methods rely on local metrics - such as single-layer attention scores - that are inherently vulnerable to the attention sink phenomenon, where uninformative tokens are paradoxically preserved over salient foreground objects. We propose ASAP (Attention Sink Anchored Pruning), a training-free framework that recasts this sink as a feature. Modeling ViT information flow as a Lazy Random Walk, ASAP identifies the sink as a dominant accumulator of probability mass. By computing the diffusion distance to the sink within the cumulative transition matrix, ASAP partitions tokens via Radial Diffusion Clustering and compresses background redundancy through Transition Weight Pooling in a single shot. Extensive experiments across image, video, and vision-language tasks demonstrate ASAP outperforms state-of-the-art methods, accelerating throughput by up to 48% while maintaining - or even exceeding - baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Boundary-targeted Membership Inference Attacks on Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22373)
*Anthony Hughes,Alexander Goldberg,Prince Jha,Adam Perer,Nikolaos Aletras,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边界目标的选择策略，通过识别分类器置信度最低的样本，显著提升成员推断攻击的效果。实验表明，该方法能以5%的假阳性率恢复19%被标记为情绪困扰的对话，效果是现有最佳方法的3.5倍。研究还发现基于内容的过滤无效，而噪声策略可有效缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分类器在生成式AI系统中至关重要，但其训练数据涉及敏感内容（如自伤、心理健康），存在隐私泄露风险。现有成员推断攻击方法效果有限，亟需更有效的攻击策略来评估和提升模型的隐私保护能力。

Method: 提出边界目标选择策略（Boundary-Targeted Selection），识别分类器置信度最低的样本作为攻击目标。通过分析模型在低置信度样本上的局部泛化失败——即依赖记忆而非泛化来解决训练集中的歧义——来放大成员信号。实验在微调用于检测情绪困扰用户的安全分类器上进行，并与现有SOTA MIA方法对比。

Result: 在5%假阳性率下，可恢复19%被标记为情绪困扰的对话，是现有SOTA MIA方法的3.5倍。基于内容的过滤无法有效防御，而噪声策略能有效降低该类样本的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 分类器的置信度边界样本存在显著的隐私泄露风险，现有基于内容的过滤防护不足，噪声注入是更有效的防御手段。研究揭示了安全分类器训练数据中潜在的隐私弱点，为构建更安全的生成式AI系统提供了重要视角。

Abstract: Safety classifiers are essential safeguards within generative AI systems, filtering harmful content or identifying at-risk users when interacting with large language models. Despite their necessity, these models are trained on sensitive datasets including discussions of self-harm and mental health, raising important, yet poorly understood, privacy concerns. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) allow adversaries to infer membership of examples used to train models. In this work, we hypothesize that identifying the examples on which the classifier is least confident are informative for an adversary to infer membership. This reflects a localized failure of generalization, where the model relies on memorization to resolve ambiguity in the training set. To investigate this, we introduce a new boundary-targeted selection strategy that identifies low confidence examples that amplify the signal of an examples membership within a training set. Our experimental results show that an adversary can recover 19\% of the conversations a safety classifier flagged as indicating user distress, at a 5\% false-positive rate, on a classifier fine-tuned for detecting a user who may require emotional support. This is $3.5$ times more than attacking using state-of-the-art MIA methods alone. Finally, we characterize the boundary laying examples and show that content-based filtering is ineffective for protection, and existing noise strategies can effectively mitigate susceptibility of these examples.

</details>


### [138] [Don't Forget the Critic: Value-Based Data Rehearsal for Multi-Cyclic Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22454)
*Benjamin Poole,Andrew Quinn,Li Yang,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多循环持续强化学习中的价值函数近似问题，提出Qreg+NWLU方法，通过连续数据排练和"无需等待"正则化两个简单改进，在DQN框架下有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并提升了学习效率与知识迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习（CRL）中的数据排练方法局限于策略梯度框架，仅对演员（actor）进行正则化，因为评论家（critic）正则化会导致性能下降。这种以演员为中心的方法忽视了数据排练对价值函数近似的潜力。同时，现有CRL评估很少考虑任务序列重复的多循环环境，而这种真实场景会加剧遗忘和可塑性下降问题。

Method: 在Deep Q-Networks（DQN）框架下进行Q值正则化，提出Qreg+NWLU方法，包含两个简单修改：(1) 连续数据排练——在训练过程中动态收集并更新存储的Q值；(2) "无需等待"（No-Wait）正则化——在第一个任务之后立即应用正则化，而非等待后续任务。

Result: Qreg+NWLU在学习效率、遗忘缓解和知识迁移方面均优于Qreg及传统的持续强化学习方法，特别是在价值函数近似设置下表现显著。

Conclusion: 数据排练方法可以有效地扩展到价值函数近似框架中，简单的修改（连续排练与即时正则化）即可在多循环持续学习中显著提升性能。

Abstract: Data rehearsal has emerged as a leading approach for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL). However, existing work remains confined to policy gradient frameworks, regularizing only actors due to the performance degradation incurred by critic regularization. This actor-centric approach overlooks the potential of data rehearsal for value function approximation. Moreover, existing evaluations in CRL rarely consider multi-cyclic environments where task sequences repeat, a critical real-world scenario that exacerbates forgetting and plasticity. We investigate data rehearsal for Deep Q-Networks using Q-value regularization in multi-cyclic settings and propose Qreg+NWLU which introduces two simple modifications: (1) continuous data rehearsal that dynamically collects and updates stored Q-values throughout training, and (2) "No-Wait" regularization that applies immediately rather than after the first task. Together, these modifications yield improvements in learning efficiency, forgetting mitigation, and knowledge transfer over Qreg and conventional CRL methods within value function approximation settings.

</details>


### [139] [Target-Aligned Bellman Backup for Cross-domain Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22376)
*Wei Liu,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TABB的跨域离线强化学习方法，通过评估源域数据对目标域贝尔曼目标估计的贡献而非表面相似性来选择性地利用源域数据，在目标域数据有限的情况下取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域离线RL方法基于过渡层级相似性评估源域数据的可迁移性，但过渡层级的相似性并不能保证长期回报的一致性，可能导致策略学习被误导。

Method: 提出Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB)方法，通过衡量源域过渡对目标域贝尔曼目标估计的准确性贡献来评估其可迁移性，而非基于表面过渡相似性。

Result: 在多种跨域离线RL设置下，目标域数据极度有限时，TABB一致取得了优异的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于贝尔曼目标对齐的源域数据选择比基于表面相似性的方法更有效，为解决跨域离线RL中的数据迁移问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (CDRL) aims to improve policy learning in a target domain by leveraging data collected from a source domain. Existing works typically assess the transferability of source-domain data by measuring its similarity to target-domain transitions, and implicitly perform transition-level selection. Transitions that are considered similar are assigned higher weights or rewards, while dissimilar ones are down-weighted. However, transition-level similarity does not necessarily imply consistency in long-term returns. Even visually or dynamically similar transitions may lead to significantly different outcomes in the target domain, which can mislead policy learning and degrade performance. To address this issue, we revisit the fundamental objective of policy learning. Since policy optimization ultimately relies on Bellman targets to evaluate the quality of decisions, we propose to assess the transferability of source-domain transitions based on their alignment with target-domain Bellman targets, rather than superficial transition similarity. Based on this insight, we propose a method termed Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB), which selectively leverages source-domain data by measuring their contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain. We evaluate TABB across a broad range of cross-domain offline RL settings with highly limited target-domain data. Experimental results show that TABB consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [140] [BioFormer: Rethinking Cross-Subject Generalization via Spectral Structural Alignment in Biomedical Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22468)
*Guikang Du,Haoran Li,Xinyu Liu,Zhibo Zhang,Xiaoli Gong,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BioFormer，通过频带对齐模块（FBAM）显式建模频谱漂移（幅度/相位偏移）来抑制生物医学时间序列中的受试者特异性变异，并结合样本条件层归一化稳定跨受试者表征，在6个数据集上F1分数提升6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学时间序列跨受试者泛化的核心挑战是抑制受试者特异性变异，现有方法大多通过模型构建或对抗学习隐式抑制，缺乏显式建模。

Method: 提出BioFormer，包含两个核心模块：1）频带对齐模块（FBAM），从频谱分布生成带级调制因子，自适应调整幅度和相位以对齐频谱结构；2）样本条件层归一化，从信号内在统计量而非受试者身份推断归一化参数。

Result: 在6个数据集上优于12个基线方法，绝对F1分数提升6%。

Conclusion: 引入频谱漂移作为表征受试者特异性变异的新视角，显式建模比隐式抑制更有效，验证了频带对齐和样本条件归一化的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-subject generalization in biomedical time-series refers to training on data from some subjects and testing on unseen subjects.The key challenge is to suppress subject specific variability in BTS representations.Most existing methods implicitly suppress the variability through model building or subject adversarial learning, but rarely model it explicitly.We introduce spectral drift as a new perspective to characterize subject specific variability.Specifically, BTS signals under the same label often share consistent oscillatory structure, yet exhibit subject-dependent magnitude or phase shifts in specific frequency components, which we interpret as subject-specific variability. Building on this insight, we propose BioFormer.At its core is a Frequency-Band Alignment Module(FBAM) that generates band-wise modulation factors from the spectral distribution and adaptively adjusts amplitude and phase to align spectral structure, thereby mitigating variability.We further pair FBAM with Sample Conditional Layer Normalization, which infers normalization parameters from intrinsic signal statistics rather than subject identity, stabilizing cross-subject representations.Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that BioFormer outperforms 12 baselines, yielding absolute F1-score improvements of 6%.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Explainability of SLMs by investigating Token Level Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22377)
*Sayantani Ghosh,Rajashik Datta,Amit Kumar Das,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的激活流网络(AFN)框架，通过计算BERT第8层隐藏状态的L2范数来量化词元级别的表征重要性，发现语义有意义的实义词集中于高激活桶中，而结构支持性词元贡献较少，表明第8层是语义整合的关键区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的语言模型（如BERT，1.1亿+参数）虽在自然语言理解中表现卓越，但其内部机制对研究人员和实践者仍不透明。传统的基于注意力的可解释性方法往往强调结构重要但语义弱的词元（如标点），而非有意义的语义关系，因此需要一种更有效的可解释性框架。

Method: 提出激活流网络(AFN)框架，利用BERT第8层隐藏表示的L2范数计算词元激活强度(Token Activation Strength)，并引入基于阈值的激活桶（activation bucket）公式，将词元按经验上四分位数边界划分为高激活组和低激活组。

Result: 实验观察表明，语义有意义的实义词始终占据高激活桶，并主导表征激活变化，而结构支持性词元贡献相对较少；第8层作为关键语义整合区，平衡了结构与语义信息的处理。

Conclusion: 激活强度集中在语义信息性词元周围，为基于注意力的分析提供了可解释且计算高效的替代方案，有助于将BERT从"黑箱"转变为更透明的"玻璃箱"模型。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models such as BERT having 110M+ parameters have revolutionized natural language understanding, yet their internal mechanisms remain largely opaque to researchers and practitioners. Traditional attention-based interpretability methods often emphasize structurally important but semantically weak tokens such as punctuation marks rather than meaningful semantic relationships. This work introduces a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for quantifying token-level representational importance using hidden-state activation strengths at Layer 8 of BERT. The proposed Activation Flow Network (AFN) framework computes Token Activation Strength using the L2 norm of Layer-8 hidden representations, enabling direct ranking of semantically salient tokens. The study further introduces a threshold-based activation bucket formulation that partitions tokens into HIGH-activation and LOW-activation groups using an empirical upper-quartile activation boundary. Experimental observations demonstrate that semantically meaningful content words consistently occupy the HIGH-activation bucket and dominate representational activation shifts, while structurally supportive tokens contribute comparatively less. The results suggest that Layer 8 acts as a critical semantic consolidation zone balancing structural and semantic information processing. By revealing how activation magnitudes concentrate around semantically informative tokens, this work provides an interpretable and computationally efficient alternative to attentioncentric analysis, contributing toward transforming BERT from a "black box" into a more transparent "glass box" model for natural language understanding.

</details>


### [142] [Implicit Regularization of Mini-Batch Training in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22480)
*Clement Wang,Antoine Vialle,Robin Vaysse,Thomas Bonald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现最简单的随机节点采样（RNS）在8/10数据集上匹配或超越全图训练，并通过后向误差分析揭示：图小批量SGD隐式优化了原始损失加上正则化项（梯度方差），而RNS因损失更接近全图损失且梯度方差更低，成为理论上强竞争力的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）的小批量训练与传统i.i.d.数据训练本质不同：采样子图会改变拓扑结构并引入边界效应，导致先前工作发展出能保留局部连接性、降低嵌入方差的结构感知采样器。本文试图重新审视这一研究范式。

Method: 1. 实验对比：在10个数据集上比较RNS与全图训练的性能和效率。2. 理论分析：应用后向误差分析（backward error analysis）到图小批量SGD，证明其隐式最小化采样损失加上与小批量梯度方差成正比的隐式正则化项。

Result: RNS在8/10数据集上匹配或超越全图训练，且显著降低墙钟时间和内存消耗。RNS产生的批次期望损失更接近全图损失，每批次梯度方差更低，从而得到更优的隐式目标函数。

Conclusion: 将图采样器的选择重新定义为一种隐式正则化形式，并指出RNS是一种理论上强基础的可扩展GNN训练方法。该工作挑战了"结构感知采样器优于简单随机采样"的既有认知。

Abstract: Mini-batch training of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) is fundamentally different from training on i.i.d. data: sampling a subgraph alters the topology and introduces boundary effects, leading prior work to develop structure-aware samplers that preserve local connectivity and reduce embedding variance. Surprisingly, we demonstrate that the simplest possible scheme, Random Node Sampling (RNS), training on the induced subgraph of uniformly sampled nodes, matches or outperforms full-graph training on 8 of 10 datasets at a fraction of the wall-clock time and memory. To explain this, we apply backward error analysis to graph mini-batch Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) and show that it implicitly minimizes the sampled loss plus a regularizer proportional to the mini-batch gradient variance, a quantity directly shaped by the sampler. Although RNS discards local structure, it produces mini-batches whose expected loss is closer to the full-graph loss, and whose per-batch gradients have lower variance, yielding a better implicit objective. Our analysis reframes the choice of graph sampler as a form of implicit regularization, and identifies RNS as a strong, theoretically grounded method for scalable GNN training.

</details>


### [143] [Efficient Higher-order Subgraph Attribution via Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22385)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GNN-LRP解释方法中指数级复杂度问题，提出了线性时间的高效子图归因算法，并通过消息传递技术实现了广义子图归因的加速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN-LRP虽能有效解释GNN中不同特征的交互，但其子图归因需要对指数数量的路径进行求和，计算复杂度过高，限制了其在大规模图上的可扩展性。

Method: 利用分配律，通过消息传递技术设计线性时间算法（相对于网络深度），直接计算高阶解释的量化结果；并将该高效算法推广到同时考虑邻接图特征的广义子图归因。

Result: 所提算法实现了显著的加速效果，验证了广义子图归因方法的高实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过避免指数级枚举，GNN-LRP的子图归因可在线性时间内完成，且广义子图归因进一步提升了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Explaining graph neural networks (GNNs) has become more and more important recently. Higher-order interpretation schemes, such as GNN-LRP (layer-wise relevance propagation for GNN), emerged as powerful tools for unraveling how different features interact thereby contributing to explaining GNNs. GNN-LRP gives a relevance attribution of walks between nodes at each layer, and the subgraph attribution is expressed as a sum over exponentially many such walks. In this work, we demonstrate that such exponential complexity can be avoided. In particular, we propose novel algorithms that enable to attribute subgraphs with GNN-LRP in linear-time (w.r.t. the network depth). Our algorithms are derived via message passing techniques that make use of the distributive property, thereby directly computing quantities for higher-order explanations. We further adapt our efficient algorithms to compute a generalization of subgraph attributions that also takes into account the neighboring graph features. Experimental results show the significant acceleration of the proposed algorithms and demonstrate the high usefulness and scalability of our novel generalized subgraph attribution method.

</details>


### [144] [Understanding Multimodal Failure in Action-Chunking Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22493)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Massimiliano Datres,Ariel Rodriguez,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Gitta Kutyniok,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为克隆中当同一观测对应多个有效动作时的多模态问题，分析了潜在变量策略和动作空间生成式策略中不同参数化方式在多模态建模上的失败机制，并通过实验验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在遇到同一观测对应多个有效动作时表现困难，现有方法对多模态策略的参数化方式缺乏系统性理解，需要深入分析不同参数化方式在多模态建模中的失效原因。

Method: 通过理论分析分别研究潜在变量策略中后验-先验正则化对模式信息的影响，以及动作空间生成式策略中基础空间到动作空间的Lipschitz连续性对多模态覆盖的限制。在合成多模态任务和机器人仿真基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 潜在变量策略中，过度的后验-先验正则化会去除区分示范模式所需的动作条件信息；动作空间生成式策略中，小Lipschitz常数的映射无法为多个分离模式分配显著概率，覆盖多模式需要基础空间中的尖锐过渡或动作空间中的桥接区域。

Conclusion: 不同多模态参数化方式以不同方式失败，理解这些机制有助于设计更鲁棒的多模态行为克隆策略。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning becomes difficult when the same observation admits several valid actions. We study this problem for action-chunking policies and show that different multimodal parameterizations fail in different ways. For latent-variable policies, posterior-prior regularization makes deployment-time sampling more reliable, but excessive regularization removes the action-conditioned information needed to distinguish demonstrated modes. Reducing this regularization can preserve mode information, but then success depends on whether the prior covers the relevant latent regions. For action-space generative policies, multimodality is constrained by the smoothness of the base-to-action transport: a map with small Lipschitz constant cannot assign substantial probability to many well-separated modes. Covering many modes therefore requires either sharp transitions in base space or off-support bridge regions in action space. Experiments on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks support these mechanisms.

</details>


### [145] [Hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold Network and XGBoost Framework for Week-Ahead Price Forecasting in Australia's National Electricity Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22387)
*Houxuan Zhou,Sriram Prasad,Chenghao Huang,Jiajie Feng,Hao Wang*
