### [181] [The Double Dilemma in Multi-Task Radiology Report Generation: A Gradient Dynamics Analysis and Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22635)
*Erjian Zhang,Yatong Hao,Liejun Wang,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务学习在放射学报告生成（RRG）中线性标量化策略的梯度动力学缺陷，提出了一种即插即用的优化器CAME-Grad，通过冲突避免的方向修正和幅度增强的能量注入来解决漂移项偏差和扩散项衰减的“双重困境”，在8种RRG方法上取得平均2.3%（MIMIC-CXR）和1.9%（IU X-Ray）的临床效能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多任务学习的自动放射学报告生成方法大多采用粗粒度的线性标量化策略，无法有效平衡判别性临床监督的硬约束与报告生成的平滑性要求，且从梯度动力学角度分析，存在漂移项偏差和扩散项衰减的“双重困境”失效机制。

Method: 提出CAME-Grad优化器，包含三个核心机制：1）冲突避免方向修正（Conflict-Averse Direction Rectification），确保几何有效性；2）幅度增强能量注入（Magnitude-Enhanced Energy Injection），避免局部最优解；3）自适应梯度融合机制（Adaptive Gradient Fusion），在理论最优方向与任务特定归纳偏差之间建立动态平衡。

Result: 作为通用即插即用优化器，在8种不同的RRG方法上均取得了一致且显著的改进，在MIMIC-CXR上临床效能平均提升2.3%，在IU X-Ray上平均提升1.9%。

Conclusion: CAME-Grad是一种骨干网络无关的优化器，通过梯度动力学角度的理论分析和针对性设计，有效解决了多任务RRG中线性标量化的固有限制，验证了其在多种方法上的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: While multi-task learning based automatic radiology report generation (RRG) is widely adopted to ensure clinical consistency, most focus on architectural designs yet remain limited to coarse linear scalarization strategies. These strategies cannot effectively balance the hard constraints of discriminative clinical supervision with the smoothness requirements of report generation. To address these problems, we analyze the failure mechanism of linear scalarization from the perspective of gradient dynamics, utilizing the stochastic differential equation (SDE) framework to characterize it as a "Double Dilemma" of drift term deviation and diffusion term decay. Based on this, we propose a backbone-agnostic optimizer named Conflict-Averse Magnitude-Enhanced Gradient Descent (CAME-Grad). Through conflict-averse direction rectification and magnitude-enhanced energy injection, the algorithm not only ensures geometric validity, but also avoids local optimal solutions. Then, the adaptive gradient fusion mechanism is used to establish a dynamic balance between the theoretical optimal direction and the task-specific inductive bias. Experiments show that as a universal plug-and-play optimizer, CAME-Grad brings substantial and consistent improvements across eight diverse RRG methods, elevating overall clinical efficacy performance by an average of 2.3\% on MIMIC-CXR and 1.9\% on IU X-Ray. Our code is available at https://github.com/vpsg-research/CAME-Grad.

</details>


### [182] [Why SGD is not Brownian Motion: A New Perspective on Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22644)
*Igor Ignashin,Anna Radovskaya,Andrew Semenov,Egor Lopatin,Stanislav Potapov,Aleksandr Kovalenko,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Shestakov,Andrey Leonidov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将SGD重新建模为波动损失景观中的确定性动力学，而非传统的Langevin过程。从离散更新出发推导出离散Fokker-Planck方程，揭示了沿loss山谷的有效扩散行为，其扩散系数与学习率成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SGD Langevin建模依赖于连续时间极限和sqrt(eta)噪声缩放，这与有限学习率下的离散SGD更新不匹配，需要更精确的建模框架。

Method: 直接从离散SGD更新出发，通过主方程推导离散Fokker-Planck方程；沿均值Hessian的特征基对临界点附近动力学进行模态分解分析。

Result: 在近乎平坦的方向上，参数分布无法达到稳态，方差随时间增长，表现为有效扩散，扩散系数与学习率成正比；在计算机视觉和NLP的神经网络模型上验证了受限模式与扩散模式的定性分离。

Conclusion: 标准的Langevin近似在有限学习率下存在局限，新框架更准确描述了SGD在loss景观平坦方向上的扩散行为，为理解深度学习中的泛化与优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) is commonly modeled as a Langevin process, assuming that minibatch noise acts as Brownian motion. However, this approximation relies on a continuous-time limit and a sqrt(eta) noise scaling that does not match the discrete SGD update at finite learning rate. In this work, we propose an alternative formulation of SGD as deterministic dynamics in a fluctuating loss landscape induced by minibatch sampling. Starting directly from the discrete update, we derive a master equation for the parameter distribution and obtain a discrete Fokker--Planck equation that differs from the standard Langevin form at order eta^2. Using this framework, we analyze SGD dynamics near critical points of the loss. We show that the behavior decomposes along the eigenbasis of the mean Hessian into qualitatively distinct regimes. In particular, nearly-flat directions do not admit a stationary distribution: the variance grows over time, corresponding to effective diffusion along valleys with a coefficient proportional to the learning rate. We provide empirical evidence supporting these predictions on neural network models in computer vision and natural language processing, observing a clear qualitative separation between confined and diffusive modes.

</details>


### [183] [ChronoVAE-HOPE: Beyond Attention -- A Next-Generation VAE Foundation Model for Specialized Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22684)
*José Alberto Rodríguez,Luis Balderas,Miguel Lastra,Antonio Arauzo-Azofra,José M. Benítez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronoVAE-HOPE 是一种新一代时间序列基础模型，通过基于 HOPE Block 的 VAE 框架（含 Titans 短期记忆模块和 CMS 长期记忆系统）替代二次注意力机制，实现解耦的趋势-季节潜在表征，在 UCR 分类基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSFM 在适配分类任务时面临两个核心挑战：标准注意力机制的二次复杂度成本高昂，以及无法解耦时间序列变异中的结构成分（趋势与季节），导致模型缺乏可解释性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出 ChronoVAE-HOPE 框架：(1) 以 HOPE Block 替代二次注意力，采用 Titans 模块（动态短期记忆）和 CMS（长期历史上下文抽象）构成双记忆系统；(2) 构建解耦潜在空间，通过独立的编码器头部和解码器路径将表征分解为趋势和季节成分；(3) 在 Monash 数据集上进行自监督预训练，联合 MTSM 辅助目标与 VAE 重构损失；(4) 冻结预训练编码器，为下游 UCR 分类任务生成固定长度嵌入。

Result: 在 UCR 时间序列分类基准上，该方法在多种时间域上表现强劲，尤其在具有严格因果结构的场景中优势明显，验证了结构化生成表征在基础模型适配分类任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: ChronoVAE-HOPE 通过可解释的结构化生成式潜在表征，成功弥合了时间序列基础模型大规模泛化能力与分类任务精细化需求之间的鸿沟，为后续研究提供了鲁棒且可解释的范式。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have become a new component of the state-of-the-art in general time series forecasting. However, adapting them to specialized classification tasks remains constrained by two interconnected challenges: the quadratic cost of standard attention mechanisms and the inability to disentangle the structural components underlying time series variability. This technical report introduces ChronoVAE-HOPE, a next-generation TSFM that reconciles massive generalization with structured latent representation for time series classification. The core of the proposal is a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) framework built upon the HOPE Block, which replaces quadratic attention with a dual-memory system: Titans modules for dynamic short-term retention and a Continuum Memory System (CMS) for the abstraction of long-term historical context. A key architectural novelty is the disentangled latent space, which factorizes representations into independent trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and separate decoder pathways. ChronoVAE-HOPE undergoes self-supervised pre-training on the Monash archive, combining a Masked Time Series Modeling (MTSM) auxiliary objective with a disentangled VAE reconstruction loss. The pre-trained encoder is subsequently frozen and used to generate fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on the UCR benchmark datasets. Empirical results demonstrate strong performance across diverse temporal domains, particularly in settings characterized by strict causal structure. ChronoVAE-HOPE establishes a robust and interpretable framework for the adaptation of foundation models to time series classification through structured generative representations.

</details>


### [184] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE中的后验坍塌实际上实现了自动化的谱剪枝。潜在模式的坍塌取决于其对重建的贡献是否低于β设定的阈值，不同β下的均衡解展现了从最不重要到最重要的潜在模式级联解耦过程。通过Landau稳定性分析推导了这一机制，定义了潜在重缩放不变的序参量来对活跃潜在模式排序。在线性高斯情况下，坍塌谱、效用谱和归一化PCA谱重合，每个坍塌遵循平均场定律，并在WorldClim数据集上验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解β-VAE中后验坍塌现象的本质机制，揭示其与谱剪枝的内在联系，并建立潜在模式重要性的定量排序方法。

Method: 采用Landau稳定性分析从损失函数推导后验坍塌的动态过程；定义潜在重缩放不变的序参量对活跃潜在模式进行排序；在线性高斯情形下建立与PCA谱的理论等价性。

Result: 发现后验坍塌是自动化的谱剪枝过程，潜在模式的坍塌级联对应于从最不重要到最重要的解耦顺序；线性高斯情况下坍塌谱、效用谱与PCA谱完全重合。

Conclusion: β-VAE的后验坍塌本质上是自动谱剪枝机制，通过调整β可以控制系统特征维度，这一发现为理解变分自编码器的表示学习特性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [185] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统分析发现，基于硬裁剪的GRPO风格目标函数中，刚性裁剪决策导致裁剪边界附近的有用信号被丢弃，是RLVR训练不稳定和收敛次优的关键瓶颈。为此提出近边界随机救援（NSR）方法，通过随机保留边界外的token来恢复丢失信号，在7B到30B的密集和MoE架构上显著提升了训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR作为扩展LLM推理能力的核心范式，其优化过程常面临训练不稳定和次优收敛的问题，现有方法未能精确识别和解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)，一种轻量级即插即用修改方法，通过随机采样机制保留略微超出裁剪边界的token，恢复被标准硬裁剪规则丢弃的有用信号。

Result: 在7B到30B模型规模、密集和MoE架构上的大量实验验证，NSR作为即插即用方案显著提升训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线方法上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 识别出硬裁剪导致边界信号丢失是RLVR训练的关键瓶颈，NSR通过随机性边界救援机制（比确定性梯度衰减更有效）有效恢复丢失信号，显著改善训练稳定性和收敛性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [186] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对GPT-2 small在间接宾语识别（IOI）任务上的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征进行审计，发现特征17,491（"cryptographic keys"）与失败强相关（Cohen's d=+2.93），但因果消融、表征基线和种子鲁棒性检验均表明该特征仅为相关性而非因果机制。主要贡献是可复现的审计流程而非具体特征发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SAE特征与模型行为失败之间的因果关系，验证稀疏特征是否真正驱动模型决策，而非仅是相关产物。

Method: 使用Bloom (2024)发布的GPT-2 small第8层残差流SAE（24,576个特征），在300个IOI提示上进行审计。对失败与成功试次进行特征激活差异分析，经Holm校正后筛选显著特征，随后对最强相关特征（17,491）进行三项控制检验：因果消融（零化该特征）、表征基线（Logistic回归对比）和种子鲁棒性检查。

Result: 146个特征通过显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量（|Cohen's d|>0.8）。特征17,491在"the keys"作为转移对象时激活最强，该条件下模型失败率为93.3%（其他对象仅7.5%）。但消融后准确率从6.7%降至4.4%（未恢复）；Logistic回归在原始768维残差流上的ROC AUC（0.929）与top-100 SAE特征（0.927）相当；跨5个随机种子中仅1次特征17,491为效应量最大的特征。

Conclusion: SAE特征主要提供可解释性而非预测能力；审计流程（廉价、模型无关、可命名相关特征）是本文的方法论贡献，而非任何单一特征的发现。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [187] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树（Ternary Decision Trees），通过在每个分裂节点引入宽度为δ的不确定区域，对落入该区域的样本进行加权混合预测并标记为边界不确定。基于CART分裂时已有的统计量，提出了五种δ估计方法。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上的实验表明，所有方法在决策准确率上均显著优于标准CART，其中margin方法效率最优，在42/72数据集上胜出，且无需额外超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策树使用硬二分阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界和恰好在边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度，这种处理方式不合理。需要一种能够识别并适当处理边界不确定样本的方法，使下游应用能够区分对待这些预测。

Method: 提出三元决策树架构，每个分裂节点增加一个以最优阈值为中心、半宽为δ的不确定区域。落入该区域的样本由左右子树的预测加权混合得到，并标记为边界不确定。δ通过节点局部统计量计算，无需外部噪声参数。提出五种δ估计方法：quality-plateau（分裂准则曲线的平台宽度）、class-overlap（类别分布重叠程度）、gain-ratio（分裂质量相对于分裂熵）、node-bootstrap（节点级重采样下的阈值方差）、margin（SVM启发的到最近异类训练样本的距离）。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上5折交叉验证，五种方法在决策准确率上均显著优于标准CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.001）。margin方法效率最优（每单位边界不确定标记率的准确率增益为0.104），在42/72个数据集上胜出。在Breiman合成基准上，margin在干净数据上自校准，node-bootstrap和quality-plateau最接近理论不可约误差。在医学和金融数据集上，node-bootstrap在乳腺X光筛查中标记10.8%样本为边界不确定，决策准确率提升+0.71%。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过引入不确定区域和概率路由机制，有效提升了决策树的预测性能。五种δ估计方法在统计上均显著优于标准CART，其中margin方法兼具高效性和零额外超参数的优势。该方法具有实际应用价值，可帮助下游系统识别并特殊处理边界不确定的预测。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [188] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqLoRA是一种基于约束持续学习的参数高效微调框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子，在无需昂贵后验融合的情况下实现多概念组合生成，支持多达101个概念的图像生成并减少属性干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法在组合多个自定义概念时面临表示干扰问题，模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适应子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。作者旨在解决表达能力与概念保真度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出SeqLoRA框架，采用约束持续学习策略，通过双层优化（bilevel optimization）联合优化两个LoRA因子；将残差层激活建模为矩阵子高斯过程，推导遗忘的高概率界；从理论上证明从数据中学习LoRA基比冻结基方法更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在多达101个概念上提升了身份保持能力和可扩展性，避免了昂贵的融合操作，并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA通过持续学习框架有效解决了多概念组合中的表示干扰问题，在概念保真度和可扩展性方面优于现有方法，同时保持了参数高效性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [189] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lumberjack算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并采用隐私保护剪枝策略，结合新型层次数据重击者检测算法，在差分隐私随机森林中实现了当前最优的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林在敏感表格数据领域广泛应用，但现有差分隐私随机森林方法性能退化严重，难以实用化。

Method: 先构建大型随机决策树，再通过隐私保护剪枝保留足够稠密的节点；核心创新是提出一种针对层次数据的$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP重击者检测算法，其误差为$O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$（h为树高），支持更深层的树结构。

Result: 在基准数据集上持续优于现有DP随机森林方法，在实用隐私预算下显著改善了隐私-效用权衡，建立了新的最优结果。

Conclusion: 精心设计的DP随机森林能够大幅缩小与非私有模型的效用差距，为未来研究提供了一个有前景但尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [190] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合持续3D重建模型和序列化策略的 curiosity-driven 强化学习方法，解决了在稀疏奖励、长时域3D环境中智能体陷入局部循环的问题，在HM3D上训练后零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界，并在下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有curiosity-driven方法在复杂逼真3D环境中失效，智能体会陷入局部循环并因重访遗忘状态而获得虚假奖励，根本原因在于缺乏空间持久性和情节上下文。

Method: 提出Recuriosity框架：1) 使用在线3D重建作为持久且持续更新的世界模型；2) 将智能体策略参数化为基于RGB观测的序列模型以保持情节轨迹历史，使智能体能导航至新颖区域。

Result: 在HM3D上仅通过curiosity训练，即超越基于RL的主动建图基线，零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界；在下游任务（摘苹果、图像目标导航）中通过端到端策略高效适应，优于从头训练的基线。

Conclusion: 持久的世界模型与情节上下文的结合是实现3D环境中有效curiosity-driven探索的关键，且训练出的策略具有良好的泛化性和下游任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


### [191] [Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22820)
*Carlos Heredia,Daniel Roncel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可积分的上下文相关需求网络（ICDN），这是一种需求优先的神经模型，用于多产品零售需求预测。该模型将 log-需求学习为 log-价格的平滑、上下文条件函数，从而能够从学习到的需求表面精确推导弹性。在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN相比有向log-log基准模型提升了样本外泛化能力，并产生了更稳定、经济上更合理的弹性估计，特别是对于弱识别的交叉价格效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多产品零售需求建模中，传统方法难以精确计算价格弹性且对弱识别交叉价格效应估计不稳定，亟需一种既能保持经济可解释性又能提升泛化能力的需求模型。

Method: 提出了ICDN（Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network），一种需求优先的神经模型。将 log-需求建模为 log-价格的光滑、上下文条件函数，从而可以精确地从学习到的需求表面推导弹性。

Result: 在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN在样本外泛化方面优于有向log-log基准模型，并产生了更稳定、经济上更合理的弹性估计，尤其是对于弱识别的交叉价格效应。

Conclusion: ICDN作为一种需求优先的神经模型，能够有效提升多产品零售需求预测的泛化性能和弹性估计的稳定性与经济合理性，特别适用于弱识别交叉价格效应场景。

Abstract: We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Adaptive RBF-KAN: A Comparative Evaluation of Dynamic Shape Parameters in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21534)
*Roberto Cavoretto,Alessandra De Rossi,Adeeba Haider,Amir Noorizadegan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应RBF-KAN，通过引入更广泛的径向基核族（包括Matérn和Wendland核）并结合留一交叉验证（LOOCV）初始化核形状参数，改进了FastKAN的固定高斯核限制，在多个二维基准函数上验证了不同核函数对不同类型函数的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FastKAN使用高斯RBF替换B样条提高了效率，但依赖固定核函数和形状参数，缺乏灵活性。现有方法未能充分利用数据驱动的方式初始化核尺度参数，限制了KAN对不同类型函数的适应能力。

Method: 1) 引入Matérn核和Wendland核族扩展RBF-KAN框架；2) 使用LOOCV方法数据驱动地初始化核形状参数；3) 在训练过程中进一步优化核尺度参数，实现自适应学习。

Result: 在多个二维基准函数上，不同核函数对平滑函数、不连续函数和振荡函数表现出各自的优势，验证了核选择和自适应形状参数的重要性。

Conclusion: 将LOOCV初始化和自适应核学习相结合，为改进基于RBF的KAN模型提供了一种实用策略，能够提升模型对不同函数类型的适应性和表现。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) approximate multivariate functions using learnable univariate edge functions, typically parameterized by B-spline bases. Although effective, spline-based implementations can be computationally expensive. A modified version of KANs, called FastKAN, improves efficiency by replacing splines with Gaussian radial basis functions (RBFs), but it relies on a fixed kernel and shape parameter. In this work, we extend the RBF-based KAN framework by introducing a broader family of radial basis kernels and by initializing the kernel shape parameter using leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that integrates LOOCV-based kernel scale estimation with deep KAN training. We also introduce Matérn and Wendland kernels into the KAN framework for the first time, enabling more flexible basis representations beyond the Gaussian kernel used in FastKAN. The LOOCV estimate provides a data-driven initialization of the kernel scale, which is subsequently refined during network training. The proposed adaptive RBF-KAN is evaluated on several two-dimensional benchmark functions. The results highlight the importance of kernel selection and adaptive shape parameters, with different kernels showing advantages for smooth functions, discontinuities, and oscillatory patterns. Overall, combining LOOCV-based initialization with adaptive kernel learning provides a practical strategy for improving RBF-based KAN models.

</details>


### [193] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需全局因果结构学习、不依赖预处理假设和因果充分性假设的局部协变量选择方法，用于无偏估计总因果效应。该方法通过刻画局部边界并设计局部识别程序，在保证因果效应估计准确性的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协变量选择方法要么依赖全局因果结构学习（高维场景下计算成本高），要么依赖预处理假设或因果充分性假设（实践中常不成立）。本文旨在克服这些不现实的假设和计算瓶颈。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部边界，该边界包含至少一个有效调整集（若存在）；然后开发局部识别程序在该边界内高效搜索；证明该方法的完备性和正确性。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够实现准确的因果效应估计，同时大幅提升计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在非参数因果效应估计中避免预处理假设和因果充分性假设的局部学习方法，理论完备且实验有效。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [194] [Scalable On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Batch Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21557)
*Jongchan Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应批量缩放（ABS）方法，通过动态调整强化学习中的批量大小来平衡早期可塑性与后期稳定收敛，揭示了在强化学习中大网络与大批量可协同实现最优性能这一反直觉发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"大批量训练与强化学习不兼容"的传统观点，解决RL中非平稳性导致的大批量性能退化问题。

Method: 提出ABS方法，核心包括：(1) 行为差异度（Behavioral Divergence）——量化相邻更新间动作层面偏移的策略非平稳性指标；(2) 根据策略波动性反向调整批量大小，在早期使用小批量保持可塑性，晚期使用大批量促进稳定收敛；(3) 集成于并行Q网络（PQN）算法中。

Result: 在ALE基准测试上，ABS成功协调了早期可塑性与晚期稳定收敛；反直觉地发现，大网络与大批量组合能达到最佳性能，解锁了此前被认为RL中无法实现的规模化行为。

Conclusion: RL中的非平稳性是动态演化的而非固定属性，通过自适应批量控制，可以实现在RL中类似监督学习的大批量规模化收益。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom holds that large-batch training is fundamentally incompatible with Reinforcement Learning (RL) - beyond a modest threshold, increasing batch sizes typically yields diminishing returns or performance degradation due to the inherent non-stationarity of the data distribution. We challenge this view by observing that non-stationarity is not a fixed property of RL, but evolves throughout training: early stages exhibit rapid behavioral shifts that demand small batches for plasticity, whereas late stages approach a quasi-stationary regime where large batches enable precise convergence. Motivated by this observation, we propose Adaptive Batch Scaling (ABS), that dynamically adjusts the effective batch size according to the stability of the learning policy. Central to ABS is Behavioral Divergence, a novel metric that quantifies policy non-stationarity by measuring action-level shifts between consecutive updates, which we use to scale batch size inversely to policy volatility. Integrated with the Parallelised Q-Network (PQN) algorithm and evaluated on the ALE benchmark, ABS seamlessly reconciles early-stage plasticity with late-stage stable convergence. Strikingly, contrary to conventional wisdom, our results reveal that the combination of larger networks and larger batch sizes achieves the best performance - a scaling behavior previously thought to be unattainable in RL, now unlocked through adaptive batch control.

</details>


### [195] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线决策框架，用于储备策略选择。该框架不是输出单一的点估计获胜者，而是将记录的实验证据转化为保守的决策对象，包括认证策略、统计支配的备选方案以及需要进一步验证的未解决候选策略。主要理论结果提供了统一的有限目录保证，表明在同时不确定性控制和保守支持门控下，该框架能保留最优的门控通过策略，同时仅剔除具有认证遗憾的策略。实验证明该方法能将19个策略的目录缩减为2个策略的验证短名单，并在多个细分市场中证明无伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线储备定价评估方法（如回放表和离线策略评估）虽然能估计或排序策略价值，但无法直接回答"现有证据是否足够强以证明验证合理性"这一操作性问题。它们可能隐藏弱阈值支持、多重比较效应、子群伤害和投标者响应不确定性等风险。

Method: 本文开发了一个支持感知的离线决策框架，将记录证据转化为包含认证策略、统计支配备选方案和未解决候选策略的保守决策对象。核心理论结果提供统一有限目录保证，在同时不确定性控制和保守支持门控下保留最优策略并剔除认证遗憾策略。还刻画了支持局部化回放泛化、信息论阈值分辨率限制以及异质投标者响应如何推翻局部化回放排序的条件。

Result: 在iPinYou实时竞价日志上的实验表明：领先储备规则在第二季实现47.66%的回放提升、40.71%的同时下界提升，第三季实现43.87%的冻结超时回放提升。框架将19个策略目录缩减为2个策略的验证短名单，并在44个广告主、交易平台和区域细分市场中证明无伤害。

Conclusion: 离线储备策略评估应该产生认证的验证决策，而非仅仅是点估计排序。本文框架通过提供保守但可靠的决策支持，有效解决了现有方法无法判断证据充分性的问题。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [196] [Uniform-in-Time Weak Propagation-of-Chaos in Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22010)
*Margalit Glasgow,Joan Bruna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了神经网络在平均场极限下的弱传播混沌（weak propagation-of-chaos）的非渐近一致时间界，利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛速度，在无噪声设定下得到了关于神经元数量、样本数和GD步数的多项式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限宽度神经网络与无限宽度平均场极限之间的误差（波动）在长时间下的行为难以刻画，因为标准Grönwall估计只能得到常数时间界，而一致时间界通常依赖强凸性或对数Sobolev不等式（这些在无噪声梯度下降中不成立）。本文旨在不依赖这些几何假设的情况下，建立一致时间界的弱传播混沌结果。

Method: 利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛率（而非传统的强凸性或LSI条件），在条件 $\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$ 下，通过分析平均场过剩MSE损失 $L_t$ 的衰减速度（$L_t \lesssim t^{-c}$），得到有限宽度网络与无限宽度网络的输出误差界 $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$。该方法还自然推广到有限样本和时间离散化情形。

Result: 建立了非渐近、一致时间（uniform-in-time）的弱传播混沌界：当平均场动力学的收敛速度快于 $t^{-2}$ 时，只需 $\text{poly}(d/ε)$ 个神经元、训练样本和GD步数即可达到 $ε$ 损失。结果不依赖于最优值附近的几何假设，且适用于无噪声设定。

Conclusion: 通过利用平均场动力学的收敛率而非局部几何性质，成功建立了神经网络在特征学习机制下的一致时间传播混沌界，为有限宽度网络逼近无限宽度网络提供了理论保证，并给出了在神经元数量、样本数和迭代步数上的多项式复杂度上界。

Abstract: We consider one-hidden layer neural networks trained in the feature-learning regime using gradient descent, and relate the output of the finite-width network $f_{\hatρ_t^m}$ to its infinite-width counterpart $f_{ρ_t^{MF}}$, which evolves in the mean-field dynamics.
  While constant-time horizon bounds for $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}} - f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|$ may be obtained via standard Grönwall estimates, the long-time behavior of the fluctuation is a more delicate matter. Uniform-in-time bounds often rely on (local) strong convexity in the landscape or Logarithmic Sobolev inequalities present in noisy gradient dynamics.
  In this work, we establish non-asymptotic weak propagation-of-chaos that holds uniformly in time, obtained by exploiting instead the convergence rate of the mean-field deterministic Wasserstein-gradient-flow dynamics. Specifically, denoting by $L_t$ the mean-field excess MSE loss at time $t$ and $m$ the number of neurons, under standard regularity assumptions and the condition $\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$, we obtain the uniform in time bound $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$ whenever $L_t \lesssim t^{-c}$. Our result holds in a noiseless setting and does not make any assumptions on the geometry of the landscape near the optimum, and extends seamlessly to other forms of discretization, including finite number of samples and time discretization. A key takeaway of our result is that whenever the convergence rate of the mean-field, population-loss dynamics is faster than $t^{-2}$, we can attain a loss of $ε$ with only $\text{poly}(d/ε)$ neurons, training samples, and GD steps.

</details>


### [197] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是作者2017-2018年撰写的一份技术报告，旨在利用在线投注策略的财富保证来推导迭代对数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过在线投注策略的财富保证这一新颖视角，为迭代对数定律提供一种新的推导方法。

Method: 利用在线投注策略（赌博策略）的财富增长保证，从博弈论角度推导迭代对数定律。

Result: 成功使用在线投注策略的财富保证推导出迭代对数定律。

Conclusion: 迭代对数定律可以通过在线投注策略的财富保证这一途径得到证明，为概率论中的经典结果提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [198] [Departure from Regularity: Degree Heterogeneity and Eigengap as the Structural Drivers of ASE-LSE Latent Subspace Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22346)
*Minh Triet Pham,Ian Gallagher*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了邻接谱嵌入（ASE）和拉普拉斯谱嵌入（LSE）在图数据分析中产生不同结果的结构性原因：节点度正则性是两种方法完全一致的必要条件，度异质性驱动差异增大，而社区结构强度则抑制差异，两者的比率可作为判断两种嵌入能否互换使用的强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ASE和LSE是图数据分析中最广泛使用的两种方法，但它们在相同网络上常常产生不同结果，而这一分歧背后的结构性原因尚未被完全理解。本文旨在提供一种结构性的解释。

Method: 通过理论分析证明正则性是两种方法完全一致的充分条件，推导出偏离正则性时差异的显式上界，并将该上界分解为度异质性和社区结构强度两个结构性因素。通过数千个模拟网络进行实证验证。

Result: 证明了当每个节点具有相同连接数（正则图）时，ASE和LSE产生相同的潜在子空间；给出了非正则情况下差异的显式界，表明度异质性驱动差异增大，社区结构强度抑制差异；实证验证了这两个驱动因素及其比率作为两种嵌入可互换性的预测指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 度异质性与社区结构强度的比率是判断ASE和LSE何时可互换、何时不可互换的强预测因子。

Abstract: Two of the most widely used methods for analysing graph data, Adjacency Spectral Embedding and Laplacian Spectral Embedding, often produce different results when applied to the same network. Yet the structural reasons behind this disagreement remain incompletely understood. This paper provides a structural account. We show that regularity is a sufficient condition for perfect agreement: when every node has the same number of connections, the two methods produce identical latent subspaces. Any departure from this regularity introduces disagreement, and we prove an explicit bound whose two terms suggest the structural ingredients controlling it: degree heterogeneity, which pushes the methods apart, and community structure strength, which pulls them back together. We validate both drivers empirically across thousands of simulated networks, confirming that heterogeneity drives disagreement up, community strength suppresses it, and their ratio provides a strong predictor of when the two embeddings can be treated as interchangeable and when they cannot.

</details>


### [199] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究重复一价拍卖中的刷单（shilling）问题——刷单通过虚假出价改变学习者的观测反馈（而非分配结果），并提出了结合鲁棒区间消除分支与乐观去偏分支的算法，实现了匹配的下界与上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示仅影响反馈而不影响分配的刷单行为如何根本性地改变重复竞价问题的统计难度。

Method: 提出混合算法：(1) 鲁棒区间消除分支，忽略刷单报告，达到动态定价率 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$；(2) 乐观分支，对失败侧报告去偏并在可靠时利用后缀信息，达到一价拍卖率 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$；通过验证与竞速过程自适应地选择更新方式。

Result: 算法实现了与下界匹配（对数因子内）的遗憾上界，证明了仅影响反馈的刷单会显著改变重复竞价的统计难度。

Conclusion: 即使刷单分布已知，仅反馈层面的操纵仍会加剧学习难度，需设计自适应算法来应对不同情境。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [200] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于学生化自归一化统计量的独立性检验方法 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC，其零分布为标准正态分布，无需置换检验校准，运行速度比传统置换校准方法快25-60倍，同时保持相当的检验功效和第一类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSIC 和 dHSIC 是退化的 V 统计量，其零分布为数据依赖的加权卡方分布，必须通过置换检验校准，每次检验的成本随置换次数倍增（通常两个数量级），计算效率低下。

Method: 借鉴基于鞅的 MMD 两样本检验思想，提出两种学生化统计量：1) mHSIC：自归一化的下三角和，使用经验中心化的 Gram 矩阵的 Hadamard 积，零分布渐近标准正态，无需样本分割，计算代价 O(n²)；2) mdHSIC：采用半样本分割，一半估计中心化，另一半运行下三角自归一化鞅，条件均值残差随维度 d 指数衰减，检验代价随 d 线性增长。

Result: 两种统计量在合成数据上（每变量输入维度1-500，联合检验变量数2-10）与置换校准基线方法具有相当的实证第一类错误率和检验功效，但运行速度快25-60倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效消除了置换检验的必要性，大幅降低计算成本，同时保持了统计有效性，为高维独立性检验提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [201] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种用于一步生成建模的保守漂移方法，该方法用KDE梯度速度替代原始位移漂移速度，解决了非保守性问题，并给出了连续时间有限粒子收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一般位移漂移场中存在的非保守性问题（非梯度场），提高一步生成建模的理论保证。

Method: 将原始位移漂移速度替换为核密度估计（KDE）梯度速度（即核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差），该速度为梯度场；分析了Laplace核下的非保守漂移方法。

Result: 建立了联合熵恒等式，得到了经验Stein漂移、KDE平滑Fisher差异和平方中心速度的界；主要有限粒子修正为倒数KDE自相互作用项；在均匀求积正则条件下得到根残差速度率N^{-1/(d+4)}，更一般条件下得到优化根率N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}；非保守方法存在不可避免的残差项。

Conclusion: 通过显式漂移大小η，连续时间残差速度界可转化为一步生成保证，为保守漂移方法提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MOOD的基准测试，用于评估LLM监控管线是否能检测到分布外（OOD）的对齐失败。研究发现安全分类器在OOD场景下泛化能力差，而结合OOD检测器（马氏距离+困惑度）可将召回率从39%提升到45%，且OOD检测带来的提升比单纯扩大模型参数20倍更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全和对齐失败常发生在分布外（OOD）场景下，现有监控方法对此类情况检测能力不足，缺乏系统性评估基准和解决方案。

Method: 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和七个分布外测试集；训练监控模型；提出将防护模型与OOD检测器（马氏距离、困惑度等四种）结合的方案。

Result: 防护模型在OOD场景下泛化差；结合OOD检测器后召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测带来的增益超过将防护模型参数扩大20倍。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组成部分，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [203] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个多智能体AI框架，能将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化连接起来，通过视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体自动调整求解器参数，在60%的试验中生成符合偏好的设计，实现从意图到原型的端到端设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计师常需手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转换为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器设置，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体框架，将自然语言问题描述转换为验证后的求解器输入，运行拓扑优化求解器，渲染3D拓扑结构，使用多视图视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体对结果进行评审并修订求解器参数。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准和手机支架产品设计两项任务中，系统在60%的试验中至少生成一个符合偏好的设计，比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管线成功率高6倍。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化能使设计师从低层次参数调优转向高层次功能与形态规范，但需建立可靠自主工程设计所需的保障机制。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [204] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模计算分析（涵盖1600余份证词），验证了USC犹太大屠杀基金会与耶鲁Fortunoff视频档案馆在口述证词结构上的传统二分法，发现两者虽存在显著结构性差异，但亦有大量重叠，挑战了简单的"结构化vs自由形式"二元对立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究中长期存在一种观点，认为USC Shoah Foundation的访谈偏向结构化、 interviewer主导的格式，而Yale Fortunoff Video Archive则更倾向于自由开放的形式。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设，但缺乏大规模、定量化的系统验证。

Method: 对两个档案库中1600余份证词进行大规模计算分析，采用话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型（LLM）的分析方法，通过主题连贯性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布来量化证词的"结构化程度"。

Result: 分析结果总体上证实了早期研究识别的结构性差异，同时也揭示了两者之间存在显著重叠——无论是单个访谈内部还是跨常见叙事模式层面。这表明简单的"结构化vs自由形式"二分法并不足以概括这些口述历史的真实面貌。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架。作为概念验证，该框架可推广至数字口述历史、叙事分析及公民科学标注平台设计等更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [205] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MindLoom框架，通过将推理问题分解为原子知识-推理转换（思维模式）并组合这些模式来合成前沿级推理数据，在9个基准测试中表现出优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对问题难度的结构因素可见性有限，导致多样性狭窄和难度控制不稳定，难以系统性地产生前沿级推理数据。

Method: 1) 将困难问题解分解为思维模式链；2) 训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配；3) 迭代应用检索到的思维模式到种子问题并采用分布对齐采样；4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并提供经评判正确的响应。

Result: 在涵盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务的9个基准上，MindLoom微调模型优于基础模型、蒸馏和外部数据基线。消融实验验证了各组件贡献，分析表明MindLoom覆盖广泛推理模式且具备有效难度控制。

Conclusion: 基于思维模式组合的视角进行推理数据合成是有效的，能够生成多样性高且难度可控的前沿级训练数据，提升LLM推理能力。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [206] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，通过对数据模型进行扩展和现代化改造，以支持计算生成AOP、定量AOP，并增强AOP框架与新方法学（NAMs）的整合，从而服务于下一代风险评估、监管科学及生物医学和One Health应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP-Wiki作为全球AOP知识库，其现有数据模型和应用基础设施限制了AOP的持续增长和演化，无法满足监管科学、生物医学及One Health等新兴领域对AOP日益增长的需求，亟需现代化改造。

Method: 提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，通过数据模型扩展、内部质量改进、证据结构化以增强FAIR性和AI就绪性，以及改善AOP框架与NAMs的集成来实现。

Result: EMOD 3.0具体展示了数据模型的扩展方案，为支持计算生成AOP和定量AOP（qAOPs）奠定了基础，提升了AOP-Wiki在监管科学和新兴应用场景中的服务能力。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0通过数据现代化改造和利用AOP核心原则指导AI应用，有望将AOP-Wiki转变为一个更能服务于监管科学、生物医学和One Health领域的下一代知识平台。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [207] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过研究AI系统对不合理类别成员（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）的判断，刻画AI对基础概念的边界理解，发现AI在概念归属上与人类存在显著差异（如将“单词”归入“交通工具”类别），并展示这些概念错位会引发AI安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有类人常识理解的AI系统是实现安全可靠系统的关键步骤，而传统通过合理类别成员提问的方式可能仅反映了训练数据中的模式，需要探索概念边界理解的新方法。

Method: 基于Rosch和Mervis的经典心理学研究，让AI系统对物体进行上位类别归属判断（包括正确类别和错配类别），并与人类参与者的判断进行比较。

Result: 发现AI在多个概念上与人类存在有意义的差异：将“单词”归入“交通工具”和“服装”类别；将部分“蔬菜”成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的示例归入“武器”类别。

Conclusion: 概念错位会转化为有问题的下游行为，对AI安全产生影响，表明需要更细致的概念对齐评估方法。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [208] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI辅助对人类逻辑推理技能发展的影响，发现高频使用AI会削弱技能发展，而AI的信息质量（低信息 vs 高信息）在这一过程中起调节作用：低信息AI既无益于即时表现，也阻碍长期学习；高信息AI能提升短期表现，但对长期技能的影响存在异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI越来越多地融入人类问题解决过程，但其对个人技能发展的影响尚不清楚，作者希望通过控制实验厘清这一关系。

Method: 在受控的逻辑推理任务中，参与者可按需访问AI辅助。通过对比高频/低频AI使用者、以及不同信息质量（低信息 vs 高信息）的AI条件，分析AI使用强度和信息性对学习效果的影响。

Result: AI使用越多，技能发展越弱；低频使用者表现与未使用AI的匹配用户相似。低信息AI既不提升即时表现，也损害长期学习；高信息AI提升短期表现，但对长期表现无显著负面影响，但存在异质性。

Conclusion: AI既可以作为互补工具增强独立推理能力，也可能成为削弱推理能力的替代品。合理调控AI的访问和使用对促进技能发展至关重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [209] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将拒绝抑制重新解释为对线性探针的潜在空间逃逸攻击，现有方法的消融操作等价于投影到决策边界（最小置信度逃逸攻击），并提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），通过将表示投影越过边界以优化置信度，在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过消融拒绝方向来抑制模型拒绝行为的方法虽然经验成功，但缺乏对其潜在空间变换及其为何能抑制拒绝的原则性解释。

Method: 将拒绝抑制重新定义为对线性探针的潜在空间逃逸攻击；指出先前工作的均值差方向天然定义了此类探针，其消融操作等价于投影到决策边界（最小置信度逃逸攻击）；提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），将表示投影越过决策边界并优化置信度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率，优于现有拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了拒绝抑制的原则性解释，揭示了先前方法仅到决策边界的局限性，并通过CLE方法在多个模型上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [210] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于200个真实多轮人机对话的情感智能基准测试，评估了11个模型在情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断四个独立能力上的表现，发现偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情绪标签准确性更具模型区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接衡量模型在真实多轮对话中推断和响应用户情感状态的能力。

Method: 基于200个真实多轮人机对话，参与者与匿名LLM对话并提供逐轮的情感状态、模型行为和偏好响应标注，构建AttuneBench基准，评估11个模型在四个维度上的表现。

Result: 模型在情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断四个维度上的排名 largely independent；偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情绪标签准确性更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为可分解为独立的子能力，关键不在于识别情绪标签，而在于预测特定用户在上下文中期望的响应类型，单轮或合成格式无法直接捕获这种跨轮能力。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [211] [SMDD-Bench: Can LLMs Solve Real-World Small Molecule Drug Design Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21740)
*Kevin Han,Renfei Zhang,Kathy Wei,Hamed Mahdavi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Amir Farimani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMDD-Bench，一个用于评估LLM代理在真实小分子药物设计任务中表现的多轮、长周期基准测试，包含502个可解任务实例和5种任务类型。实验表明，最先进的GPT5.4也仅能解决40.2%的任务，揭示了当前LLM在自主药物设计方面的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估方法存在临时性、过于简单、规模有限或局限于单轮问答等问题，缺乏标准化的小分子药物设计评估基准。为了标准化LLM代理在小分子药物设计上的评估，作者引入SMDD-Bench。

Method: 构建了包含502个保证可解的任务实例的基准，涵盖5种任务类型（2D药效团识别、相互作用点发现、骨架跃迁、先导化合物优化和片段组装），涉及102个独特蛋白质靶点。在7个前沿开源和闭源LLM上进行了基准测试。

Result: 性能最好的模型GPT5.4仅能解决40.2%的任务，表明当前LLM在真实小分子药物设计任务上表现有限。

Conclusion: SMDD-Bench为评估LLM代理在自主计算药物设计中的能力提供了标准化测试平台，期望推动该领域发展。

Abstract: LLM agents have incredible potential for scientific discovery applications. However, the performance of LLM agents on real-world, small molecule drug design (SMDD) tasks across diverse chemistries and targets is unclear. Current evaluation methods are either ad hoc, too simple for real-world discovery, limited in scale, or restricted to single-turn question answering. In effort to standardize the evaluation of LLM agents on small molecule design, we introduce SMDD-Bench, a challenging, multi-turn, long-horizon agentic benchmark consisting of 502 guaranteed-solvable task instances spanning 5 task types: 2D Pharmacophore Identification, Interaction Point Discovery, Scaffold Hopping, Lead Optimization, and Fragment Assembly. SMDD-Bench tasks span a wide region of chemical space and involve 102 unique protein targets. Completely solving the benchmark would require having strong chemical and biological reasoning and 3D intuition, understanding specialized tool use, and displaying planning expertise over a limited number of oracle calls. We benchmark 7 frontier open and closed source LLMs and find even the most performant LLM, GPT5.4, solves only 40.2\% of tasks. We hope SMDD-Bench provides a standardized testbed to invigorate the field towards training and evaluating LLM agents for fully autonomous computational drug design. We host a public leaderboard at smddbench.com .

</details>


### [212] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基于AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度存在系统性偏差：分数反映平台用户结构而非真实劳动力市场，不同平台输入导致弹性估计差异达1.9倍，消费级与企业级渠道估计符号相反，经BLS劳动力份额调整后估计值衰减42%-93%。作者形式化了非经典测量误差，推导了就业弹性的概率极限和部分识别边界，发现偏差导致替代效应被低估的程度大于互补效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用AI平台对话日志来测量职业暴露度，但该方法可能存在测量误差——分数反映的是平台用户结构而非真实劳动力市场特征。本文旨在量化这一偏差，并形式化非经典测量误差对就业弹性估计的影响。

Method: 固定结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计量，仅改变平台输入（消费级vs企业级版本），比较估计系数变化；使用BLS劳动力份额数据进行重加权调整；推导非经典测量误差下估计量的概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 仅改变平台输入导致ChatGPT后就业系数变化1.9倍；同一供应商的消费级和企业级渠道估计符号相反；BLS调整后估计值衰减42%-93%；非经典测量误差导致替代弹性被低估的程度大于互补弹性。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在显著的系统性偏差，研究者需要审慎处理平台选择问题，并考虑测量误差对因果推断的影响。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [213] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合因果关系与论证推理的新方法，通过因果发现构建双极论证框架（BAF），利用半稳定语义识别支持与反对特征的解释，阐明模型为何做出特定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法仅能识别哪些特征与预测相关，但无法解释为何模型做出特定决策，缺乏因果层面的推理能力。

Method: 先使用因果发现方法识别变量间因果关系，再将其转化为双极论证框架（BAF），以表示特征间的支持与反对交互，最后通过半稳定语义找出能够解释预测结果的特征扩展。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了方法，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该方法通过融合因果性与论证推理，能够提供比传统事后解释方法更深入的、关于模型为何做出预测的解释。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [214] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling with Random Job Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22773)
*Yu Tang,Muhammad Zakwan,Efe Balta,John Lygeros,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的深度强化学习（DRL）方法，结合近端策略优化（PPO）算法和轻量级多层感知机（MLP），通过从一组既定的调度规则中选择动作，以最小化总完工时间的方式解决带随机任务到达的柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP）。实验表明，该方法在异构数据集和不同任务到达率下均优于单个调度规则，且在数据集异构性强时性能接近到达触发的混合整数线性规划求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FJSP面临两个主要挑战：未来作业到达时间的不可预测性以及问题的组合复杂性，导致传统混合整数线性规划求解器难以处理。

Method: 采用基于事件的DRL框架，使用PPO算法和轻量级MLP作为智能体；状态表示直接从环境中获取；动作空间限定为一系列成熟的调度规则。

Result: DRL方法在具有不同异构程度和作业到达率的数据集上，表现优于任何单一调度规则；在与到达触发MILP方法的对比中，数据异构性强时性能良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于事件的DRL方法能够有效解决随机作业到达的FJSP，尤其适合异构数据集，综合性能优于传统调度规则。

Abstract: The Flexible Job Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is the optimal allocation of a set of jobs to machines. Two primary challenges persist in FJSP: the unpredictable arrival of future jobs and the combinatorial complexity of the problem, rendering it intractable for conventional mixed-integer linear programming solvers. This paper proposes an event-based \gls{DRL} approach to solve FJSP with random job arrivals. Specifically, we employ the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm and use lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptrons to train the \gls{DRL} agent for minimizing the total completion time of all jobs. We design the state representation to be directly accessible from the environment, and limit the learning agent to selecting from among a set of well-established dispatching rules. Simulations show that our \gls{DRL} approach outperforms any of the individual dispatching rules on datasets with varying heterogeneity and job arrival rates. We benchmark our \gls{DRL} against an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solution and show that our method achieves good performance especially when the datasets are heterogeneous.

</details>


### [215] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI谄媚行为定义不一致的问题，通过综述70篇论文构建了分类体系（区分谄媚对象为信念/人格特质，以及行为表现为显性/隐性），并通过对106位专家的调查发现专家对谄媚行为的认定存在显著分歧，表明AI谄媚是一个需要统一词汇表的多样化行为家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚作为一个术语缺乏一致定义，研究者、企业和政策制定者用同一术语描述不同行为，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对某一形式的谄媚具有抵抗力但仍会表现出其他形式的谄媚。

Method: 1) 综述70篇关于AI谄媚的论文，开发行为定义和测量的分类体系；2) 调查106位AI谄媚及相关领域专家，检验研究者对哪些模型行为属于谄媚的共识程度。

Result: 分类体系区分了(1)谄媚对象：用户立场/信念 vs. 用户个人特质/情绪；(2)表现方式：显性直接语言 vs. 隐性微妙行为（如框架、省略、语气）。现有研究集中于对用户信念的 overt 谄媚，subtle 和面向个人的行为研究不足。专家调查中94.3%认为谄媚是重大问题，但对具体行为的认定存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一个包含多种行为类型的广泛家族，不同行为具有不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理影响，本文提出的分类体系为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇表。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [216] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Skill是一种无需微调硬件专用模型、通过测试时扩展框架提升硬件LLM智能体在复杂Verilog设计任务上表现的方法。它通过挖掘智能体轨迹中的成功/失败模式，转化为任务特定技能，并利用稠密验证反馈指导搜索、编辑、验证和恢复过程，在先前未解决的任务上取得突破性进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂Verilog设计问题（CVDP）对硬件LLM智能体构成挑战，因为需要在大型仓库快照中定位验证器相关的RTL、测试台、包含路径和构建依赖，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏验证器失败中恢复。现有方法要么需要专用模型微调，要么仅依赖更多采样，效率低下。

Method: Trace2Skill采用测试时扩展框架，将智能体的自然语言技能视为可进化的策略。它通过三个核心组件循环工作：Oracle（将轨迹转换为密集诊断和专家教训）、Mutator（变异技能）、Selector（选择最优技能）。同时支持有界运行时稠密验证反馈，返回经处理的函数观测结果，同时保持隐藏的测试台和参考解对智能体不可访问。

Result: 在困难的CVDP任务上，Trace2Skill（结合稠密验证反馈）显著提高了任务通过率，在先前未解决的任务上取得突破性进展，且无需高质量微调数据、专用RTL模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill提供了一种通用的测试时扩展策略，可扩展到数字设计之外的其他可验证EDA任务。其核心贡献在于无需模型微调即可提升硬件智能体能力，证明了通过技能进化而非模型更新来改进智能体性能的有效性。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [217] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人群偏好的安全强化学习框架（Safe Crowd Preference-based RL），通过从人群偏好中提取安全对齐的技能，并将其组合以安全地解决下游任务，从而在没有显式安全奖励的情况下降低安全成本并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF可以从偏好中揭示安全等隐式目标，但不同用户虽有相似的安全原则却可能表达不同偏好。直接优化偏好学习奖励模型与任务奖励的组合存在固有局限性，因此需要一种能从人群偏好中发现共享安全准则并将其迁移到下游RL任务的方法。
